# 1.7.4 Model

- [3D-LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.12981): An early 3D-language architecture that connects 3D scene representations with LLMs for object, relation, and spatial-context reasoning.
- [Unified 3D and 4D Panoptic Segmentation via Dynamic Shifting Networks](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2023.3349304): Unifies static 3D and temporal 4D panoptic segmentation with dynamic shifting modules that track instance and semantic changes across point-cloud sequences.
- [ViTA: Video Transformer Adaptor for Robust Video Depth Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2023.3309559): Adapts video transformers for depth estimation so temporal context improves robustness under motion, appearance change, and difficult scene geometry.
- [GPT-COPE: A Graph-Guided Point Transformer for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2023.3309902): Combines graph guidance with point transformers to estimate category-level object pose from 3D point observations.
- [GraphAlign++: An Accurate Feature Alignment by Graph Matching for Multi-Modal 3D Object Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2023.3306361): Aligns multimodal 3D detection features through graph matching so camera and point-cloud cues support the same object hypotheses.
- [Ortho-NeRF: generating a true digital orthophoto map using the neural radiance field from unmanned aerial vehicle images](https://doi.org/10.1080/10095020.2023.2296014): Uses NeRF reconstruction from UAV imagery to produce geometrically corrected digital orthophoto maps.
- [Deblurring 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.00834): Extends 3D Gaussian splatting with blur-aware optimization so scenes can be reconstructed from degraded image captures.
- [Street Gaussians for Modeling Dynamic Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01339): Represents dynamic urban scenes with Gaussian primitives that separate moving actors from the static street environment for view synthesis.
- [DDN-SLAM: Real Time Dense Dynamic Neural Implicit SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01545): Builds real-time dense SLAM with neural implicit maps that handle dynamic scene content instead of assuming a fixed environment.
- [Image Sculpting: Precise Object Editing with 3D Geometry Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01702): Edits image objects through explicit 3D geometry controls, allowing spatially constrained shape and pose changes rather than only text-guided appearance edits.
- [LEAP-VO: Long-term Effective Any Point Tracking for Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01887): Uses long-term any-point tracking to stabilize visual odometry across extended camera motion and difficult correspondence gaps.
- [FMGS: Foundation Model Embedded 3D Gaussian Splatting for Holistic 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01970): Embeds foundation-model features into 3D Gaussian splats so rendered scenes carry semantic information for holistic 3D understanding.
- [RoboFusion: Towards Robust Multi-Modal 3D obiect Detection via SAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.03907): Uses SAM-guided multimodal fusion to improve robust 3D object detection.
- [GPT-4V(ision) is a Human-Aligned Evaluator for Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04092): Tests GPT-4V as an automatic evaluator for text-to-3D outputs and frames visual-language judging as a human-aligned feedback signal.
- [AGG: Amortized Generative 3D Gaussians for Single Image to 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04099): Generates 3D Gaussian representations from a single image through an amortized model instead of per-scene optimization.
- [Towards Real-World Aerial Vision Guidance With Categorical 6D Pose Tracker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04377): Tracks categorical 6D object poses for aerial vision guidance in real-world scenes.
- [PartSTAD: 2D-to-3D Part Segmentation Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05906): Transfers 2D part segmentation supervision into 3D part segmentation so object parts can be parsed in spatial representations.
- [TRIPS: Trilinear Point Splatting for Real‐Time Radiance Field Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06003): Uses trilinear point splatting to accelerate radiance-field rendering while retaining real-time spatial detail.
- [GroundingGPT:Language Enhanced Multi-modal Grounding Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06071): Grounds language queries in multimodal visual content, including spatially localized objects and relations.
- [SD-MVS: Segmentation-Driven Deformation Multi-View Stereo with Spherical Refinement and EM optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06385): Couples segmentation-driven deformation with spherical refinement and EM optimization for multi-view stereo reconstruction.
- [Real3D-Portrait: One-shot Realistic 3D Talking Portrait Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08503): Synthesizes realistic 3D talking portraits from one reference image by modeling facial geometry, motion, and appearance.
- [Multi-Track Timeline Control for Text-Driven 3D Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08559): Lets text-driven 3D human motion generation control multiple timeline tracks so body actions can be specified with finer temporal structure.
- [Data-Driven Physics-Informed Neural Networks: A Digital Twin Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08667): Reviews data-driven physics-informed neural networks as digital-twin models for spatially grounded simulation and prediction.
- [Efficient4D: Fast Dynamic 3D Object Generation from a Single-view Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08742): Generates dynamic 3D objects from a single-view video with a faster 4D representation.
- [Consistent3D: Towards Consistent High-Fidelity Text-to-3D Generation with Deterministic Sampling Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09050): Improves text-to-3D fidelity and view consistency by using a deterministic sampling prior during generation.
- [PIN-SLAM: LiDAR SLAM Using a Point-Based Implicit Neural Representation for Achieving Global Map Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09101): Uses point-based implicit neural maps in LiDAR SLAM to maintain global map consistency.
- [SceneVerse: Scaling 3D Vision-Language Learning for Grounded Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09340): Scales 3D vision-language learning for grounded scene understanding.
- [GARField: Group Anything with Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09419): Groups radiance-field content into spatially coherent objects or parts, connecting 3D scene representation with segmentation-style grouping.
- [GaussianBody: Clothed Human Reconstruction via 3d Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09720): Reconstructs clothed human bodies with 3D Gaussian splatting to capture pose, clothing geometry, and view-dependent appearance.
- [ParaHome: Parameterizing Everyday Home Activities Towards 3D Generative Modeling of Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10232): Parameterizes household activities so 3D generative models can synthesize structured human-object interactions.
- [S$^{3}$M-Net: Joint Learning of Semantic Segmentation and Stereo Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11414): Jointly learns semantic segmentation and stereo matching so road-scene semantics and depth estimation reinforce each other.
- [MVSFormer++: Revealing the Devil in Transformer's Details for Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11673): Refines transformer design choices for multi-view stereo to improve dense 3D reconstruction quality.
- [Fourier Transporter: Bi-Equivariant Robotic Manipulation in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12046): Uses Fourier-domain bi-equivariance to represent 3D manipulation policies that respect rotations and translations.
- [SpatialVLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12168): A VLM training method that injects spatial reasoning through spatial data, supervision, and model adaptation.
- [Template-Free Single-View 3D Human Digitalization with Diffusion-Guided LRM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12175): Combines a large reconstruction model with diffusion guidance to digitize a 3D human from one image without a fixed body template.
- [RGBD Objects in the Wild: Scaling Real-World 3D Object Learning from RGB-D Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12592): Scales real-world 3D object learning by extracting RGB-D object observations from unconstrained videos.
- [TIP-Editor: An Accurate 3D Editor Following Both Text-Prompts And Image-Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.14828): Edits 3D content with both text and image prompts, improving prompt-following control over geometry and appearance.
- [Gaussian Splashing: Dynamic Fluid Synthesis with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15318): Models dynamic fluids with Gaussian splatting so fluid motion can be synthesized as temporally changing 3D content.
- [VR-GS: A Physical Dynamics-Aware Interactive Gaussian Splatting System in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16663): Adds physical dynamics and virtual-reality interaction to Gaussian splatting for editable 3D scenes.
- [BlockFusion: Expandable 3D Scene Generation using Latent Tri-plane Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17053): Expands generated 3D scenes by extrapolating latent tri-plane representations beyond an initial spatial block.
- [SAGD: Boundary-Enhanced Segment Anything in 3D Gaussian via Gaussian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17857): Decomposes 3D Gaussians with boundary enhancement so Segment Anything-style masks can operate on Gaussian scene representations.
- [StopThePop: Sorted Gaussian Splatting for View-Consistent Real-time Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00525): Sorts Gaussian splats to reduce popping artifacts and improve view consistency during real-time rendering.
- [360-GS: Layout-Guided Panoramic Gaussian Splatting for Indoor Roaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00763): Uses indoor layout guidance to build panoramic Gaussian-splatting scenes for 360-degree roaming.
- [Geometry Transfer for Stylizing Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00863): Transfers geometric structure when stylizing radiance fields so appearance changes preserve 3D scene layout.
- [ViCA-NeRF: View-Consistency-Aware 3D Editing of Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00864): Edits NeRF scenes with explicit view-consistency constraints to avoid geometry or appearance drift across viewpoints.
- [AToM: Amortized Text-to-Mesh using 2D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00867): Amortizes text-to-mesh generation by using 2D diffusion guidance to produce 3D mesh assets.
- [GaMeS: Mesh-Based Adapting and Modification of Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01459): Uses mesh structure to adapt and modify Gaussian-splatting scenes with more controllable geometry.
- [Exploiting Low-Level Representations for Ultra-Fast Road Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02430): Uses low-level visual representations to accelerate road segmentation for spatial scene understanding in driving imagery.
- [TensoSDF: Roughness-aware Tensorial Representation for Robust Geometry and Material Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02771): Uses a roughness-aware tensorial representation for robust geometry and material reconstruction.
- [Retrieval-Augmented Score Distillation for Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02972): Retrieves relevant 3D priors to guide score distillation, improving text-to-3D generation beyond prompt-only optimization.
- [ActiveAnno3D - An Active Learning Framework for Multi-Modal 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03235): Applies active learning to select informative multimodal 3D object-detection annotations.
- [SGS-SLAM: Semantic Gaussian Splatting For Neural Dense SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03246): Fuses semantic labels with Gaussian splatting inside dense neural SLAM maps.
- [4D-Rotor Gaussian Splatting: Towards Efficient Novel View Synthesis for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03307): Uses rotor-based 4D Gaussian splatting to synthesize novel views of dynamic scenes efficiently.
- [3Doodle: Compact Abstraction of Objects with 3D Strokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03690): Represents objects as compact 3D strokes, providing an abstract but editable spatial shape model.
- [EscherNet: A Generative Model for Scalable View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03908): Scales generative view synthesis by modeling consistent scene appearance across requested viewpoints.
- [Meet JEANIE: A Similarity Measure for 3D Skeleton Sequences via Temporal-Viewpoint Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04599): Aligns 3D skeleton sequences across time and viewpoint to compare human motions in a viewpoint-aware way.
- [OV-NeRF: Open-Vocabulary Neural Radiance Fields With Vision and Language Foundation Models for 3D Semantic Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04648): Injects vision-language foundation-model features into NeRFs to support open-vocabulary 3D semantic understanding.
- [Mesh-based Gaussian Splatting for Real-time Large-scale Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04796): Anchors Gaussian splats to mesh structure so large deformations can be rendered in real time.
- [LGM: Large Multi-View Gaussian Model for High-Resolution 3D Content Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05054): Predicts high-resolution 3D Gaussian content from multiple views using a large feed-forward reconstruction model.
- [SPAD: Spatially Aware Multi-View Diffusers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05235): Conditions multi-view diffusion on spatial awareness so generated views stay geometrically consistent.
- [IM-3D: Iterative Multiview Diffusion and Reconstruction for High-Quality 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08682): Alternates multi-view diffusion with reconstruction steps to improve the quality of generated 3D assets.
- [L3GO: Language Agents with Chain-of-3D-Thoughts for Generating Unconventional Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09052): Uses language-agent decomposition and chain-of-3D-thought prompts to generate unusual 3D objects.
- [Loopy-SLAM: Dense Neural SLAM with Loop Closures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09944): Adds loop-closure handling to dense neural SLAM so reconstructed maps remain globally consistent.
- [GES: Generalized Exponential Splatting for Efficient Radiance Field Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10128): Replaces standard splat kernels with generalized exponential splatting for faster radiance-field rendering.
- [GaussianObject: High-Quality 3D Object Reconstruction from Four Views with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10259): Reconstructs high-quality 3D objects from only four views using Gaussian splatting.
- [GaussianHair: Hair Modeling and Rendering with Light-aware Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10483): Models hair geometry and appearance with light-aware Gaussians for realistic reconstruction and rendering.
- [PointMamba: A Simple State Space Model for Point Cloud Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10739): Applies Mamba-style state space modeling to point-cloud analysis as an alternative to point transformers.
- [Binary Opacity Grids: Capturing Fine Geometric Detail for Mesh-Based View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12377): Uses a discrete opacity grid, multi-ray antialiasing, entropy binarization, and fusion-based meshing to recover thin structures as compact real-time meshes.
- [MVDiffusion++: A Dense High-resolution Multi-view Diffusion Model for Single or Sparse-view 3D Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12712): Synthesizes dense high-resolution object views from one or a few images with pose-free self-attention and view dropout instead of explicit camera poses.
- [FlashTex: Fast Relightable Mesh Texturing with LightControlNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13251): Textures input meshes by generating LightControlNet reference views and refining them with SDS while separating material reflectance from lighting.
- [Hybrid Video Diffusion Models with 2D Triplane and 3D Wavelet Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13729): Encodes videos with projected 2D triplanes, 3D wavelet volumes, and frequency cues so diffusion can model spatiotemporal detail more directly.
- [Identifying Unnecessary 3D Gaussians using Clustering for Fast Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13827): Clusters nearby Gaussians offline and projects clusters at runtime to cull view-irrelevant splats before expensive rendering.
- [Real-time 3D-aware Portrait Editing from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14000): Distills a lightweight 3D portrait editor from a 3D face generator and text-to-image model so prompt edits preserve portrait geometry in real time.
- [Seamless Human Motion Composition with Blended Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15509): Introduces FlowMDM with blended absolute and relative positional encodings to generate long text-guided motion sequences with smooth transitions.
- [CLIPose: Category-Level Object Pose Estimation With Pre-Trained Vision-Language Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15726): Aligns 3D point, image, and text features with contrastive learning and pose-aware CLIP prompt tuning for real-time category-level 6D pose estimation.
- [Spec-Gaussian: Anisotropic View-Dependent Appearance for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15870): Replaces spherical harmonics with anisotropic spherical Gaussian appearance fields and coarse-to-fine training for specular 3DGS scenes.
- [GVA: Reconstructing Vivid 3D Gaussian Avatars from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16607): Refines body poses with normal and silhouette alignment and reinitializes surface-guided Gaussians for controllable monocular 3D avatars.
- [Disentangled 3D Scene Generation with Layout Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16936): Jointly optimizes per-object NeRFs and scene layouts, using a pretrained image generator as the prior for unsupervised object-level 3D scene decomposition.
- [VastGaussian: Vast 3D Gaussians for Large Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17427): Partitions large scenes into spatial cells and trains Gaussian blocks with appearance and visibility handling for city-scale reconstruction.
- [Weakly Supervised Monocular 3D Detection with a Single-View Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19144): Uses self-knowledge distillation, depth-fused 3D-like features, uncertainty-aware loss, and transfer modulation to train monocular 3D detection without 3D labels.
- [Learning Intra-View and Cross-View Geometric Knowledge for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19270): Builds ICGNet to combine interest-point geometry within each image with correspondence cues across stereo views for disparity estimation.
- [Learning to walk in confined spaces using 3D representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00187): Trains a hierarchical RL locomotion controller on 3D volumetric terrain representations so quadrupeds can navigate under overhangs and through confined spaces.
- [Can Transformers Capture Spatial Relations between Objects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00729): Defines physically grounded spatial-relation labels and introduces RelatiViT, a transformer design that improves in-the-wild object relation prediction.
- [Point Cloud Mamba: Point Cloud Learning via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00762): Serializes 3D points with consistent traverse orders, point prompts, and coordinate positional encodings so Mamba can model point clouds with linear complexity.
- [3DGStream: On-the-Fly Training of 3D Gaussians for Efficient Streaming of Photo-Realistic Free-Viewpoint Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01444): Trains per-frame 3D Gaussians online from multi-view video streams to deliver compact free-viewpoint video representations.
- [MatchU: Matching Unseen Objects for 6D Pose Estimation from RGB-D Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01517): Fuses RGB texture with rotation-invariant 3D descriptors in a Fuse-Describe-Match pipeline for 6D pose estimation on unseen objects.
- [TripoSR: Fast 3D Object Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02151): Adapts the LRM transformer architecture and training pipeline to produce a 3D mesh from one image in under half a second.
- [3DTopia: Large Text-to-3D Generation Model with Hybrid Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02234): Combines a text-conditioned tri-plane latent diffusion prior with 2D diffusion texture refinement and Objaverse caption cleaning for fast text-to-3D assets.
- [FastOcc: Accelerating 3D Occupancy Prediction by Fusing the 2D Bird’s-Eye View and Perspective View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02710): Replaces heavy 3D occupancy heads with lightweight BEV convolutions plus interpolated perspective-view voxel features for faster driving-scene occupancy prediction.
- [DART: Implicit Doppler Tomography for Radar Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03896): Uses radar-specific NeRF-style reflectance and transmittance rendering to synthesize range-Doppler views and tomographic images.
- [3D Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03954): Conditions diffusion-policy imitation learning on compact sparse point-cloud encodings, improving visuomotor generalization with few demonstrations.
- [Radiative Gaussian Splatting for Efficient X-ray Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04116): Introduces X-Gaussian with radiative point modeling, differentiable radiative rasterization, and scanner-aware cuboid initialization for fast X-ray view synthesis.
- [BAGS: Blur Agnostic Gaussian Splatting through Multi-Scale Kernel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04926): Learns per-pixel blur kernels and quality masks around 3DGS optimization so blurry captures can still reconstruct photorealistic scenes.
- [CRM: Single Image to 3D Textured Mesh with Convolutional Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05034): Generates six orthographic views, feeds them to a convolutional U-Net triplane model, and optimizes Flexicubes to output textured meshes without test-time optimization.
- [OccFusion: Depth Estimation Free Multi-sensor Fusion for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05329): Fuses camera and LiDAR features for 3D occupancy without a depth-estimation stage and adds active training and decoding modules.
- [Is Vanilla MLP in Neural Radiance Field Enough for Few-Shot View Synthesis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06092): Modifies NeRF with multi-input MLP layers, separate color-density modeling, and regularization to reduce few-shot overfitting.
- [SemGauss-SLAM: Dense Semantic Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07494): Embeds semantic features into 3D Gaussians and uses semantic-informed bundle adjustment for dense SLAM with mapping, tracking, and segmentation.
- [Robust Synthetic-to-Real Transfer for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07705): Preserves stereo domain generalization during fine-tuning by comparing ground-truth and pseudo labels through frozen, EMA, and student networks.
- [Unleashing HyDRa: Hybrid Fusion, Depth Consistency and Radar for Unified 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07746): Combines camera and radar features in perspective and BEV spaces with a Height Association Transformer and radar-weighted depth consistency.
- [StyleGaussian: Instant 3D Style Transfer with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07807): Embeds VGG features in 3D Gaussians, transfers reference-image style, and decodes with a KNN-based 3D CNN for multi-view-consistent stylization.
- [MRC-Net: 6-DoF Pose Estimation with MultiScale Residual Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08019): Classifies coarse CAD-rendered poses and regresses residuals with a multi-scale correlation layer between RGB input and rendered object views.
- [Task and Motion Planning in Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08094): Derives sparse task-and-motion planning domains from hierarchical 3D scene graphs and incrementally adds only planning-relevant objects.
- [Gaussian Splatting in Style](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08498): Stylizes 3D Gaussian scenes by learning style-aware transformations over the splatting representation while preserving 3D consistency.
- [GaussianImage: 1000 FPS Image Representation and Compression by 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08551): Represents images as 2D Gaussians with accumulated-sum rendering and vector quantization, yielding INR-like compression with much lower memory and very fast decoding.
- [GaussCtrl: Multi-View Consistent Text-Driven 3D Gaussian Splatting Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08733): Edits 3DGS scenes by rendering multi-view images, applying depth-conditioned ControlNet edits, and aligning latent attention across views for consistent text-driven changes.
- [Envision3D: One Image to 3D with Anchor Views Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08902): Generates dense multi-view-consistent images from one input through anchor-view diffusion and video-diffusion interpolation, then reconstructs textured meshes with coarse-to-fine sampling.
- [When Semantic Segmentation Meets Frequency Aliasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09065): Analyzes hard segmentation pixels as aliasing artifacts and adds de-aliasing filters plus frequency mixing modules to improve semantic and low-light instance segmentation.
- [A New Split Algorithm for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09143): Splits each Gaussian into two mathematically consistent Gaussians, making 3DGS representations more uniform, surface-bounded, and suitable for editing or point-cloud extraction.
- [Hyper-3DG: Text-to-3D Gaussian Generation via Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09236): Uses a geometry-and-texture hypergraph refiner to capture high-order correlations among 3D Gaussian attributes and reduce text-to-3D degradation such as oversmoothing and Janus artifacts.
- [Relaxing Accurate Initialization Constraint for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09413): Introduces RAIN-GS, an optimization strategy that trains 3D Gaussians from noisy or random point clouds by revising frequency-sensitive initialization behavior.
- [Reconstruction and Simulation of Elastic Objects with Spring-Mass 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09434): Integrates spring-mass dynamics into 3D Gaussian kernels so elastic objects can be reconstructed from video and simulated under new states or environments.
- [Make-Your-3D: Fast and Consistent Subject-Driven 3D Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09625): Personalizes 3D content from a single subject image by co-evolving a multi-view diffusion model with an identity-specific 2D generator for fast text-controlled customization.
- [Touch-GS: Visual-Tactile Supervised 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09875): Fuses optical tactile sensing with monocular depth into uncertainty-weighted depth supervision, improving few-view 3DGS reconstruction for opaque, reflective, and transparent objects.
- [Controllable Text-to-3D Generation via Surface-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09981): Adds MVControl and surface-aligned Gaussians bound to mesh faces so text-to-3D generation can follow depth, normal, edge, or scribble controls.
- [Visual Foundation Models Boost Cross-Modal Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for 3D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10001): Uses visual foundation models to create target-domain pseudo labels, semantically augmented frustum mixes, and cross-modal prediction fusion for 3D segmentation adaptation.
- [Texture-GS: Disentangling the Geometry and Texture for 3D Gaussian Splatting Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10050): Separates 3DGS geometry from appearance by learning UV mappings and textures over Gaussian surfaces, enabling high-fidelity appearance edits and real-time rendering.
- [GeoGS3D: Single-view 3D Reconstruction via Geometric-aware Diffusion Model and Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10242): Reconstructs single-view objects by extracting orthogonal-plane geometric features, generating consistent views, and using epipolar attention plus GDS pruning during Gaussian splatting.
- [Isotropic3D: Image-to-3D Generation Based on a Single CLIP Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10395): Conditions image-to-3D score distillation on a single CLIP image embedding, avoiding hard reference-view supervision that can flatten or distort generated geometry.
- [SWAG: Splatting in the Wild images with Appearance-conditioned Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10427): Models unconstrained photo collections with appearance-conditioned 3D Gaussians so in-the-wild illumination and capture variation can be absorbed during reconstruction.
- [GS-Pose: Generalizable Segmentation-Based 6D Object Pose Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10683): Builds a generalizable 6D pose estimator around segmentation cues and 3D Gaussian object representation rather than requiring object-specific dense depth.
- [Match-Stereo-Videos: Bidirectional Alignment for Consistent Dynamic Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10755): Introduces BiDAStereo, combining triple-frame local correlation with a motion-propagation recurrent unit to make stereo-video disparities temporally consistent.
- [N2F2: Hierarchical Scene Understanding with Nested Neural Feature Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10997): Distills CLIP-aligned hierarchical 2D segment embeddings into nested dimensions of one neural feature field for coarse-to-fine open-vocabulary 3D segmentation and localization.
- [Analytic-Splatting: Anti-Aliased 3D Gaussian Splatting via Analytic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11056): Replaces point-sampled pixel shading with an analytic Gaussian integral over each pixel footprint, improving anti-aliasing across rendering resolutions.
- [Recent advances in 3D Gaussian splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11134): Surveys 3DGS rendering, reconstruction, editing, dynamic scenes, physical simulation, and downstream applications, positioning Gaussian ellipsoids against NeRF-style implicit fields.
- [Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting For Dense Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11247): Compresses Gaussian-SLAM maps with sliding-window masking, a geometry codebook, and global bundle adjustment to reduce storage while preserving tracking and rendering quality.
- [BrightDreamer: Generic 3D Gaussian Generative Framework for Fast Text-to-3D Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11273): Generates 3D Gaussians feed-forward from text by deforming an anchor shape and predicting Gaussian attributes from a text-guided triplane generator.
- [GeoGaussian: Geometry-aware Gaussian Splatting for Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11324): Initializes thin, surface-aligned Gaussians and constrains densification and optimization to preserve scene geometry in weakly textured regions.
- [3DGS-ReLoc: 3D Gaussian Splatting for Map Representation and Visual ReLocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11367): Uses LiDAR-initialized 3DGS maps, voxel and KD-tree indexing, NCC matching, and PnP refinement for visual relocalization on KITTI360.
- [Scene-LLM: Extending Language Model for 3D Visual Understanding and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11401): Projects hybrid scene-level and egocentric 3D features into an LLM embedding space to support dense captioning, question answering, and indoor planning.
- [BAGS: Building Animatable Gaussian Splatting from a Monocular Video with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11427): Builds animatable 3D Gaussian models from monocular video by using diffusion priors and rigid regularization to compensate for limited viewpoint coverage.
- [Motion-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11447): Mines optical-flow motion cues to augment dynamic 3DGS, adding flow-to-Gaussian correspondence and transient-aware deformation supervision.
- [GenFlow: Generalizable Recurrent Flow for 6D Pose Refinement of Novel Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11510): Refines novel-object 6D poses by recurrently predicting optical flow between rendered and observed images with 3D-shape constraints and coarse-to-fine correlations.
- [BAD-Gaussians: Bundle Adjusted Deblur Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11831): Jointly optimizes Gaussian parameters and camera motion trajectories through a blur-aware image-formation model for sharp real-time reconstruction from motion-blurred captures.
- [View-Consistent 3D Editing with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11868): Adds cross-attention and editing-consistency modules to diffusion-guided 3DGS editing, reducing multi-view guidance conflicts and mode collapse.
- [LN3Diff++: Scalable Latent Neural Fields Diffusion for Speedy 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12019): Encodes images into compact 3D-aware latent neural fields, trains diffusion in that latent space, and decodes with a transformer for fast conditional 3D generation.
- [Ultraman: Single Image 3D Human Reconstruction with Ultra Speed and Detail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12028): Reconstructs textured 3D humans from one image through mesh reconstruction, multi-view-consistent human texture generation, and optimized texture mapping.
- [Generic 3D Diffusion Adapter Using Controlled Multi-View Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12032): Proposes MVEdit, a training-free 3D adapter that lifts denoised multi-view diffusion outputs into coherent textured meshes for image, text, and 3D editing tasks.
- [OV9D: Open-Vocabulary Category-Level 9D Object Pose and Size Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12396): Defines open-vocabulary 9D pose and size estimation with OO3D-9D data, DinoV2 features, and text-to-image diffusion priors for unseen categories.
- [High-Fidelity SLAM Using Gaussian Splatting with Rendering-Guided Densification and Regularized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12535): Performs RGB-D SLAM by optimizing Gaussian maps with rendering-guided densification and regularization that prevents continuous mapping from forgetting earlier views.
- [Rgbd Gs-icp Slam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12550): Couples generalized ICP tracking with a single 3DGS map, exchanging covariance information between tracking and mapping for fast dense RGB-D SLAM.
- [Planner3D: LLM-enhanced Graph Prior Meets 3D Indoor Scene Explicit Regularization.](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12848): Uses LLM-enriched scene-graph features and explicit layout regularizers to jointly generate object shapes and collision-aware indoor layouts.
- [TexDreamer: Towards Zero-Shot High-Fidelity 3D Human Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12906): Adapts a text-to-image model to semantic UV maps and adds a feature translator for seconds-level text or image conditioned 3D human texture generation.
- [GVGEN: Text-to-3D Generation with Volumetric Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12957): Arranges 3D Gaussians into a structured GaussianVolume and uses a coarse-to-fine diffusion pipeline with pruning and densification for fast text-to-3D generation.
- [Fresco: Spatial-Temporal Correspondence for Zero-Shot Video Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12962): Enforces intra-frame and inter-frame correspondences during zero-shot video diffusion translation, explicitly updating features for stronger spatial-temporal consistency.
- [Text‐to‐3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13289): Reviews text-to-3D shape generation by organizing methods around supervision data, 3D representations, differentiable rendering, and remaining limitations.
- [Gaussian Splatting on the Move: Blur and Rolling Shutter Compensation for Natural Camera Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13327): Models camera pose as changing during exposure and incorporates VIO-estimated velocities into differentiable 3DGS rendering to handle blur and rolling shutter.
- [Scaling Diffusion Models to Real-World 3D LiDAR Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13470): Reformulates diffusion noising and denoising directly on scene-scale LiDAR points, with a regularization loss that stabilizes single-scan scene completion.
- [Compress3D: a Compressed Latent Space for 3D Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13524): Compresses geometry and texture into a triplane autoencoder latent space and trains diffusion with image and inferred shape conditions for rapid single-image 3D generation.
- [DepthFM: Fast Monocular Depth Estimation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13788): Frames monocular depth as flow matching between image and depth distributions, using diffusion-model knowledge and synthetic data for faster generative depth sampling.
- [RadSplat: Radiance Field-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Robust Real- Time Rendering with 900+ FPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13806): Uses radiance fields as priors for Gaussian optimization, then prunes points and filters at test time to render complex scenes above 900 FPS.
- [Mini-Splatting: Representing Scenes with a Constrained Number of Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14166): Improves compact 3DGS by reorganizing Gaussian spatial distribution through blur split, depth reinitialization, intersection preservation, and sampling.
- [HCTO: Optimality-Aware LiDAR Inertial Odometry with Hybrid Continuous Time Optimization for Compact Wearable Mapping System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14173): Recognizes motion regimes from IMU signals, builds hybrid continuous-time factors, and selects optimal LiDAR correspondences for robust wearable mapping odometry.
- [Zero123-6D: Zero-shot Novel View Synthesis for RGB Category-level 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14279): Uses diffusion-based novel-view synthesis to obtain coarse category-level 6D poses from RGB images, then refines them online for unseen object categories.
- [SurroundSDF: Implicit 3D Scene Understanding Based on Signed Distance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14366): Predicts continuous signed-distance and semantic fields from surround-view driving images, replacing discrete voxel-only occupancy with implicit obstacle surfaces.
- [InfNeRF: Towards Infinite Scale NeRF Rendering with O(log n) Space Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14376): Extends level-of-detail rendering to NeRF by organizing scene data for logarithmic-space storage and dynamic fetching at very large scene scale.
- [HAC: Hash-grid Assisted Context for 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14530): Compresses 3DGS anchors by using structured hash-grid context to model relations among otherwise sparse, unorganized Gaussian attributes.
- [VAPO: Visibility-Aware Keypoint Localization for Efficient 6DoF Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14559): Localizes only visibility-relevant 3D keypoints and generates visibility labels to improve 3D-to-2D correspondences for instance-level 6DoF pose.
- [DreamReward: Text-to-3D Generation with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14613): Collects 25k expert text-to-3D comparisons, trains the Reward3D preference model, and uses that reward to steer text-to-3D generation.
- [GRM: Large Gaussian Reconstruction Model for Efficient 3D Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14621): Uses a feed-forward transformer to turn sparse multi-view pixels into pixel-aligned 3D Gaussians, reconstructing assets in roughly 0.1 seconds.
- [MVSplat: Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14627): Constructs efficient 3D Gaussian splats from sparse multi-view images, fitting the spatial reconstruction model section.
- [STAG4D: Spatial-Temporal Anchored Generative 4D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14939): Initializes multi-view dynamic Gaussians from diffusion models and optimizes spatial-temporal anchors for high-fidelity, consistent 4D generation.
- [DreamFlow: High-Quality Text-to-3D Generation by Approximating Probability Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14966): Reduces score-distillation gradient variance by approximating probability flow, speeding and stabilizing text-to-3D optimization.
- [Tri-Perspective view Decomposition for Geometry-Aware Depth Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15008): Decomposes sparse depth into three perspective views so depth completion can capture fine-grained 3D geometry beyond 2D maps or raw point compensation.
- [ThemeStation: Generating Theme-Aware 3D Assets from Few Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15383): Performs few-exemplar 3D-to-3D generation, producing new assets that preserve a shared theme while varying individual geometry and appearance.
- [LATTE3D: Large-scale Amortized Text-To-Enhanced3D Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15385): Scales amortized text-to-3D synthesis so prompts can produce detailed 3D assets quickly instead of relying on per-prompt hour-long optimization.
- [Semantic Gaussians: Open-Vocabulary Scene Understanding with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15624): Optimizes 3D Gaussian splats with semantic features so one representation supports both photorealistic rendering and open-vocabulary scene understanding.
- [SceneX: Procedural Controllable Large-scale Scene Generation via Large-language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15698): Uses PCGHub assets and API documents plus a PCGPlanner that emits Blender actions, turning text descriptions into controllable procedural 3D scenes such as nature environments and unbounded cities.
- [Gaussian in the Wild: 3D Gaussian Splatting for Unconstrained Image Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15704): Handles in-the-wild photo collections by modeling point-level appearance variation and transient occluders inside a 3DGS reconstruction.
- [CG-SLAM: Efficient Dense RGB-D SLAM in a Consistent Uncertainty-aware 3D Gaussian Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16095): Replaces slow NeRF volume rendering in dense RGB-D SLAM with an uncertainty-aware 3D Gaussian field for faster consistent mapping.
- [Entity-NeRF: Detecting and Removing Moving Entities in Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16141): Combines entity segmentation, thing/stuff cues, and entity-wise stationarity statistics to mask moving urban objects before NeRF reconstructs the static background.
- [Frankenstein: Generating Semantic-Compositional 3D Scenes in One Tri-Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16210): Generates semantic-compositional 3D scenes in one tri-plane by decoding multiple SDF fields for separated parts, then training a latent diffusion model over those compositional tri-planes.
- [latentSplat: Autoencoding Variational Gaussians for Fast Generalizable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16292): Predicts semantic Gaussians in a compact 3D latent space and decodes them with a lightweight 2D generator trained on real video.
- [CVT-xRF: Contrastive In-Voxel Transformer for 3D Consistent Radiance Fields from Sparse Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16885): Samples rays through shared voxels, infers neighboring point properties with an in-voxel Transformer, and adds contrastive voxel losses to regularize sparse-input radiance fields in 3D space.
- [GSDF: 3DGS Meets SDF for Improved Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16964): Couples a 3D Gaussian branch with a neural SDF branch through mutual guidance and joint supervision, improving detailed surface reconstruction while keeping high-quality splatting renderings.
- [Comp4D: LLM-Guided Compositional 4D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16993): Uses an LLM to decompose a scene into objects and generates separate 4D object representations before composing them into a dynamic scene.
- [Learning Spatial Adaptation and Temporal Coherence in Diffusion Models for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17000): Freezes a pretrained diffusion UNet/VAE and learns spatial feature adaptation plus tubelet temporal feature alignment modules to guide video super-resolution across pixels and frames.
- [Is Your LiDAR Placement Optimized for 3D Scene Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17009): Introduces Place3D, optimizing multi-LiDAR layouts with a semantic-occupancy-grid surrogate metric and validating placements on 280k clean/adverse driving frames for segmentation and 3D detection.
- [Generation of Asset Administration Shell With Large Language Model Agents: Toward Semantic Interoperability in Digital Twins in the Context of Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17209): Off-axis for core spatial modeling; it uses LLM-generated semantic-node structures to produce Asset Administration Shell digital-twin metadata rather than reconstructing or reasoning over geometry.
- [LaRE2: Latent Reconstruction Error Based Method for Diffusion-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17465): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it detects diffusion-generated images by refining visual features with latent reconstruction error rather than contributing 3D reconstruction or geometry reasoning.
- [WordRobe: Text-Guided Generation of Textured 3D Garments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17541): Learns a disentangled latent garment space with coarse-to-fine training, then aligns it with text prompts to generate textured unposed 3D garment meshes.
- [Octree-GS: Towards Consistent Real-time Rendering with LOD-Structured 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17898): Structures Gaussians with an octree level-of-detail hierarchy so zoomed-out large scenes avoid frustum overload and unstable rendering speed.
- [EgoLifter: Open-world 3D Segmentation for Egocentric Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18118): Builds object-level egocentric scene decomposition by using 3D Gaussians as the representation and SAM masks as weak supervision.
- [Object Pose Estimation via the Aggregation of Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18791): Aggregates multi-granularity Stable Diffusion features for object pose estimation, improving generalization to unseen objects in 3D scene understanding.
- [Gamba: Marry Gaussian Splatting with Mamba for single view 3D reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18795): Combines Gaussian splatting with a Mamba-style sequence model for millisecond single-image 3D reconstruction with lower memory than SDS optimization.
- [Garment3DGen: 3D Garment Stylization and Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18816): Uses image-to-3D diffusion outputs as pseudo targets, deforms a simulation-ready garment template to preserve topology, and generates globally and locally consistent texture maps from image guidance.
- [Lift3D: Zero-Shot Lifting of Any 2D Vision Model to 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18922): Trains on DINO and CLIP feature spaces to predict unseen views, then zero-shot lifts new 2D operators such as style transfer, super-resolution, colorization, and open-vocabulary segmentation into 3D.
- [Instance-Adaptive and Geometric-Aware Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19527): Learns instance-adaptive and geometric-aware keypoints for category-level 6D object pose estimation.
- [TOD3Cap: Towards 3D Dense Captioning in Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19589): Defines outdoor 3D dense captioning from LiDAR plus panoramic RGB input and predicts object boxes with captions using a BEV-centered TOD3Cap network.
- [SA-GS: Scale-Adaptive Gaussian Splatting for Training-Free Anti-Aliasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19615): Adds a test-time 2D scale-adaptive filter to pretrained 3D Gaussian fields, improving anti-aliasing without retraining the splatting model.
- [InterDreamer: Zero-Shot Text to 3D Dynamic Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19652): Generates dynamic 3D human-object interactions without paired HOI training data by decoupling interaction semantics from dynamics and combining LLM and text-to-motion priors.
- [GaussianCube: Structuring Gaussian Splatting using Optimal Transport for 3D Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19655): Fits a fixed set of high-accuracy Gaussians and rearranges them into a voxel grid with optimal transport, making 3DGS usable with standard 3D U-Net diffusion backbones.
- [HO-Gaussian: Hybrid Optimization of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20032): Combines grid-based volume modeling with 3DGS, point densification, Gaussian direction encoding, and sky handling to render urban scenes without relying on SfM initialization.
- [HGS-Mapping: Online Dense Mapping Using Hybrid Gaussian Representation in Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20159): Builds online urban dense maps with hybrid Gaussian representations that combine LiDAR-covered geometry and extrapolated regions while reducing large-scene computation.
- [InstantSplat: Sparse-view Gaussian Splatting in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20309): Performs sparse-view Gaussian splatting quickly, improving practical 3D reconstruction from limited images.
- [3DGSR: Implicit Surface Reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00409): Aligns 3D Gaussians with an implicit SDF through a differentiable SDF-to-opacity transform, using splatting supervision to recover detailed surfaces.
- [MM3DGS SLAM: Multi-modal 3D Gaussian Splatting for SLAM Using Vision, Depth, and Inertial Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00923): Tracks and maps with unposed images, inertial preintegration, depth estimates, and photometric rendering losses over a 3D Gaussian map, adding the UT-MM robot dataset.
- [FlexiDreamer: Single Image-to-3D Generation with FlexiCubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00987): Reconstructs meshes directly from diffusion-generated multi-view images with FlexiCubes optimization, avoiding slow implicit-field training and post-hoc mesh extraction.
- [HAHA: Highly Articulated Gaussian Human Avatars with Textured Mesh Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01053): Renders SMPL-X driven humans with a textured mesh plus Gaussians only where needed, such as hair and loose clothing, reducing splat count while preserving articulated fingers and novel-pose quality.
- [PhysReaction: Physically Plausible Real-Time Humanoid Reaction Synthesis via Forward Dynamics Guided 4D Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01081): Uses forward-dynamics-guided 4D imitation to synthesize real-time humanoid reactions while reducing kinematic artifacts such as foot sliding and penetration.
- [CityGaussian: Real-time High-quality Large-Scale Scene Rendering with Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01133): Trains large-scale 3DGS with divide-and-conquer scene fusion and renders across scales using compressed level-of-detail Gaussian primitives.
- [Mirror-3DGS: Incorporating Mirror Reflections into 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01168): Adds mirror attributes and plane-mirror imaging to 3DGS, rendering a virtual viewpoint behind the mirror so real-time novel views preserve reflected geometry and appearance.
- [Getting it Right: Improving Spatial Consistency in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01197): Creates the SPRIGHT spatial-relation caption dataset from six million images and shows that fine-tuning on spatially focused data improves text-to-image placement accuracy.
- [Feature Splatting: Language-Driven Physics-Based Scene Synthesis and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01223): Distills object-centric vision-language features into 3D Gaussians, then uses text queries to decompose scenes and assign simulator material properties for physics-based edits.
- [Neural Implicit Representation for Building Digital Twins of Unknown Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01440): Reconstructs unknown articulated objects from two RGB-D scans by recovering per-state shape, part segmentation, joint articulation, and point correspondences without category priors.
- [Diffusion2: Dynamic 3D Content Generation via Score Composition of Orthogonal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02148): Composes scores from separate video and multi-view diffusion models so generated 4D assets inherit both temporal smoothness and geometric consistency.
- [Segment Any 3D Object with Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02157): Builds SOLE, a semantic and geometry-aware 3D visual-language segmentation model that fuses multimodal features and supervises language-mask associations directly on point clouds.
- [LiDAR4D: Dynamic Neural Fields for Novel Space-Time View LiDAR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02742): Builds a LiDAR-only 4D neural field with multi-planar and grid features, point-cloud geometric constraints, and ray-drop optimization for dynamic space-time LiDAR synthesis.
- [iSeg: Interactive 3D Segmentation via Interactive Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03219): Performs shape segmentation directly in 3D from positive and negative surface clicks, using an interactive attention module that handles varying click counts and types.
- [DreamScene: 3D Gaussian-based Text-to-3D Scene Generation via Formation Pattern Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03575): Generates text-conditioned 3D scenes with Formation Pattern Sampling, Gaussian filtering, reconstruction-based texture generation, and progressive camera sampling for indoor and outdoor layouts.
- [Per-Gaussian Embedding-Based Deformation for Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03613): Models dynamic 3DGS deformation as functions of per-Gaussian and temporal embeddings, with coarse and fine deformation fields plus local smoothness regularization.
- [WorDepth: Variational Language Prior for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03635): Uses variational language priors to improve monocular depth estimation, connecting textual world knowledge with spatial prediction.
- [MVD-Fusion: Single-view 3D via Depth-consistent Multi-view Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03656): Trains a diffusion model to generate mutually consistent multi-view RGB-D images from one input view, using intermediate depth reprojection to constrain single-view 3D inference.
- [Know Your Neighbors: Improving Single-View Reconstruction via Spatial Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03658): Modulates point features with vision-language semantics and aggregates them through language-guided spatial attention so single-view reconstruction can infer occluded geometry from nearby 3D context.
- [Robust Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04211): Makes 3DGS robust to handheld captures by modeling motion blur as a pose distribution and adding defocus and color-inconsistency compensation.
- [SpatialTracker: Tracking Any 2D Pixels in 3D Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04319): Tracks arbitrary 2D pixels in 3D space, supporting spatial-temporal grounding.
- [StylizedGS: Controllable Stylization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05220): Fine-tunes 3DGS geometry and color with nearest-neighbor style loss, depth preservation, floater filtering, and user controls over color, scale, and stylized regions.
- [Rethinking the Spatial Inconsistency in Classifier-Free Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05384): Replaces global CFG with semantic-aware regional guidance by segmenting latent patches from attention maps at each denoising step and rescaling token guidance per semantic region.
- [3DMambaIPF: A State Space Model for Iterative Point Cloud Filtering via Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05522): Uses a Mamba selective state-space backbone for large-scale point-cloud denoising and adds differentiable rendering loss to keep filtered points near realistic object surfaces.
- [Revising Densification in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06109): Replaces heuristic adaptive density control with pixel-error-driven Gaussian densification, primitive-count control, and corrected opacity handling during cloning.
- [Playing to Vision Foundation Model's Strengths in Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06261): Adapts ViT foundation features for stereo with spatial differentiation, patch attention fusion, and cross-attention modules before a cost-volume stereo backend.
- [3D Geometry-aware Deformable Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06270): Learns dynamic 3DGS deformations with explicit 3D geometry features so moving Gaussians stay geometrically coherent across time.
- [Magic-Boost: Boost 3D Generation with Mutli-View Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06429): Refines coarse generated assets with a multi-view conditioned diffusion model that extracts 3D priors from synthesized views and iteratively improves local geometry and texture.
- [QueSTMaps: Queryable Semantic Topological Maps for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06442): Extracts indoor floorplan topology from multi-channel occupancy, labels rooms with object-conditioned CLIP-aligned features, and supports natural-language room queries.
- [Reconstructing Hand-Held Objects in 3D from Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06507): Combines hand-conditioned single-frame geometry, GPT-4V prompted retrieval of a matching 3D object, and temporal alignment to reconstruct hand-held objects in videos.
- [DreamScene360: Unconstrained Text-to-3D Scene Generation with Panoramic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06903): Uses prompt-refined panoramic diffusion, globally aligned monocular depth, and panoramic 3D Gaussians to create explorable 360-degree text-to-3D scenes.
- [Gaussian-LIC: Real-Time Photo-Realistic SLAM with Gaussian Splatting and LiDAR-Inertial-Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06926): Tightly fuses LiDAR, IMU, and camera tracking, initializes Gaussians from LiDAR plus triangulated visual points, and handles sky/exposure for real-time unbounded-scene mapping.
- [Fast Encoder-Based 3D from Casual Videos via Point Track Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07097): Introduces TracksTo4D, a feed-forward architecture over 2D point tracks that estimates dynamic 3D structure and camera poses from casual videos.
- [InstantMesh: Efficient 3D Mesh Generation from a Single Image with Sparse-view Large Reconstruction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07191): Generates 3D meshes from a single image with sparse-view reconstruction models.
- [RealmDreamer: Text-Driven 3D Scene Generation with Inpainting and Depth Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07199): Optimizes 3D Gaussians from text using inpainting diffusion for low-variance supervision of unseen regions and depth diffusion for high-fidelity scene geometry.
- [Learning to Localize Objects Improves Spatial Reasoning in Visual-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07449): Fine-tunes VLMs with image-coordinate instruction objectives, pseudo-data, and coordinate-representation ablations to improve left-right and object-localization spatial reasoning.
- [Stereo-LiDAR Depth Estimation with Deformable Propagation and Learned Disparity-Depth Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07545): Propagates sparse LiDAR hints into semi-dense confidence maps with learned deformable windows and converts stereo disparity to depth with a learned module for distant regions.
- [Reinforcement Learning with Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07950): Uses generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting as an explicit environment representation for vision-based RL, improving RoboMimic manipulation performance over image, point, voxel, and NeRF baselines.
- [Gaga: Group Any Gaussians via 3D-aware Memory Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07977): Associates inconsistent 2D zero-shot masks across camera poses with a 3D-aware memory bank, producing open-world Gaussian scene grouping from sparse or unordered views.
- [View Selection for 3D Captioning via Diffusion Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07984): Ranks rendered object views with a pretrained text-to-3D model before GPT-4V captioning, reducing hallucinated 3D object captions and correcting Cap3D-scale annotations.
- [Any2Point: Empowering Any-modality Large Models for Efficient 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07989): Adapts frozen vision, language, or audio transformers to point-cloud understanding through 3D-to-1D or 3D-to-2D virtual projection and parameter-efficient tuning.
- [GoMAvatar: Efficient Animatable Human Modeling from Monocular Video Using Gaussians-on-Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07991): Represents monocular human avatars with Gaussians-on-Mesh, combining deformable mesh compatibility with Gaussian rendering for real-time novel-pose animation at small memory footprint.
- [Probing the 3D Awareness of Visual Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08636): Diagnoses whether frozen visual foundation features encode 3D structure and cross-view surface consistency using task-specific probes and zero-shot 3D inference tests.
- [COCONut: Modernizing COCO Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08639): Off-axis for 3D spatial modeling; it revises COCO segmentation annotations and training data quality for 2D segmentation rather than geometry, pose, or scene reconstruction.
- [DeDoDe v2: Analyzing and Improving the DeDoDe Keypoint Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08928): Analyzes and improves the DeDoDe keypoint detector for local-feature matching.
- [DeferredGS: Decoupled and Relightable Gaussian Splatting With Deferred Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09412): Adds texture, normal, and learnable environment-map attributes to Gaussians, distills normals from a joint SDF, and uses deferred shading for editable relighting.
- [kNN-CLIP: Retrieval Enables Training-Free Segmentation on Continually Expanding Large Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09447): Extends open-vocabulary semantic and panoptic segmentation with a compact k-nearest-neighbor database of instance embeddings, avoiding retraining and catastrophic forgetting.
- [CompGS: Efficient 3D Scene Representation via Compressed Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09458): Compresses 3DGS with anchor primitives, residual primitive prediction, and rate-constrained optimization to reduce representation bitrate without losing rendering quality.
- [SparseOcc: Rethinking Sparse Latent Representation for Vision-Based Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09502): Replaces dense occupancy latents with lossless sparse 3D latents, sparse diffusion completion, feature pyramids, interpolation, and a sparse transformer head for semantic occupancy prediction.
- [3D Gaussian Splatting as Markov Chain Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09591): Recasts 3D Gaussians as MCMC samples, converts updates to stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics, and reframes cloning and pruning as sample-state transitions.
- [SRGS: Super-Resolution 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10318): Formalizes 3DGS super-resolution as a modular objective with 2D prior injection and geometry-aware cross-view regularization for low-resolution multi-view captures.
- [AbsGS: Recovering Fine Details for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10484): Diagnoses gradient collision in 3DGS densification and uses homodirectional view-space positional gradients to split large Gaussians in high-frequency regions.
- [Gaussian Opacity Fields: Efficient Adaptive Surface Reconstruction in Unbounded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10772): Extracts surfaces directly from 3DGS by ray-tracing Gaussian opacity level sets and regularizing normals at ray-Gaussian intersection planes.
- [Closely Interactive Human Reconstruction with Proxemics and Physics-Guided Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11291): Reconstructs closely interacting people from monocular video with a VQ-VAE interaction prior and proxemics-plus-physics-guided diffusion refinement.
- [Learning with 3D rotations, a hitchhiker's guide to SO(3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11735): Surveys SO(3) rotation representations for deep learning, comparing how representation choices affect gradient optimization for rotation inputs and outputs.
- [From Form(s) to Meaning: Probing the Semantic Depths of Language Models Using Multisense Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12145): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it probes LLM semantic understanding with multisense consistency tests rather than a 3D or geometry-specific model.
- [MeshLRM: Large Reconstruction Model for High-Quality Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12385): Fine-tunes a NeRF-style large reconstruction model with differentiable mesh extraction and rendering, producing high-quality meshes from four images in under a second.
- [EfficientGS: Streamlining Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale High-Resolution Scene Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12777): Makes large high-resolution 3DGS scenes cheaper by limiting densification to key primitives, pruning auxiliary Gaussians, and sparsely increasing spherical-harmonic order.
- [Learn2Talk: 3D Talking Face Learns From 2D Talking Face](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12888): Transfers 2D talking-face expertise into 3D by training a 3D audio-visual lip-sync expert and distilling a 2D talking-face teacher for audio-to-3D facial motion regression.
- [PhysDreamer: Physics-Based Interaction with 3D Objects via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13026): Distills video-generation priors into physics-based 3D object dynamics, estimating material behavior so static objects respond plausibly to new interactions.
- [GScream: Learning 3D Geometry and Feature Consistent Gaussian Splatting for Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13679): Removes objects from 3DGS by online depth-guided Gaussian registration plus cross-attention feature propagation between uncertain and certain regions to restore geometry and texture.
- [MaterialSeg3D: Segmenting Dense Materials from 2D Priors for 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13923): Infers dense 3D material maps from 2D semantic priors, fusing per-view UV material stacks with weighted voting and region unification after training on the MIO material dataset.
- [CLIP-GS: CLIP-Informed Gaussian Splatting for View-Consistent 3D Indoor Semantic Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14249): Compresses CLIP semantics into compact Gaussian attributes and adds 3D coherent regularization for efficient, view-consistent open-vocabulary indoor scene understanding.
- [X-Ray: A Sequential 3D Representation for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14329): Converts a 3D object into layered surface frames containing depth, normal, and color along camera rays, then applies X-Ray diffusion and upsampling for single-image 3D generation.
- [GeoDiffuser: Geometry-Based Image Editing with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14403): Treats image edits as geometric transforms and injects 2D or 3D object translation, rotation, removal, and disocclusion constraints into diffusion attention without training.
- [Mamba3D: Enhancing Local Features for 3D Point Cloud Analysis via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14966): Combines Local Norm Pooling with bidirectional state-space modeling over token and feature-channel directions, giving point-cloud classification and segmentation linear complexity.
- [OccGen: Generative Multi-modal 3D Occupancy Prediction for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15014): Casts occupancy prediction as noise-to-occupancy diffusion, conditioning denoising on camera or LiDAR features to refine dense 3D semantic occupancy and estimate uncertainty.
- [Re-Thinking Inverse Graphics With Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15228): Uses an inverse-graphics LLM framework to infer scene variables such as 3D shape, color, and material from images for zero-shot inverse rendering.
- [GaussianTalker: Real-Time Talking Head Synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16012): Builds a canonical 3D Gaussian head and audio-conditioned deformation network with spatial-audio attention to update Gaussian attributes for real-time pose-controllable talking heads.
- [NeRF-XL: Scaling NeRFs with Multiple GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16221): Distributes NeRF training and rendering across multiple GPUs with a formulation equivalent to single-GPU NeRFs, enabling arbitrarily large radiance-field capacity.
- [Point-JEPA: A Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Self-Supervised Learning on Point Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16432): Adapts JEPA-style self-supervision to point clouds with point ordering, masked target blocks, and latent-space prediction instead of input reconstruction or extra modalities.
- [Interactive3D: Create What You Want by Interactive 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16510): Adds an interactive 3D generation loop where users refine generated objects through iterative text, sketch, and regional controls rather than one-shot prompting.
- [PhyRecon: Physically Plausible Neural Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16666): Jointly learns neural implicit surfaces with differentiable rendering and a particle-based differentiable physics simulator, using SP-MC surface extraction and uncertainty-guided physical losses.
- [Make-it-Real: Unleashing Large Multimodal Model's Ability for Painting 3D Objects with Realistic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16829): Uses GPT-4V-guided material reasoning to assign realistic physically based materials to 3D objects, reducing manual texture and material painting.
- [Multi-view Image Prompted Multi-view Diffusion for Improved 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17419): Extends ImageDream into MultiImageDream, conditioning multi-view diffusion on several image prompts so 3D generation preserves object evidence across views.
- [MaPa: Text-driven Photorealistic Material Painting for 3D Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17569): Generates segment-wise procedural material graphs for meshes by using segment-controlled diffusion images to initialize material parameters and differentiable rendering to fit text-described appearance.
- [BlenderAlchemy: Editing 3D Graphics with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17672): Uses vision-language models to translate graphics-editing goals into Blender operations, automating material-node and scene-edit sequences that are otherwise hand-authored.
- [LMM-PCQA: Assisting Point Cloud Quality Assessment with LMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18203): Teaches an LMM point-cloud quality assessment knowledge through text prompts and demonstrates whether low-level 3D distortion judgments transfer to point clouds.
- [Reconstructing Satellites in 3D from Amateur Telescope Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18394): Reconstructs low-Earth-orbit satellites from noisy ground telescope images using hybrid preprocessing, controlled Gaussian Splatting, and branch-and-bound pose search.
- [Bootstrap-GS: Self-Supervised Augmentation for High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18669): Bootstraps 3DGS with self-supervised pseudo-view augmentation so training covers larger view deviations and reduces zoom-related dilation and aliasing.
- [DGE: Direct Gaussian 3D Editing by Consistent Multi-view Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18929): Makes InstructPix2Pix-style edits multi-view consistent with 3D geometry cues, then directly optimizes a 3DGS scene from the edited image sequence for faster language-driven 3D editing.
- [GS-LRM: Large Reconstruction Model for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19702): Predicts per-pixel 3D Gaussian parameters from two to four posed sparse images with a transformer-based large reconstruction model, enabling sub-second feed-forward 3DGS.
- [Invisible Stitch: Generating Smooth 3D Scenes with Depth Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19758): Trains a depth-completion model with teacher distillation and self-training to inpaint geometry where generated scene views are stitched into an existing 3D representation.
- [Lightplane: Highly-Scalable Components for Neural 3D Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19760): Provides scalable render and splatter operators for mapping between images and neural 3D fields, targeting the memory bottleneck in 2D-supervised 3D reconstruction and generation.
- [Spectrally Pruned Gaussian Fields with Neural Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.00676): Compresses 3DGS by pruning spectrally redundant Gaussian primitives and using neural compensation to preserve rendering quality after memory reduction.
- [MiniGPT-3D: Efficiently Aligning 3D Point Clouds with Large Language Models using 2D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01413): Aligns point clouds to an LLM through 2D-LLM priors, four-stage cascaded training, query-expert aggregation, and LoRA/Norm tuning with 47.8M learnable parameters.
- [DreamScene4D: Dynamic Multi-Object Scene Generation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02280): Lifts monocular videos into complete multi-object 4D scenes by combining view-predictive priors with motion-aware optimization for dynamic scene generation.
- [A Construct-Optimize Approach to Sparse View Synthesis without Camera Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03659): Builds pose-free sparse-view 3DGS by back-projecting monocular-depth pixels, registering rendered and input views with 2D correspondences, and jointly adjusting camera poses and depths.
- [Language-Image Models with 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03685): Introduces Cube-LLM, pretraining MLLMs on the LV3D 2D/3D QA corpus so box prompts and chain-of-thought instructions support 3D grounded reasoning in driving scenes.
- [Tactile-Augmented Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04534): Registers sparse touch probes into a NeRF capture and trains a conditional diffusion model to generate tactile signals from rendered RGB-D views in a shared 3D space.
- [OpenESS: Event-Based Semantic Scene Understanding with Open Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05259): Adds open-vocabulary semantic scene understanding for event-based visual sensors.
- [Coin3D: Controllable and Interactive 3D Assets Generation with Proxy-Guided Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08054): Brings 2D-style sketch, pose, and masked inpainting controls to 3D asset generation through proxy-guided conditioning.
- [CAT3D: Create Anything in 3D with Multi-View Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10314): Uses multi-view diffusion to create 3D assets, fitting spatial generation models.
- [Grounded 3D-LLM with Referent Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10370): Inserts scene referent tokens into 3D LMM sequences, pretrains phrase-region alignment on one million scene-text correspondences, and unifies dense captioning, QA, detection, and grounding.
- [Unifying 3D Vision-Language Understanding via Promptable Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11442): Uses promptable queries to retrieve task-specific information from voxels, point clouds, and multi-view images in a shared 3D coordinate space, supporting ten 3D-VL tasks.
- [Depth Prompting for Sensor-Agnostic Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11867): Uses sensor-specific depth prompts to compensate for density, scan-pattern, and range biases so one depth estimator can adapt across depth sensors.
- [MirrorGaussian: Reflecting 3D Gaussians for Reconstructing Mirror Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11921): Jointly optimizes real-world Gaussians and a mirror plane, using a dual-rendering strategy that reflects Gaussians into virtual mirror space for real-time mirror-scene reconstruction.
- [CoR-GS: Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting via Co-Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12110): Trains paired Gaussian radiance fields with point and prediction co-regularization to reduce sparse-view 3DGS overfitting.
- [Fast Generalizable Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction from Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12218): Builds MVSGaussian from multi-view stereo features, decoding geometry-aware Gaussian parameters and using hybrid rendering for fast generalizable reconstruction of unseen scenes.
- [GarmentDreamer: 3DGS Guided Garment Synthesis with Diverse Geometry and Texture Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12420): Uses 3DGS guidance to synthesize garments with varied geometry and texture, addressing multi-view inconsistency in diffusion-based garment generation.
- [MOSS: Motion-based 3D Clothed Human Synthesis from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12806): Uses kinematic Gaussian locating splatting with matrix-Fisher motion propagation plus a surface deformation detector to reconstruct motion-aware clothed humans from monocular video.
- [Talk2Radar: Bridging Natural Language with 4D mmWave Radar for 3D Referring Expression Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12821): Grounds natural-language referring expressions in 4D mmWave radar observations, emphasizing qualitative and quantitative 3D object queries beyond camera-only perception.
- [EgoChoir: Capturing 3D Human-Object Interaction Regions from Egocentric Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13659): Predicts where egocentric human-object interactions occur in 3D space, linking interaction semantics with spatial contact and operation regions.
- [Context and Geometry Aware Voxel Transformer for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13675): Builds CGFormer with image-specific context-aware queries, 3D deformable cross-attention, and voxel/TPV representations to resolve depth ambiguity in semantic scene completion.
- [DOGS: Distributed-Oriented Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale 3D Reconstruction Via Gaussian Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13943): Splits large-scale 3DGS training across distributed workers and aligns local Gaussian fields through Gaussian consensus for faster reconstruction.
- [D-MiSo: Editing Dynamic 3D Scenes using Multi-Gaussians Soup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14276): Links parameterized dynamic Gaussian splats into a mesh-inspired Triangle Soup so object trajectories in a dynamic 3D scene can be edited independently over time.
- [LDM: Large Tensorial SDF Model for Textured Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14580): Generates textured meshes by converting image or text prompts into sparse multi-view images, predicting a tensorial SDF with a Transformer, and refining it through differentiable mesh optimization.
- [Direct3D: Scalable Image-to-3D Generation via 3D Latent Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14832): Encodes high-resolution shapes into continuous triplane latents with D3D-VAE and models them with D3D-DiT using semantic and pixel image conditions for native 3D generation.
- [Generative Camera Dolly: Extreme Monocular Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14868): Performs depth-free video-to-video novel-view synthesis from a monocular dynamic video, conditioning a diffusion prior on relative camera pose parameters learned from synthetic multi-view video.
- [NeRF-Casting: Improved View-Dependent Appearance with Consistent Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14871): Casts reflection rays through the NeRF representation and decodes traced feature vectors, producing consistent close-range specular reflections without a large outgoing-radiance network.
- [Federated Online Adaptation for Deep Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14873): Adapts deep stereo networks collaboratively across distributed clients, letting resource-constrained deployments improve from online stereo experience without centralizing data.
- [EvGGS: A Collaborative Learning Framework for Event-based Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14959): Reconstructs 3D scenes from sparse, colorless event streams with a collaborative learning framework for event-based generalizable Gaussian splatting.
