- [CraftsMan3D: High-fidelity Mesh Generation with 3D Native Generation and Interactive Geometry Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14979): Generates regular, detailed 3D meshes with a native 3D generative model and an interactive geometry refiner for user-guided shape correction.
- [GS-Hider: Hiding Messages into 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15118): Replaces 3DGS spherical-harmonic attributes with secured coupled features and decodes both RGB scenes and hidden multimodal messages, adding steganography to explicit Gaussian assets.
- [HDR-GS: Efficient High Dynamic Range Novel View Synthesis at 1000x Speed via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15125): Replaces NeRF-style HDR novel-view synthesis with Gaussian splatting to render high dynamic range scenes much faster while preserving wide brightness detail.
- [DisC-GS: Discontinuity-aware Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15196): Adds discontinuity-aware rendering to Gaussian Splatting and approximates Bezier-boundary gradients so object boundaries remain differentiable during optimization.
- [PointRWKV: Efficient RWKV-Like Model for Hierarchical Point Cloud Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15214): Adapts RWKV recurrence to point patches with matrix-valued states, dynamic attention recurrence, near-neighbor graph stabilization, and multi-scale hierarchy for point-cloud tasks.
- [Diff3DS: Generating View-Consistent 3D Sketch via Differentiable Curve Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15305): Generates 3D sketches with differentiable curve rendering so learned sketch primitives remain consistent across viewpoints.
- [NVS-Solver: Video Diffusion Model as Zero-Shot Novel View Synthesizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15364): Modulates a pretrained video diffusion sampler with warped input-view priors and adaptive score guidance, enabling training-free novel-view synthesis for static and dynamic scenes.
- [PoinTramba: A Hybrid Transformer-Mamba Framework for Point Cloud Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15463): Uses Transformers for intra-group point dependencies, Mamba for inter-group relations, and bidirectional importance-aware ordering to improve point-cloud recognition efficiency.
- [GSDeformer: Direct Cage-based Deformation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15491): Transfers cage-based point-cloud deformations onto 3D Gaussians and adjusts rotations and scales to keep deformed splats coherent in real time.
- [Feature Splatting for Better Novel View Synthesis with Low Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15518): Stores per-Gaussian feature vectors instead of only spherical harmonics, alpha-blends them in the image plane, and decodes RGB or semantic labels with viewpoint conditioning.
- [LAM3D: Large Image-Point-Cloud Alignment Model for 3D Reconstruction from Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15622): Generates point-cloud latent triplanes and aligns single-image features to those 3D triplanes, producing high-fidelity meshes from one image in roughly six seconds.
- [InstructAvatar: Text-Guided Emotion and Motion Control for Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15758): Off-axis for core 3D spatial modeling; it is a 2D talking-avatar diffusion system controlled by audio and text instructions rather than a 3D reconstruction or geometry model.
- [Splat-SLAM: Globally Optimized RGB-Only SLAM with 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16544): Performs RGB-only SLAM with a dense 3D Gaussian map that deforms under globally optimized keyframe pose and depth updates, then refines inaccurate depths with monocular estimates.
- [ID-to-3D: Expressive ID-guided 3D Heads via Score Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16570): Generates identity- and text-guided 3D heads from casual images by combining expression-aware diffusion priors with score distillation.
- [Vidu4D: Single Generated Video to High-Fidelity 4D Reconstruction with Dynamic Gaussian Surfels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16822): Optimizes dynamic Gaussian surfels from generated video, using time-varying warping fields, normal regularization, and rotation/scale refinements for text-to-4D reconstruction.
- [PyGS: Large-scale Scene Representation with Pyramidal 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16829): Initializes a pyramidal hierarchy of coarse-to-fine Gaussians from a quick grid NeRF and learns cluster-level weighting across pyramid levels for large-scale rendering.
- [PivotMesh: Generic 3D Mesh Generation via Pivot Vertices Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16890): Tokenizes meshes with a Transformer autoencoder, generates coarse pivot vertices first, and autoregressively completes face-to-vertex mesh tokens for scalable native mesh generation.
- [SA-GS: Semantic-Aware Gaussian Splatting for Large Scene Reconstruction with Geometry Constrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16923): Uses SAM/DINO semantic masks, a geometric complexity regularizer, and semantic-area splat-count bounds to improve fine-grained large-scene Gaussian geometry extraction.
- [F-3DGS: Factorized Coordinates and Representations for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17083): Factorizes 3DGS coordinates and representations to reduce computation and improve suitability for real-time, resource-constrained scene rendering.
- [GenWarp: Single Image to Novel Views with Semantic-Preserving Generative Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17251): Augments cross-view attention with self-attention and geometric warping signals so text-to-image priors decide where to warp and where to generate novel-view content.
- [DOF-GS: Adjustable Depth-of-Field 3D Gaussian Splatting for Post-Capture Refocusing, Defocus Rendering and Blur Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17351): Adds a finite-aperture camera model and differentiable defocus rendering to 3DGS for refocusing, defocus synthesis, and blur removal after capture.
- [MoSca: Dynamic Gaussian Fusion from Casual Videos via 4D Motion Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17421): Lifts casual monocular videos into 4D Motion Scaffolds, anchors Gaussians on the motion representation, and jointly optimizes deformation, appearance, focal length, and poses.
- [Reason3D: Searching and Reasoning 3D Segmentation via Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17427): Extends an LLM to point clouds and text prompts with a hierarchical mask decoder that predicts coarse location and object masks for reasoning segmentation and 3D QA.
- [GaussianFormer: Scene as Gaussians for Vision-Based 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17429): Represents driving-scene semantic occupancy as sparse Gaussians rather than dense voxels, improving resource allocation across object scales.
- [FreeSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting Towards Free-View Synthesis of Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17958): Localizes 3D Gaussians for indoor scenes with a lighter generalizable backbone, enabling free-view synthesis beyond narrow stereo interpolation.
- [EG4D: Explicit Generation of 4D Object without Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18132): Generates multi-view videos from one image, reconstructs them with dynamic Gaussian Splatting, and refines semantic defects with diffusion priors instead of SDS.
- [NegGS: Negative Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18163): Introduces negative-color Gaussians and Diff-Gaussian density ratios to approximate nonlinear structures, high-frequency color transitions, and shadows with fewer splats.
- [Intent3D: 3D Object Detection in RGB-D Scans Based on Human Intention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18295): Defines 3D intention grounding on ScanNet with 44,990 intention texts and proposes IntentNet for intention understanding, object-candidate reasoning, and cascaded adaptive detection losses.
- [3D StreetUnveiler with Semantic-Aware 2DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18416): Removes temporarily static street objects from in-car observations by using semantic-aware 2D Gaussian splatting to reconstruct empty urban scenes.
- [3DitScene: Editing Any Scene via Language-guided Disentangled Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18424): Disentangles Gaussian scenes into editable components and uses language guidance to localize and modify scene content while preserving unrelated geometry.
- [GFlow: Recovering 4D World from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18426): Recovers dynamic 3D geometry and camera poses from a single monocular video without known cameras, multiview input, or static-scene assumptions.
- [Atlas3D: Physically Constrained Self-Supporting Text-to-3D for Simulation and Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18515): Adds physical balance and self-support constraints to text-to-3D generation so produced objects are more usable in simulation and fabrication.
- [REPARO: Compositional 3D Assets Generation with Differentiable 3D Layout Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18525): Generates compositional 3D assets by aligning generated parts with a differentiable layout objective, reducing spatial mismatch between object components.
- [DGD: Dynamic 3D Gaussians Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19321): Distills dynamic 3D semantic radiance fields from a monocular video, enabling novel views plus language-specified 3D entity segmentation and tracking.
- [GaussianRoom: Improving 3D Gaussian Splatting with SDF Guidance and Monocular Cues for Indoor Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19671): Adds SDF guidance and monocular geometric cues to indoor 3DGS so sparse views recover cleaner room structure and surface geometry.
- [Improve Student’s Reasoning Generalizability through Cascading Decomposed CoTs Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19842): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it distills decomposed chain-of-thought reasoning into smaller LLMs for out-of-domain generalization rather than geometry or 3D perception.
- [PLA4D: Pixel-Level Alignments for Text-to-4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19957): Aligns video-diffusion motion priors and multiview geometry priors at the pixel level to reduce conflicting score-distillation gradients in text-to-4D Gaussian splatting.
- [MG-SLAM: Structure Gaussian Splatting SLAM With Manhattan World Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20031): Uses Manhattan-world structure priors inside RGB-D Gaussian SLAM to fill indoor reconstruction holes caused by occlusion and limited viewpoints.
- [Object-centric Reconstruction and Tracking of Dynamic Unknown Objects Using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20104): Tracks and reconstructs unknown moving objects as object-centric Gaussian models, separating dynamic object motion from the surrounding scene.
- [Evagaussians: Event Stream Assisted Gaussian Splatting from Blurry Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20224): Uses event streams to support 3DGS training from blurry images and imperfect camera poses in fast-motion or low-light capture.
- [TetSphere Splatting: Representing High-Quality Geometry with Lagrangian Volumetric Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20283): Represents shapes with deformable tetrahedral-sphere primitives and geometric constraints to improve mesh regularity and high-quality 3D modeling.
- [GECO: Generative Image-to-3D within a SECOnd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20327): Uses a feed-forward generative image-to-3D pipeline to produce 3D assets within seconds, targeting fast inference rather than per-prompt optimization.
- [VividDream: Generating 3D Scene with Ambient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20334): Builds explorable 4D scenes by expanding a prompt or image into a static 3D point cloud and animating ambient dynamics with video diffusion priors.
- [Unique3D: High-Quality and Efficient 3D Mesh Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20343): Generates high-quality meshes from single images with an efficient image-to-3D pipeline designed to avoid slow per-case score-distillation optimization.
- [Physically Compatible 3D Object Modeling from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20510): Decomposes single-image 3D object modeling into rest-shape geometry, mechanical properties, and external forces so reconstructed objects are physically plausible.
- [ContextGS: Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting with Anchor Level Context Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20721): Compresses 3DGS by modeling anchor-level context, using neighboring Gaussian structure to reduce representation size while keeping rendering quality.
- [MeshXL: Neural Coordinate Field for Generative 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20853): Represents 3D shapes with neural coordinate fields designed for large generative 3D models, improving continuous geometry decoding for mesh assets.
- [Bootstrap3D: Improving Multi-View Diffusion Model with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00093): Bootstraps multi-view diffusion with synthetic data so generated views become more consistent for downstream single-image 3D reconstruction.
- [Query2CAD: Generating CAD models using natural language queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00144): Converts natural-language CAD design queries into iteratively refinable CAD models, targeting the revision-heavy workflow of engineering design.
- [Cascade-CLIP: Cascaded Vision-Language Embeddings Alignment for Zero-Shot Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00670): Aligns CLIP intermediate visual layers with text embeddings through a cascaded design so zero-shot segmentation keeps object-detail information.
- [RaDe-GS: Rasterizing Depth in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01467): Adds depth rasterization to Gaussian splatting to improve shape extraction and detailed geometry reconstruction from otherwise unstructured splats.
- [DreamPhysics: Learning Physical Properties of Dynamic 3D Gaussians with Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01476): Learns physical properties for dynamic 3D Gaussians by distilling video diffusion priors, reducing manual material tuning for physics-based 4D animation.
- [SpatialRGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01584): A grounded spatial-reasoning method that ties language answers to region-level visual grounding and explicit spatial relations.
- [Reconstructing and Simulating Dynamic 3D Objects with Mesh-adsorbed Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01593): Adsorbs Gaussians onto mesh surfaces so dynamic objects can be reconstructed and then simulated with geometry-aware deformation.
- [End-to-End Rate-Distortion Optimized 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01597): Optimizes 3D Gaussian attributes under an explicit rate-distortion objective, jointly balancing rendering fidelity and compressed bit cost.
- [OpenGaussian: Towards Point-Level 3D Gaussian-based Open Vocabulary Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02058): Trains 3DGS features for point-level open-vocabulary understanding, addressing weak 2D-to-3D feature association in pixel-level Gaussian semantics.
- [WE-GS: An In-the-wild Efficient 3D Gaussian Representation for Unconstrained Photo Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02407): Builds efficient 3DGS representations from unconstrained photo collections by handling appearance variation and noisy capture conditions.
- [TopViewRS: Vision-Language Models as Top-View Spatial Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02537): Evaluates and adapts VLMs for top-view spatial reasoning, emphasizing map-like layouts where metric relations differ from ordinary perspective images.
- [3D-HGS: 3D Half-Gaussian Splatting*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02720): Introduces half-Gaussian kernels as a plug-in 3DGS primitive to reduce shape and color discontinuities while preserving fast rendering.
- [Ouroboros3D: Image-to-3D Generation via 3D-aware Recursive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03184): Generates 3D assets from images through 3D-aware recursive diffusion.
- [Gear-NeRF: Free-Viewpoint Rendering and Tracking with Motion-Aware Spatio-Temporal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03723): Uses motion-aware spatiotemporal sampling to couple free-viewpoint rendering with tracking, improving dynamic-scene NeRF reconstruction.
- [LLplace: The 3D Indoor Scene Layout Generation and Editing via Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03866): Uses an LLM to generate and edit 3D indoor layouts while avoiding heavy prompt engineering or proprietary-only layout inference.
- [STAR: Skeleton-aware Text-based 4D Avatar Generation with In-Network Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04629): Generates animated 3D avatars from text with skeleton-aware optimization and in-network motion retargeting to avoid pose-agnostic SDS artifacts.
- [CityCraft: A Real Crafter for 3D City Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04983): Generates 3D city scenes with a pipeline that moves beyond two-stage layout-plus-rendering baselines for urban simulation and planning assets.
- [Efficient 3D Shape Generation via Diffusion Mamba with Bidirectional SSMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05038): Replaces heavier attention blocks in 3D diffusion with bidirectional state-space modeling, improving efficient shape generation over long geometric sequences.
- [GTR: Improving Large 3D Reconstruction Models through Geometry and Texture Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05649): Refines LRM-style mesh reconstruction with geometry and texture modules that improve triplane-NeRF outputs from multi-view images.
- [Generalizable Human Gaussians from Single-View Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06050): Predicts human Gaussian representations from a single image with a generalizable model, targeting animatable human reconstruction without per-person optimization.
- [MVGamba: Unify 3D Content Generation as State Space Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06367): Casts multi-view 3D content generation as state-space sequence modeling, using Mamba-style dynamics to coordinate generated views and geometry.
- [Generative Gaussian Splatting for Unbounded 3D City Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06526): Adapts 3DGS from bounded objects to unbounded city scenes by addressing storage and representation issues in infinite-scale urban generation.
- [Beyond Bare Queries: Open-Vocabulary Object Grounding with 3D Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07113): Builds 3D scene graphs for open-vocabulary grounding so agents can resolve relational object descriptions rather than only simple object names.
- [3D-Properties: Identifying Challenges in DPO and Charting a Path Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07327): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it analyzes Direct Preference Optimization limitations for LLM alignment rather than 3D properties or geometry models.
- [Trim 3D Gaussian Splatting for Accurate Geometry Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07499): Reconstructs cleaner geometry by trimming inaccurate Gaussians while preserving reliable scene structure in a 3DGS representation.
- [An Image is Worth 32 Tokens for Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07550): Compresses images into a compact 32-token latent representation for efficient high-resolution reconstruction and generation.
- [OpenObj: Open-Vocabulary Object-Level Neural Radiance Fields With Fine-Grained Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08009): Combines object-level NeRF reconstruction with fine-grained open-vocabulary understanding to avoid blurry point-wise semantics.
- [Human 3Diffusion: Realistic Avatar Creation via Explicit 3D Consistent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08475): Uses explicit 3D-consistent diffusion priors to create realistic avatars from a single RGB image where 2D diffusion alone lacks multiview shape consistency.
- [Real3D: Scaling Up Large Reconstruction Models with Real-World Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08479): Trains large reconstruction models on single-view real images through self-training, unsupervised pixel and semantic losses, and automatic in-the-wild data curation.
- [Dual Attribute-Spatial Relation Alignment for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08907): Separates attribute and spatial-relation streams in DASANet, then fuses them with cross-attention for interpretable 3D visual grounding.
- [Beyond the Frontier: Predicting Unseen Walls From Occupancy Grids by Learning From Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09160): Predicts unobserved wall line segments from partial LiDAR occupancy grids using an autoregressive sequence model trained on floor-plan trajectories.
- [Neural Assets: 3D-Aware Multi-Object Scene Synthesis with Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09292): Replaces text tokens with per-object Neural Asset tokens that disentangle reference appearance from target 3D pose for controllable multi-object diffusion synthesis.
- [LRM-Zero: Training Large Reconstruction Models with Synthesized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09371): Trains sparse-view large reconstruction models entirely on the procedural Zeroverse dataset of primitive shapes with rich local geometry and texture.
- [WonderWorld: Interactive 3D Scene Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09394): Generates connected 3D scenes from one image with Fast Layered Gaussian Surfels and guided depth diffusion for low-latency interactive editing.
- [ConsistDreamer: 3D-Consistent 2D Diffusion for High-Fidelity Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09404): Makes instruction-guided scene editing 3D-consistent by feeding surrounding views, structured noise, and self-supervised consistency constraints into 2D diffusion.
- [ImageNet3D: Towards General-Purpose Object-Level 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09613): Adds 3D pose, location, viewpoint, and 3D-interleaved captions to 200 ImageNet categories for general object-level 3D understanding.
- [OpenECAD: An efficient visual language model for editable 3D-CAD design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09913): Fine-tunes VLMs to convert 3D design images into structured 2D sketch and 3D construction commands that remain editable in CAD tools.
- [D‐NPC: Dynamic Neural Point Clouds for Non‐Rigid View Synthesis from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10078): Represents casual monocular videos as dynamic neural point clouds with separate static and dynamic hash-encoded feature grids for non-rigid view synthesis.
- [GaussianSR: 3D Gaussian Super-Resolution with 2D Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10111): Distills 2D diffusion priors into 3D Gaussian super-resolution while annealing SDS timesteps and dropping redundant Gaussians during densification.
- [MeshAnything: Artist-Created Mesh Generation with Autoregressive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10163): Treats mesh extraction as autoregressive generation, using a VQ-VAE mesh vocabulary and shape-conditioned decoder-only transformer to produce artist-style meshes.
- [PUP 3D-GS: Principled Uncertainty Pruning for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10219): Scores Gaussian sensitivity with a second-order reconstruction-error approximation and applies multi-round prune-refine compression to pretrained 3DGS scenes.
- [L4GM: Large 4D Gaussian Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10324): Extends LGM with temporal self-attention and a multiview-video training set to output animated 4D Gaussian objects from a single-view video.
- [GeoGPT4V: Towards Geometric Multi-modal Large Language Models with Geometric Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11503): Uses GPT-4 and GPT-4V to synthesize aligned geometry problems and diagrams, improving multimodal geometry performance on MathVista and MathVision.
- [RetinaGS: Scalable Training for Dense Scene Rendering with Billion-Scale 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11836): Introduces model-parallel 3DGS training with a corrected rendering equation, scaling dense scene rendering to more than one billion primitives.
- [A Hierarchical 3D Gaussian Representation for Real-Time Rendering of Very Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12080): Organizes 3D Gaussians hierarchically so very large captures can be streamed and rendered in real time with level-of-detail control.
- [DistillNeRF: Perceiving 3D Scenes from Single-Glance Images by Distilling Neural Fields and Foundation Model Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12095): Distills neural-field geometry and foundation-model semantics from single-glance images to infer 3D scene structure beyond direct image evidence.
- [HumanSplat: Generalizable Single-Image Human Gaussian Splatting with Structure Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12459): Reconstructs animatable human Gaussian splats from one image by injecting body-structure priors into a generalizable feed-forward model.
- [4K4DGen: Panoramic 4D Generation at 4K Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13527): Generates panoramic 4D scenes at 4K resolution with a pipeline tuned for high-resolution spatial-temporal consistency.
- [SpatialBot: Precise Spatial Understanding with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13642): Trains a VLM on RGB-depth SpatialQA data and evaluates it with SpatialBench to improve depth-aware spatial understanding.
- [CLAY: A Controllable Large-scale Generative Model for Creating High-quality 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13897): Builds a controllable large-scale 3D asset generator that conditions high-quality shape creation on user-specified controls.
- [Deblurring Neural Radiance Fields with Event-driven Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14360): Uses event-driven bundle adjustment to sharpen NeRF reconstruction from blurry captures by jointly correcting camera motion and radiance fields.
- [Gaussian-Informed Continuum for Physical Property Identification and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14927): Uses Gaussian-informed continuum modeling to identify physical properties and simulate deformable objects from spatial observations.
- [Gaussian Splatting to Real World Flight Navigation Transfer with Liquid Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15149): Transfers navigation policies from Gaussian-splatting scene representations to real flight using liquid neural networks.
- [GeoLRM: Geometry-Aware Large Reconstruction Model for High-Quality 3D Gaussian Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15333): Adds geometry-aware conditioning to large reconstruction models so sparse inputs produce higher-quality 3D Gaussian assets.
- [Taming 3DGS: High-Quality Radiance Fields with Limited Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15643): Optimizes 3DGS under limited memory and compute, targeting high-quality radiance fields without full-resource training assumptions.
- [LiveScene: Language Embedding Interactive Radiance Fields for Physical Scene Rendering and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16038): Embeds language features into radiance fields so text queries can control physical scene rendering and interaction.
- [Articulate your NeRF: Unsupervised articulated object modeling via conditional view synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16623): Learns articulated object parts and motion without labels by using conditional view synthesis as the supervision signal.
- [ClotheDreamer: Text-guided garment generation with 3D gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16815): Generates text-conditioned garments as 3D Gaussians, targeting editable clothing geometry and appearance rather than flat texture transfer.
- [Mamba24/8D: Enhancing Global Interaction in Point Clouds via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17442): Uses a point-cloud state space model to capture long-range interactions more efficiently than local-only point operations.
- [Director3D: Real-world Camera Trajectory and 3D Scene Generation from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17601): Jointly generates camera trajectories and 3D scenes from text so scene creation is tied to controllable real-world camera motion.
- [RealTalk: Real-time and Realistic Audio-driven Face Generation with 3D Facial Prior-guided Identity Alignment Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18284): Uses 3D facial priors and identity alignment to drive real-time audio-conditioned talking-face generation.
- [DoubleTake: Geometry Guided Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18387): Refines monocular depth by taking a second geometry-guided pass that corrects inconsistent spatial structure.
- [GaussianDreamerPro: Text to Manipulable 3D Gaussians with Highly Enhanced Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18462): Improves text-to-3D Gaussian generation with higher-quality geometry and manipulable Gaussian representations.
- [MultiDiff: Consistent Novel View Synthesis from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18524): Uses multi-view diffusion consistency to synthesize coherent novel views from a single input image.
- [On Scaling Up 3D Gaussian Splatting Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18533): Studies scaling laws for 3DGS training, showing how primitive count, image resolution, and training resources affect reconstruction quality.
- [GS-ROR2: Bidirectional-guided 3DGS and SDF for Reflective Object Relighting and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18544): Couples 3DGS and SDF with bidirectional guidance to reconstruct and relight reflective objects.
- [Dream-in-Style: Text-to-3D Generation Using Stylized Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18581): Adapts score distillation for stylized text-to-3D generation so style guidance affects both geometry and appearance.
- [Dynamic Gaussian Marbles for Novel View Synthesis of Casual Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18717): Models casual monocular videos with dynamic Gaussian marbles that track scene motion for novel-view synthesis.
- [3D Feature Distillation with Object-Centric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18742): Distills 2D foundation-model features into 3D representations using object-centric priors for scene-level semantic consistency.
- [How Far are AI-Generated Videos from Simulating the 3D Visual World: A Learned 3D Evaluation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19568): Trains a learned 3D evaluator to measure whether generated videos preserve plausible three-dimensional scene structure.
- [EgoGaussian: Dynamic Scene Understanding from Egocentric Video with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19811): Reconstructs egocentric dynamic scenes with 3D Gaussian splatting to support scene understanding from wearable-video viewpoints.
- [SpotLessSplats: Ignoring Distractors in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.20055): Learns to ignore transient distractors during 3DGS optimization so in-the-wild scenes reconstruct stable background geometry.
- [HouseCrafter: Lifting Floorplans to 3D Scenes with 2D Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.20077): Converts floor plans into 3D indoor scenes by using 2D diffusion priors to lift layout structure into spatial assets.
- [ScanReason: Empowering 3D Visual Grounding with Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01525): Adds reasoning supervision to 3D visual grounding so models can resolve references that require spatial and relational inference.
- [ScaleDreamer: Scalable Text-to-3D Synthesis with Asynchronous Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02040): Speeds text-to-3D synthesis with asynchronous score distillation, decoupling slow diffusion guidance from 3D optimization steps.
- [BeNeRF: Neural Radiance Fields from a Single Blurry Image and Event Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02174): Reconstructs a NeRF from one blurry frame plus an event stream, using event cues to recover geometry lost to blur.
- [Meta 3D AssetGen: Text-to-Mesh Generation with High-Quality Geometry, Texture, and PBR Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02445): Generates production-oriented text-to-mesh assets with geometry, texture, and physically based rendering material outputs.
- [Meta 3D Gen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02599): Combines Meta's text-to-3D asset generation components into a system for high-quality meshes, textures, and material generation.
- [Explicitly Guided Information Interaction Network for Cross-modal Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02887): Completes point clouds by explicitly guiding information exchange between sparse geometry and complementary cross-modal cues.
- [VEGS: View Extrapolation of Urban Scenes in 3D Gaussian Splatting using Learned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02945): Uses learned urban-scene priors to extrapolate unseen viewpoints in 3DGS where direct camera coverage is sparse.
- [SegVG: Transferring Object Bounding Box to Segmentation for Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03200): Transfers box-level visual grounding into segmentation masks so grounded objects are localized with finer spatial boundaries.
- [Visual Grounding with Attention-Driven Constraint Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03243): Balances attention-driven constraints to improve visual grounding when language cues and visual regions compete.
- [GSD: View-Guided Gaussian Splatting Diffusion for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04237): Uses view-guided diffusion to predict Gaussian splats for 3D reconstruction from sparse image evidence.
- [Segment Any 4D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04504): Segments dynamic 4D Gaussian scenes with temporal identity feature fields and a refinement stage that removes segmentation artifacts.
- [LaRa: Efficient Large-Baseline Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04699): Combines Gaussian Volumes with local-global transformer group attention for efficient feed-forward large-baseline radiance fields.
- [A Likelihood-Based Generative Approach for Spatially Consistent Precipitation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04724): Fuses likelihood and adversarial objectives so sampled precipitation downscaling remains spatially consistent.
- [Multi-branch Collaborative Learning Network for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05363): Uses separate 3D referring-comprehension and referring-segmentation branches plus superpoint aggregation and soft alignment for mutual grounding gains.
- [Bringing Masked Autoencoders Explicit Contrastive Properties for Point Cloud Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05862): Turns random masking in point-cloud MAE pretraining into contrastive pairs, adding explicit contrastive behavior without heavy augmentation.
- [4D Contrastive Superflows are Dense 3D Representation Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06190): Pretrains dense 3D representations from LiDAR-camera sequences with density-invariant consistency and flow-based contrastive temporal cues.
- [Tailor3D: Customized 3D Assets Editing and Generation with Dual-Side Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06191): Edits and generates 3D assets from paired front and back images, reducing multi-view conflicts during localized customization.
- [Category-level Object Detection, Pose Estimation and Reconstruction from Stereo Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06984): Uses an implicit stereo matching module and transform decoder for joint category-level detection, pose estimation, and reconstruction.
- [3D Gaussian Ray Tracing: Fast Tracing of Particle Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07090): Ray-traces 3D Gaussian particles with bounding-volume hierarchies and depth-ordered shading to support secondary lighting effects beyond rasterization.
- [Let Occ Flow: Self-Supervised 3D Occupancy Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07587): Predicts camera-only occupancy and occupancy flow with TPV features, temporal fusion, differentiable volumetric flow rendering, and 2D self-supervision.
- [Flow4D: Leveraging 4D Voxel Network for LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07995): Temporally fuses multiple LiDAR frames in a 4D voxel network to estimate scene flow with explicit spatial-temporal features.
- [WildGaussians: 3D Gaussian Splatting in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08447): Handles occlusions and illumination changes in in-the-wild 3DGS by combining robust DINO features with an appearance modeling module.
- [STD-LLM: Understanding Both Spatial and Temporal Properties of Spatial-Temporal Data with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09096): Adapts pretrained language models to spatial-temporal forecasting and imputation by modeling temporal, connectivity, and high-order correlations.
- [Radiance Fields from Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09386): Builds radiance fields directly from single-photon camera events, enabling reconstruction under low light, high dynamic range, and high-speed motion.
- [Simplicits: Mesh-Free, Geometry-Agnostic Elastic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09497): Fits small implicit networks over occupancy queries to simulate elastic deformation for objects in arbitrary geometry representations.
- [Hamba: Single-view 3D Hand Reconstruction with Graph-guided Bi-Scanning Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09646): Reformulates Mamba scanning as graph-guided bidirectional scanning so single-image hand reconstruction captures joint spatial relations efficiently.
- [3x2: 3D Object Part Segmentation by 2D Semantic Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09648): Transfers 2D foundation-model features and semantic correspondences to 3D part segmentation under limited 3D annotation.
- [VividDreamer: Invariant Score Distillation For Hyper-Realistic Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09822): Replaces SDS reconstruction terms with an invariant score from DDIM sampling to reduce over-smoothing and over-saturation in text-to-3D.
- [Eliminating Feature Ambiguity for Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09842): Off-axis for core spatial modeling; it adds ambiguity-elimination features for 2D few-shot segmentation rather than 3D reconstruction or spatial reasoning.
- [SpikeGS: 3D Gaussian Splatting from Spike Streams with High-Speed Camera Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10062): Trains 3D Gaussian splatting from spike-camera streams by matching Gaussian rendering to instantaneous imaging and spike firing processes.
- [RAPiD-Seg: Range-Aware Pointwise Distance Distribution Networks for 3D LiDAR Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10159): Defines range-aware pointwise distance-distribution features with a double-nested autoencoder for invariant outdoor LiDAR segmentation.
- [Scaling 3D Reasoning with LMMs to Large Robot Mission Environments Using Datagraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10743): Uses datagraphs and graph traversal so LMMs can query large 3D robot environments in context-sized pieces.
- [Gaussian Splatting Lucas-Kanade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11309): Derives a Lucas-Kanade-style analytical velocity field for dynamic Gaussian splatting, enforcing motion constraints when camera parallax is weak.
- [TCFormer: Visual Recognition via Token Clustering Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11321): Off-axis for core spatial modeling; it clusters semantic 2D vision tokens for generic recognition rather than 3D or geometric scene understanding.
- [EV-GS: Event-Based Gaussian Splatting for Efficient and Accurate Radiance Field Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11343): Infers 3D Gaussian splats from monocular event-camera supervision, improving radiance-field reconstruction under fast motion and low light.
- [Centering the Value of Every Modality: Towards Efficient and Resilient Modality-agnostic Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11344): Trains segmentation to work when modalities are missing or shuffled, centering each modality's contribution instead of relying on a fixed sensor set.
- [DreamCatalyst: Fast and High-Quality 3D Editing via Controlling Editability and Identity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11394): Controls the trade-off between edit strength and identity preservation during 3D editing, aiming for faster high-quality edits than repeated SDS optimization.
- [Click-Gaussian: Interactive Segmentation to Any 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11793): Learns two-granularity feature fields for 3D Gaussians and uses Global Feature-guided Learning to cluster noisy multi-view 2D segments, enabling 10 ms click-based 3D scene segmentation without post-processing.
- [MVG-Splatting: Multi-View Guided Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Quantile-Based Geometric Consistency Densification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11840): Uses image-gradient normals and adaptive quantile densification guided by MVS-style depth maps to improve 2DGS mesh extraction while preserving rendering quality.
- [Temporally Consistent Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11950): Turns video stereo into temporal disparity completion plus dual-space iterative refinement, projecting previous disparities to the current view to stabilize depth maps over time.
- [UrbanWorld: An Urban World Model for 3D City Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11965): Builds an urban world model for 3D city generation, capturing structured road, building, and scene layout priors for controllable urban assets.
- [F-HOI: Toward Fine-grained Semantic-Aligned 3D Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12435): Introduces Semantic-HOI with over 20K state-level 3D HOI descriptions and trains F-HOI to align 2D, 3D, and language spaces for fine-grained interaction tasks.
- [EaDeblur-GS: Event assisted 3D Deblur Reconstruction with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13520): Combines event-camera data with 3DGS, an Adaptive Deviation Estimator for Gaussian-center motion, and blur-aware losses to reconstruct sharp scenes under severe camera motion.
- [SegPoint: Segment Any Point Cloud via Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13761): Uses an MLLM to infer implicit instructions and output point-wise masks across 3D instruction, referring, semantic, and open-vocabulary point-cloud segmentation, evaluated with Instruct3D.
- [Shape of Motion: 4D Reconstruction From a Single Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13764): Reconstructs temporally changing 3D structure from one video, estimating motion and geometry jointly rather than assuming a static scene.
- [Learning Visual Grounding from Generative Vision and Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14563): Prompts generative VLMs to create object-level referring expressions, explicitly modeling attributes and spatial relations to build 16M grounding queries for REC and RES transfer.
- [RGB2Point: 3D Point Cloud Generation from Single RGB Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14979): Generates point clouds from a single RGB image, learning to infer object geometry where only one perspective observation is available.
- [Enhancement of 3D Gaussian Splatting using Raw Mesh for Photorealistic Recreation of Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15435): Uses raw architectural meshes to guide 3DGS optimization, improving photorealistic building recreation when image-only splats miss structural detail.
- [6DGS: 6D Pose Estimation from a Single Image and a 3D Gaussian Splatting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15484): Inverts 3DGS rendering by sampling radiant Ellicell rays from ellipsoids and solving pixel-ray bindings, giving closed-form single-image 6DoF pose without initialization.
- [MonoWAD: Weather-Adaptive Diffusion Model for Robust Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16448): Uses weather-adaptive diffusion to improve monocular 3D detection under adverse conditions, reducing domain shift from rain, fog, or snow.
- [HDRSplat: Gaussian Splatting for High Dynamic Range 3D Scene Reconstruction from Raw Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16503): Trains 3DGS directly on 14-bit linear raw images with an HDR-suited loss and rasterization tuning, preserving saturated and dark-region content for high-dynamic-range reconstruction.
- [LangOcc: Self-Supervised Open Vocabulary Occupancy Estimation via Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17310): Learns open-vocabulary occupancy with volume-rendering supervision, connecting 3D occupancy fields to language-aligned semantic features without dense labels.
- [SV4D: Dynamic 3D Content Generation with Multi-Frame and Multi-View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17470): Generates dynamic 3D content by enforcing both multi-frame temporal coherence and multi-view geometric consistency.
- [CityX: Controllable Procedural Content Generation for Unbounded 3D Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17572): Produces unbounded 3D cities with controllable procedural generation, exposing layout and content controls for large-scale urban environments.
- [LION: Linear Group RNN for 3D Object Detection in Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18232): Applies linear group RNNs over grouped sparse voxel features, adds 3D spatial descriptors, and densifies foreground voxels for long-range point-cloud object detection without transformer quadratic cost.
- [RefMask3D: Language-Guided Transformer for 3D Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18244): Fuses language with geometrically coherent sub-clouds through Geometry-Enhanced Group-Word Attention, then decodes linguistic primitives and object clusters for 3D referring segmentation.
- [MSP-MVS: Multi-granularity Segmentation Prior Guided Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19323): Guides patch deformation in multi-view stereo with Semantic-SAM multi-granularity depth-edge priors, adaptive anchor redistribution, and edge-confined matching to stabilize reconstruction.
- [Cycle3D: High-quality and Consistent Image-to-3D Generation via Generation-Reconstruction Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19548): Cycles a 2D diffusion generator with a feed-forward 3D reconstruction module during denoising so image-to-3D generation keeps texture quality and multi-view consistency.
- [From Flat to Spatial: Comparison of 4 methods constructing 3D, 2 and 1/2D Models from 2D Plans with neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19970): Compares neural methods that lift 2D plans into 2.5D and 3D models, making the entry closer to architectural reconstruction than general LLM spatial reasoning.
- [Global Structure-from-Motion Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20219): Reworks global structure-from-motion as GLOMAP, a general-purpose open-source SfM system that matches or exceeds COLMAP accuracy while running orders of magnitude faster.
- [Improving 2D Feature Representations by 3D-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20229): Lifts 2D semantic features into a 3D Gaussian representation, re-renders them from arbitrary views, and fine-tunes 2D foundation models with 3D-aware feature supervision.
- [3D-GRES: Generalized 3D Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20664): Generalizes 3D referring segmentation beyond a fixed object vocabulary, grounding language expressions to point-level masks in 3D scenes.
- [Chat2Layout: Interactive 3D Furniture Layout With a Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21333): Uses a multimodal LLM to iteratively place and revise furniture in 3D rooms, combining visual layout state with conversational constraints.
- [A Federated Learning-Friendly Approach for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of SAM in 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21739): Adapts SAM for 3D segmentation with parameter-efficient tuning that is compatible with federated training constraints.
- [LoopSparseGS: Loop-Based Sparse-View Friendly Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00254): Uses loop-based constraints to improve sparse-view Gaussian splatting, stabilizing geometry when only a few input views are available.
- [Head360: Learning a Parametric 3D Full-Head for Free-View Synthesis in 360°](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00296): Learns a parametric full-head model for 360-degree free-view synthesis, covering face, hair, and back-of-head geometry beyond frontal portraits.
- [EmoTalk3D: High-Fidelity Free-View Synthesis of Emotional 3D Talking Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00297): Trains on calibrated multi-view EmoTalk3D data and maps speech to geometry then to 4D Gaussian appearance, with canonical and dynamic Gaussians for emotional free-view talking heads.
- [SF3D: Stable Fast 3D Mesh Reconstruction with UV-unwrapping and Illumination Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00653): Produces fast single-image meshes with UV unwrapping and illumination disentanglement, making reconstructed assets easier to texture and relight.
- [MotionFix: Text-Driven 3D Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00712): Introduces MotionFix triplets of source motion, target motion, and edit text, then trains TMED conditional diffusion to edit 3D human motion instead of regenerating from text alone.
- [UniTalker: Scaling up Audio-Driven 3D Facial Animation through A Unified Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00762): Unifies audio-driven 3D facial animation across identities and expression styles, scaling talking-head motion generation with one model.
- [A General Framework to Boost 3D GS Initialization for Text-to-3D Generation by Lexical Richness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.01269): Enriches text prompts lexically before initializing 3D Gaussians, improving text-to-3D optimization starts with more detailed semantic cues.
- [GLDiTalker: Speech-Driven 3D Facial Animation with Graph Latent Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.01826): Models speech-driven 3D facial motion with graph latent diffusion, capturing structured dependencies among facial vertices or regions.
- [REVISION: Rendering Tools Enable Spatial Fidelity in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02231): Uses rendering tools to provide spatially faithful visual evidence for VLMs, improving answers that depend on geometry, viewpoint, and object placement.
- [MeshAnything V2: Artist-Created Mesh Generation with Adjacent Mesh Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02555): Uses Adjacent Mesh Tokenization so autoregressive mesh generation represents many faces with fewer vertex tokens, enabling more complex artist-created meshes at target shapes.
- [An Object is Worth 64×64 Pixels: Generating 3D Object via Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03178): Generates 3D objects by using compact 64-by-64 image diffusion signals as a bridge from 2D appearance priors to 3D geometry.
- [Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting for Static and Dynamic Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03822): Compresses static and dynamic 3DGS by learning masks that prune Gaussian points and quantizing view-dependent color and covariance attributes to cut memory and storage.
- [Sketch2Scene: Automatic Generation of Interactive 3D Game Scenes from User's Casual Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04567): Generates playable 3D game scenes from casual sketches by using a 2D diffusion prior in isometric projection, segmenting the concept image, and assembling interactive assets.
- [Large Models for Aerial Edges: An Edge-Cloud Model Evolution and Communication Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04927): Off-axis for this 3D model list: it discusses large-model evolution and edge-cloud communication for aerial networks rather than a concrete spatial reconstruction model.
- [DreamCouple: Exploring High Quality Text-to-3D Generation Via Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05008): Applies rectified-flow generation to text-to-3D, aiming to improve geometry and texture quality over score-distillation-only pipelines.
- [Mipmap-GS: Let Gaussians Deform with Scale-Specific Mipmap for Anti-Aliasing Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06286): Injects scale information into 3D Gaussians with mipmap-inspired pseudo targets and scale-consistency guidance so splats self-adjust color, shape, size, and position across zoom levels.
- [Rethinking Open-Vocabulary Segmentation of Radiance Fields in 3D Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07416): Directly supervises 3D points to learn language embedding fields for radiance-field volume segmentation, transfers them to 3DGS, and adds a 3D querying and evaluation protocol.
- [LLMI3D: Empowering LLM with 3D Perception from a Single 2D Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07422): Augments LLM reasoning with inferred 3D perception from one image, bridging monocular spatial reconstruction and language-based scene understanding.
- [FlashGS: Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-scale and High-resolution Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07967): Optimizes differentiable 3DGS rasterization with CUDA-level redundancy removal, pipelining, scheduling, and memory-access changes, reaching about four-times faster rendering on large scenes.
- [CT4D: Consistent Text-to-4D Generation with Animatable Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08342): Generates text-conditioned 4D content on animatable meshes with a generate-refine-animate loop, regional driving functions, and rigidity regularization for temporally consistent motion.
- [SG-GS: Photo-realistic Animatable Human Avatars with Semantically-Guided Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09665): Guides human Gaussian splats with semantic body regions so avatars remain photorealistic while supporting animation and pose changes.
- [On the Integration of Spatial-Temporal Knowledge: A Lightweight Approach to Atmospheric Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09695): Off-axis for 3D model coverage: it integrates spatial-temporal knowledge for atmospheric forecasting rather than geometry, scene, or point-cloud modeling.
- [3D-Aware Instance Segmentation and Tracking in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09860): Combines scene geometry, 3D object-centroid tracking, and instance segmentation to improve egocentric video tracking under fast camera motion and occlusion on EPIC Fields.
- [LoopSplat: Loop Closure by Registering 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10154): Builds LoopSplat with RGB-D frame-to-model tracking, 3DGS submaps, and online loop-closure constraints to restore global consistency in Gaussian SLAM maps.
- [SpaRP: Fast 3D Object Reconstruction and Pose Estimation from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10195): Jointly estimates object pose and reconstructs 3D shape from sparse views, targeting fast feed-forward recovery when camera coverage is limited.
- [MeshFormer: High-Quality Mesh Generation with 3D-Guided Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10198): Uses a 3D-guided reconstruction model to generate higher-quality meshes, emphasizing explicit mesh structure rather than only implicit fields.
- [Large Point-to-Gaussian Model for Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10935): Converts image-conditioned point features into Gaussian primitives with a large feed-forward model for image-to-3D asset generation.
- [GS-CPR: Efficient Camera Pose Refinement via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11085): Refines camera poses at test time by rendering synthetic RGB and depth from 3DGS, matching with MASt3R, and adding exposure-adaptive 3DGS for outdoor robustness.
- [HumanCoser: Layered 3D Human Generation via Semantic-Aware Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11357): Generates layered 3D humans with semantic-aware diffusion, separating body and clothing structure for controllable human assets.
- [Positional Prompt Tuning for Efficient 3D Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11567): Freezes most point-transformer weights and tunes added patch tokens plus trainable positional encodings, using positional prompts as parameter-efficient 3D representation adaptation.
- [Robust 3D Gaussian Splatting for Novel View Synthesis in Presence of Distractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11697): Uses self-supervised image residuals and pretrained segmentation masks to identify distractors during 3DGS optimization and exclude them from the reconstruction loss.
- [DreamCinema: Cinematic Transfer with Free Camera and 3D Character](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12601): Decomposes cinematic video creation into 3D character, motion, free camera, and environment controls, using 3D-space composition to keep generated film shots consistent.
- [GSFusion: Online RGB-D Mapping Where Gaussian Splatting Meets TSDF Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12677): Fuses TSDF-style volumetric mapping with 3DGS, initializing compact splats through image quadtrees so online RGB-D mapping keeps geometry and photorealistic feedback.
- [FLoD: Integrating Flexible Level of Detail into 3D Gaussian Splatting for Customizable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12894): Adds flexible level-of-detail control to 3DGS so rendering can trade fidelity for speed according to scene scale or user needs.
- [Learning 2D Invariant Affordance Knowledge for 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13024): Extracts invariant affordance knowledge from multiple human-object interaction images and grounds common interaction patterns onto 3D object regions with MIFAG.
- [Atlas Gaussians Diffusion for 3D Generation with Infinite Number of Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13055): Represents shapes as local atlas patches that decode arbitrarily dense 3D Gaussians, then trains latent diffusion for feed-forward native 3D generation with fine detail.
- [An In-Depth Investigation of Data Collection in LLM App Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13247): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it studies data collection practices in LLM app ecosystems rather than a spatial model or 3D method.
- [LayerPano3D: Layered 3D Panorama for Hyper-Immersive Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13252): Decomposes a generated panorama into depth layers and uses diffusion priors to reveal occluded regions, producing explorable text-driven panoramic 3D scenes.
- [Segment Any Mesh: Zero-shot Mesh Part Segmentation via Lifting Segment Anything 2 to 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13679): Lifts Segment Anything 2 cues onto meshes for zero-shot part segmentation, transferring 2D mask priors to 3D surface regions.
- [SceneDreamer360: Text-Driven 3D-Consistent Scene Generation with Panoramic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13711): Fine-tunes a panoramic generator, initializes with point-cloud fusion, and optimizes panoramic 3DGS so text-driven scene generation stays consistent across 360-degree views.
- [TranSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Multi-View Images with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13770): Adds transformer-based sparse-view 3DGS reconstruction with monocular-depth priors to improve matching and depth in non-overlapping views.
- [Build-A-Scene: Interactive 3D Layout Control for Diffusion-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14819): Lets users specify 3D scene layout controls for diffusion image generation, grounding object placement and camera geometry before image synthesis.
- [Can Transformers Do Enumerative Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14915): Tests transformer models on enumerative-geometry problems, probing symbolic geometric reasoning rather than visual 3D reconstruction.
- [Drone-assisted Road Gaussian Splatting with Cross-view Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15242): Combines drone and road-view imagery with cross-view uncertainty modeling to improve 3DGS reconstruction of road scenes.
- [Generic Objects as Pose Probes for Few-Shot View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16690): Uses everyday segmented objects as pose probes, jointly optimizing object and scene NeRF branches to estimate poses for few-shot view synthesis from 3 to 6 unposed images.
- [Spatially-Aware Diffusion Models with Cross-Attention for Global Field Reconstruction with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00230): Reconstructs global spatial fields from sparse observations using diffusion with cross-attention that propagates spatial context across locations.
- [3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-scale Surface Reconstruction from Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00381): Chunks large aerial MVS image sets, adds ray-Gaussian depth and normals, and enforces multi-view geometric consistency so 3DGS supports large-scale surface reconstruction.
- [IGEV++: Iterative Multi-Range Geometry Encoding Volumes for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00638): Constructs multi-range geometry encoding volumes and iteratively refines disparity to handle ambiguous stereo regions and large disparities.
- [GraspSplats: Efficient Manipulation with 3D Feature Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02084): Embeds manipulation-relevant features into 3D splats so policies can reason about grasp affordances in an explicit scene representation.
- [Volumetric Surfaces: Representing Fuzzy Geometries with Layered Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02482): Represents uncertain or fuzzy geometry with layered meshes, capturing volumetric thickness where single crisp surfaces are inadequate.
- [LM-Gaussian: Boost Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting with Large Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03456): Bootstraps sparse-view 3DGS with stereo-prior pose and point-cloud initialization, plus large-model priors that regularize under-constrained details from few images.
- [Geometry Image Diffusion: Fast and Data-Efficient Text-to-3D with Image-Based Surface Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03718): Encodes 3D shapes as 2D geometry images and uses collaborative control with text-to-image diffusion priors, enabling fast data-efficient text-to-3D generation.
- [Lexicon3D: Probing Visual Foundation Models for Complex 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03757): Probes visual foundation models on complex 3D scene understanding, exposing how well 2D-pretrained representations transfer to spatial semantics.
- [GST: Precise 3D Human Body from a Single Image with Gaussian Splatting Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04196): Uses a transformer to predict human-body Gaussian splats from one image, targeting precise full-body geometry and appearance reconstruction.
- [Diagram Formalization Enhanced Multi-Modal Geometry Problem Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04214): Combines visual features, geometric formal language, and natural language, training on SynthGeo228K diagram captions to improve MLLM geometry-problem solving.
- [Fisheye-GS: Lightweight and Extensible Gaussian Splatting Module for Fisheye Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04751): Re-derives 3DGS projection and gradients for fisheye cameras, making a lightweight module for wide-field novel-view rendering without image undistortion.
- [GS-PT: Exploiting 3D Gaussian Splatting for Comprehensive Point Cloud Understanding via Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04963): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting as a self-supervised pretraining signal for point-cloud understanding, aligning point features with rendered spatial structure.
- [DreamMapping: High-Fidelity Text-to-3D Generation via Variational Distribution Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05099): Reformulates SDS as rendered-image distribution modeling and uses Variational Distribution Mapping with timestep-dependent coefficient annealing for higher-fidelity text-to-3D.
- [GASP: Gaussian Splatting for Physic-Based Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05819): Connects Gaussian splatting representations to physics-based simulation, enabling scene geometry to participate in physical dynamics.
- [G3PT: Unleash the power of Autoregressive Modeling in 3D Generation via Cross-scale Querying Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06322): Tokenizes point-based 3D data at multiple levels of detail and uses a cross-scale querying transformer, making autoregressive 3D generation coarse-to-fine rather than order-dependent.
- [Sources of Uncertainty in 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06407): Analyzes where uncertainty enters 3D reconstruction pipelines, separating sensor, correspondence, geometry, and rendering sources that affect model reliability.
- [GeoCalib: Learning Single-image Calibration with Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06704): Learns single-image camera calibration with geometric optimization.
- [gsplat: An Open-Source Library for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06765): Provides an open-source Gaussian splatting library with optimized training and rasterization components for reproducible 3DGS research.
- [SwinGS: Sliding Window Gaussian Splatting for Volumetric Video Streaming with Arbitrary Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07759): Streams arbitrary-length volumetric videos by maintaining a sliding window of Gaussian splats instead of optimizing the whole dynamic sequence at once.
- [LT3SD: Latent Trees for 3D Scene Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08215): Structures 3D scene diffusion with latent trees, modeling hierarchical scene composition rather than treating the scene as a flat latent vector.
- [FlashSplat: 2D to 3D Gaussian Splatting Segmentation Solved Optimally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08270): Treats rendered 2D mask supervision as a linear function of Gaussian labels and solves 3DGS segmentation by closed-form linear programming instead of iterative descent.
- [DreamHOI: Subject-Driven Generation of 3D Human-Object Interactions with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08278): Uses diffusion priors to generate subject-driven 3D human-object interactions, preserving the target subject while synthesizing plausible contact and motion.
- [Robust Dual Gaussian Splatting for Immersive Human-centric Volumetric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08353): Separates volumetric-video motion and appearance into joint and skin Gaussians, using coarse-to-fine training for compressed real-time human performance playback.
- [PrimeDepth: Efficient Monocular Depth Estimation with a Stable Diffusion Preimage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09144): Extracts a frozen Stable Diffusion preimage representation with a single denoising pass, then predicts zero-shot monocular depth without iterative diffusion at test time.
- [SAM4MLLM: Enhance Multi-Modal Large Language Model for Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10542): Links MLLMs to SAM through language-derived prompt points, letting the MLLM perform referring-expression segmentation with pixel-level masks and minimal architecture changes.
- [GS-Net: Generalizable Plug-and-Play 3D Gaussian Splatting Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11307): Provides a plug-and-play module that predicts or refines Gaussian splats for generalizable reconstruction across scenes.
- [Renderworld: World Model with Self-Supervised 3D Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11356): Uses Gaussian-based self-supervised Img2Occ labels, AM-VAE occupancy encoding, and an autoregressive world model for vision-only 4D occupancy forecasting and planning.
- [Phidias: A Generative Model for Creating 3D Content from Text, Image, and 3D Conditions with Reference-Augmented Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11406): Guides diffusion-based 3D generation with retrieved or user-provided 3D references through meta-ControlNet, dynamic reference routing, and self-reference curriculum training.
- [Generation of Complex 3D Human Motion by Temporal and Spatial Composition of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11920): Composes diffusion models along temporal and spatial axes to synthesize complex 3D human motions from simpler motion components.
- [WiLoR: End-to-end 3D Hand Localization and Reconstruction in-the-wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12259): Pairs a real-time fully convolutional hand localizer with a transformer 3D reconstruction model trained on over two million in-the-wild hand images for monocular tracking.
- [Spectral-GS: Taming 3D Gaussian Splatting with Spectral Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12771): Regularizes Gaussian splatting with spectral entropy to suppress unstable high-frequency artifacts during reconstruction and rendering.
- [3DGS-LM: Faster Gaussian-Splatting Optimization with Levenberg-Marquardt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12892): Replaces 3DGS ADAM optimization with tailored Levenberg-Marquardt updates using cached Jacobian-vector products in CUDA kernels, reducing reconstruction time at matched quality.
- [LI-GS: Gaussian Splatting With LiDAR Incorporated for Accurate Large-Scale Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12899): Incorporates LiDAR geometry into Gaussian splatting so large-scale scenes retain accurate depth and structure beyond image-only optimization.
- [3DTopia-XL: Scaling High-quality 3D Asset Generation via Primitive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12957): Introduces PrimX, a compact primitive representation for shape, albedo, and material fields, then applies latent primitive diffusion to generate PBR-ready 3D assets.
- [Oryx MLLM: On-Demand Spatial-Temporal Understanding at Arbitrary Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12961): Builds an MLLM for arbitrary-resolution spatial-temporal understanding, using on-demand visual token processing for fine-grained video and image reasoning.
- [3D-GSW: 3D Gaussian Splatting for Robust Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13222): Embeds robust watermarks into 3D Gaussian representations so ownership signals survive rendering, viewpoint changes, and common scene edits.
- [Elite-EvGS: Learning Event-based 3D Gaussian Splatting by Distilling Event-to-Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13392): Distills event-to-video priors into event-based 3DGS with warm-up initialization and coarse-to-fine optimization, reconstructing scenes from sparse event streams.
- [SplatLoc: 3D Gaussian Splatting-based Visual Localization for Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14067): Localizes AR cameras against Gaussian-splat maps, using rendered scene evidence to estimate pose for augmented-reality alignment.
- [Dynamic 2D Gaussians: Geometrically Accurate Radiance Fields for Dynamic Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14072): Represents dynamic objects with deformable 2D Gaussians and sparse control points, masking rendered depth to remove floaters and extract high-quality dynamic meshes.
- [AR Overlay: Training Image Pose Estimation on Curved Surface in a Synthetic Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14577): Trains curved-surface image pose estimation synthetically from original logo images, using known shape constraints for AR overlay without curvature-specific inputs.
- [SpikeGS: Learning 3D Gaussian Fields from Continuous Spike Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15176): Learns 3D Gaussian fields from continuous spike-camera streams, matching the event-like sensing process for high-speed scene reconstruction.
- [LaPose: Laplacian Mixture Shape Modeling for RGB-Based Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15727): Models category-level object shape as a Laplacian mixture, combining generalized and specialized RGB streams to form 2D-3D correspondences for pose, size, and translation.
- [Unimotion: Unifying 3D Human Motion Synthesis and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15904): Unifies 3D human motion generation and understanding in one model, sharing motion representations across synthesis and recognition tasks.
- [Semantics-Controlled Gaussian Splatting for Outdoor Scene Reconstruction and Rendering in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15959): Conditions outdoor Gaussian-splat reconstruction on semantic controls, improving VR rendering and scene editing for large outdoor environments.
- [Gaussian Déjà-vu: Creating Controllable 3D Gaussian Head-Avatars with Enhanced Generalization and Personalization Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16147): Generates controllable 3D Gaussian head avatars with mechanisms for both cross-person generalization and identity-specific personalization.
- [MIMO: Controllable Character Video Synthesis with Spatial Decomposed Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16160): Decomposes character, motion, and scene space for controllable character video synthesis, keeping generated motion spatially consistent.
- [SDFit: 3D Object Pose and Shape by Fitting a Morphable SDF to a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16178): Fits a morphable signed-distance-function model to a single image in a render-and-compare loop, retrieving an initial shape and jointly refining pose and geometry.
- [Low Latency Point Cloud Rendering with Learned Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16504): Learns splatting-based point-cloud rendering to reduce latency while preserving enough spatial detail for interactive visualization.
- [Single Image, Any Face: Generalisable 3D Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16990): Generates 3D faces from a single image with a generalizable model, aiming to handle diverse identities without subject-specific fitting.
- [Can Vision Language Models Learn from Visual Demonstrations of Ambiguous Spatial Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17080): Introduces Spatial Visual Ambiguity Tasks to test whether VLMs learn visuospatial rules from demonstrations, showing curriculum data improves in-context spatial reasoning.
- [Text2CAD: Generating Sequential CAD Models from Beginner-to-Expert Level Text Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17106): Builds Text2CAD with Mistral and LLaVA-NeXT annotations over DeepCAD and an autoregressive transformer that emits parametric CAD command sequences from text.
- [Disco4D: Disentangled 4D Human Generation and Animation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17280): Disentangles SMPL-X body Gaussians from clothing Gaussians and uses diffusion to hallucinate occluded regions, enabling single-image 4D human generation and animation.
- [SimVG: A Simple Framework for Visual Grounding with Decoupled Multi-modal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17531): Decouples visual-language fusion from grounding heads by reusing multimodal pretrained features and adding object tokens, improving region grounding for complex expressions.
- [Revisit Anything: Visual Place Recognition via Image Segment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18049): Performs visual place recognition by retrieving image segments rather than whole images, improving localization when only parts of a scene match.
- [EdgeRunner: Auto-regressive Auto-encoder for Artistic Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18114): Compresses triangular meshes into efficient one-dimensional token sequences with an autoregressive autoencoder, then uses latent diffusion for point-cloud and image-conditioned mesh generation.
- [RT-GuIDE: Real-Time Gaussian Splatting for Information-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18122): Uses real-time Gaussian splatting as the map representation for information-driven exploration, selecting views that reduce scene uncertainty.
- [LLaVA-3D: A Simple Yet Effective Pathway to Empowering LMMs with 3D Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18125): Extends LLaVA-style multimodal models with 3D inputs and instruction data so language reasoning can reference objects and relations in point-cloud scenes.
- [Space-time 2D Gaussian Splatting for Accurate Surface Reconstruction under Complex Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18852): Extends 2D Gaussian splatting across space and time to reconstruct surfaces in dynamic scenes with complex motion.
- [S2O: Static to Openable Enhancement for Articulated 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18896): Defines static-to-openable 3D object enhancement with openable-part detection, motion prediction, and interior-geometry completion, plus a dataset for articulated-object evaluation.
- [Multimodal Markup Document Models for Graphic Design Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19051): Off-axis for 3D spatial modeling: it models structured graphic-design documents for completion rather than scene geometry or point-cloud understanding.
- [G3R: Gradient Guided Generalizable Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19405): Guides generalizable 3D reconstruction with gradient information, improving feed-forward recovery where sparse observations leave geometry ambiguous.
- [DAOcc: 3D Object Detection Assisted Multi-Sensor Fusion for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19972): Uses 3D detection cues to assist multi-sensor fusion for occupancy prediction, linking object-level boxes with dense scene occupancy.
- [Dual Encoder GAN Inversion for High-Fidelity 3D Head Reconstruction from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20530): Uses dual-encoder GAN inversion to reconstruct high-fidelity 3D heads from single images, separating identity and detail cues for better geometry.
- [Robin3D Improving 3D Large Language Model via Robust Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00255): Improves 3D large language models through robust instruction tuning.
- [GSPR: Multimodal Place Recognition Using 3D Gaussian Splatting for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00299): Represents synchronized multi-view RGB and LiDAR as Multimodal Gaussian Splatting, then extracts graph-convolution and transformer descriptors for autonomous-driving place recognition.
- [CaRtGS: Computational Alignment for Real-Time Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00486): Improves real-time GS-SLAM through computational alignment, adaptive optimization iterations, long-tail optimization handling, and refined densification for fewer primitives and higher-fidelity maps.
- [Flex3D: Feed-Forward 3D Generation With Flexible Reconstruction Model And Input View Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00890): Combines a flexible feed-forward reconstruction model with input-view curation so 3D generation can adapt to varying image evidence.
- [Towards Native Generative Model for 3D Head Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01226): Trains a native generative model for 3D head avatars instead of relying mainly on 2D priors, targeting controllable head geometry and appearance.
- [LaGeM: A Large Geometry Model for 3D Representation Learning and Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01295): Maps unordered 3D vector sets into hierarchical latent levels of detail, then uses cascaded diffusion conditioned across levels for large-geometry representation and generation.
- [EVA-Gaussian: 3D Gaussian-based Real-time Human Novel View Synthesis under Diverse Camera Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01425): Fuses sparse high-resolution multi-view human images with Efficient Cross-View Attention and refined Gaussian attributes for real-time free-view synthesis under diverse camera setups.
- [UW-GS: Distractor-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting for Enhanced Underwater Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01517): Models underwater 3DGS with distance-dependent color, physics-based density control, dynamic-object masks, scattering-aware losses, and pseudo-depth supervision.
- [3DGS-DET: Empower 3D Gaussian Splatting with Boundary Guidance and Box-Focused Sampling for 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01647): Adapts 3DGS to indoor object detection with boundary guidance and box-focused sampling, reducing ambiguous Gaussian blobs and background over-reconstruction.
- [FabricDiffusion: High-Fidelity Texture Transfer for 3D Garments Generation from In-The-Wild Clothing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01801): Transfers textures from in-the-wild clothing photos onto 3D garments with diffusion, preserving fabric appearance across garment geometry.
- [EVER: Exact Volumetric Ellipsoid Rendering for Real-Time View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01804): Replaces billboard alpha compositing with exact volumetric ellipsoid ray tracing, enabling real-time differentiable view synthesis with fewer popping and density artifacts.
- [Depth Pro: Sharp Monocular Metric Depth in Less Than a Second](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02073): Predicts sharp monocular metric depth in less than a second, adding a practical spatial-perception model line.
