- [MVGS: Multi-view-regulated Gaussian Splatting for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02103): Changes 3DGS training from single-view updates to multi-view regulation, reducing per-view overfitting and improving novel-view appearance and geometry.
- [Efficient High-Resolution Visual Representation Learning with State Space Model for Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03174): Uses state-space modeling for high-resolution visual features in human pose estimation, improving long-range spatial interactions without full attention cost.
- [Img2CAD: Conditioned 3-D CAD Model Generation From Single Image With Structured Visual Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03417): Generates structured 3D CAD models from a single image by conditioning on visual geometry rather than treating CAD as an unconstrained shape-generation target.
- [MonST3R: A Simple Approach for Estimating Geometry in the Presence of Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03825): Estimates geometry under motion, adding a spatial model for dynamic scenes.
- [SPARTUN3D: Situated Spatial Understanding of 3D World in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03878): Adds situated 3D spatial-reasoning data and a situated spatial alignment module to 3D LLMs, aligning scene representations with language from observer-specific contexts.
- [Mode-GS: Monocular Depth Guided Anchored 3D Gaussian Splatting for Robust Ground-View Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04646): Anchors Gaussian splats to pixel-aligned monocular-depth estimates and calibrates per-view depth scale for robust ground-robot novel-view rendering.
- [Next Best Sense: Guiding Vision and Touch with FisherRF for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04680): Uses FisherRF over 3D Gaussian splats to choose next camera and touch actions for robots, making active sensing target uncertain scene regions.
- [GS-VTON: Controllable 3D Virtual Try-on with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05259): Uses Gaussian splatting to build controllable 3D virtual try-on, separating garment geometry and appearance so clothing can be rendered consistently across body views.
- [HiSplat: Hierarchical 3D Gaussian Splatting for Generalizable Sparse-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06245): Builds hierarchical Gaussian representations for generalizable sparse-view reconstruction, using coarse-to-fine splats to recover scene structure from limited views.
- [Trans4D: Realistic Geometry-Aware Transition for Compositional Text-to-4D Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07155): Synthesizes compositional 4D scenes with geometry-aware transitions so generated objects change over time without breaking spatial consistency.
- [Spiking GS: Towards High-Accuracy and Low-Cost Surface Reconstruction via Spiking Neuron-based Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07266): Adapts Gaussian splatting with spiking-neuron style updates to lower the cost of surface reconstruction while preserving geometric accuracy.
- [3D Vision-Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07577): Balances visual and language features inside 3D Gaussian splatting so reconstructed scenes support semantic rasterization and 3D vision-language understanding.
- [MotionGS: Exploring Explicit Motion Guidance for Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07707): Adds explicit motion priors to deformable 3DGS, guiding Gaussian deformation for dynamic-scene reconstruction instead of relying only on implicit optimization.
- [MMHead: Towards Fine-grained Multi-modal 3D Facial Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07757): Builds a 49-hour multimodal 3D facial-animation dataset and model so audio, text, and expression cues can control fine-grained facial motion.
- [Fast Feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08017): Compresses 3D Gaussian splatting scenes with a feed-forward model, replacing slow per-scene compression optimization with direct prediction.
- [IncEventGS: Pose-Free Gaussian Splatting from a Single Event Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08107): Reconstructs pose-free 3D Gaussian scenes incrementally from a single event camera, adapting splatting to asynchronous high-dynamic-range event streams.
- [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning in Spectral Domain for Point Cloud Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08114): Adapts point-cloud models with parameter-efficient fine-tuning in the spectral domain.
- [Efficient Perspective‐Correct 3D Gaussian Splatting Using Hybrid Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08129): Introduces hybrid transparency for perspective-correct 3DGS rendering, improving efficiency while preserving correct splat compositing under perspective projection.
- [Enhancing Single Image to 3D Generation using Gaussian Splatting and Hybrid Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09467): Combines Gaussian splatting with hybrid diffusion priors so single-image-to-3D generation can recover geometry and appearance from limited input evidence.
- [ControLRM: Fast and Controllable 3D Generation via Large Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09592): Introduces ControLRM, an end-to-end feed-forward large reconstruction model for fast controllable 3D generation from aligned input conditions.
- [GALA: Geometry-Aware Local Adaptive Grids for Detailed 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10037): Uses geometry-aware local adaptive grids to allocate detail where generated 3D shapes need fine structure instead of relying on a uniform representation.
- [PointNet with KAN versus PointNet with MLP for 3D Classification and Segmentation of Point Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10084): Compares Kolmogorov-Arnold Network layers with MLP layers inside PointNet for point-cloud classification and segmentation.
- [Spatial-Aware Efficient Projector for MLLMs via Multi-Layer Feature Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10319): Aggregates multi-layer visual features in the MLLM projector to reduce spatial mismatch between 2D visual-token sequences and language tokens.
- [Tex4D: Zero-shot 4D Scene Texturing with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10821): Textures 4D scenes zero-shot by using video diffusion priors, keeping appearance coherent across both viewpoints and time.
- [Multiview Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11187): Builds multiview scene graphs from unposed images, representing scenes as linked place and object nodes for topological spatial reasoning.
- [GS3: Efficient Relighting with Triple Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11419): Uses spatial Gaussians, angular Gaussians, and triple splatting to synthesize real-time relit views from multiview point-lit images.
- [CoTracker3: Simpler and Better Point Tracking by Pseudo-Labeling Real Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11831): Improves point tracking with pseudo-labeled real videos, supporting dynamic visual-spatial modeling.
- [Dynamic Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs for Long-Term Language-Guided Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11989): Maintains dynamic open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs over long horizons so language-guided mobile manipulation can reason over changing objects and relations.
- [Long-LRM: Long-Sequence Large Reconstruction Model for Wide-Coverage Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12781): Extends large reconstruction models to long image sequences, producing wide-coverage Gaussian splats rather than single-object or narrow-view reconstructions.
- [UniGS: Modeling Unitary 3D Gaussians for Novel View Synthesis from Sparse-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13195): Models a unitary set of world-space 3D Gaussians and updates it layer by layer for sparse-view novel-view synthesis.
- [GlossyGS: Inverse Rendering of Glossy Objects With 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13349): Combines 3DGS inverse rendering with material priors to recover geometry and materials of glossy objects more faithfully.
- [MEGA: Memory-Efficient 4D Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13613): Reduces memory use in 4D Gaussian splatting for dynamic scenes by sharing or compacting temporal Gaussian representations.
- [Articulate-Anything: Automatic Modeling of Articulated Objects via a Vision-Language Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13882): Automates articulated-object modeling from text, images, or videos by using a vision-language foundation model to infer movable parts and joints.
- [Neural Signed Distance Function Inference through Splatting 3D Gaussians Pulled on Zero-Level Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14189): Merges 3D Gaussian splatting with neural SDF learning by pulling Gaussians toward the zero-level set for surface reconstruction.
- [LUDVIG: Learning-Free Uplifting of 2D Visual Features to Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14462): Lifts DINO, SAM, and CLIP image features into Gaussian-splat scenes with feature aggregation and graph diffusion instead of reconstruction-loss training.
- [Spatial-Mamba: Effective Visual State Space Models via Structure-Aware State Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15091): Adds structure-aware state fusion to visual state-space models so Mamba-style backbones retain spatial layout information.
- [Layout-your-3D: Controllable and Precise 3D Generation with 2D Blueprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15391): Controls 3D generation with a 2D blueprint, using layout constraints to place objects and geometry more precisely than text-only prompting.
- [Joint Top-Down and Bottom-Up Frameworks for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15615): Combines top-down detector proposals and bottom-up point-cloud regression to improve both accuracy and efficiency for 3D visual grounding.
- [Fully Explicit Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15629): Uses fully explicit 4D Gaussians with progressive training and point backtracking to model dynamic motion during novel-view synthesis.
- [LucidFusion: Generating 3D Gaussians with Arbitrary Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15636): Introduces relative coordinate Gaussians that align arbitrary unposed images to a main view and recover consistent geometry without pose estimation.
- [MBPU: A Plug-and-Play State Space Model for Point Cloud Upsamping with Fast Point Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15941): Uses a plug-and-play state-space module for point-cloud upsampling and fast point rendering, improving dense point recovery without redesigning the full pipeline.
- [Zero-Shot Scene Reconstruction from Single Images with Deep Prior Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15971): Assembles depth, pose, scale, and occlusion priors from large models to reconstruct single-image scenes in a zero-shot pipeline.
- [Sparkle: Mastering Basic Spatial Capabilities in Vision Language Models Elicits Generalization to Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16162): Generates targeted synthetic supervision for 2D spatial primitives, showing that mastering basic spatial skills improves VLM spatial reasoning.
- [Joker: Conditional 3D Head Synthesis with Extreme Facial Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16395): Synthesizes 3D heads with identity from one image and expression control from 3DMM or text, using 3D distillation for view consistency.
- [TIPS: Text-Image Pretraining with Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16512): Pretrains image-text representations with spatial-awareness objectives so visual-language models encode locations and relations more explicitly.
- [The Scene Language: Representing Scenes with Programs, Words, and Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16770): Represents scenes jointly as programs, language, and embeddings, giving models a structured interface for objects, relations, and spatial layouts.
- [Vistadream: Sampling Multiview Consistent Images for Single-View Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16892): Samples multiview-consistent images from one input view to support single-view scene reconstruction with stronger geometric consistency.
- [LVSM: A Large View Synthesis Model with Minimal 3D Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17242): Trains a large view-synthesis model with minimal hand-coded 3D inductive bias, learning cross-view consistency directly from data.
- [SpectroMotion: Dynamic 3D Reconstruction of Specular Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17249): Combines 3DGS, physically based rendering, deformation fields, and deformable environment maps to reconstruct dynamic specular scenes.
- [Efficient neural implicit representation for 3D human reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17741): Uses pretrained human-motion priors inside a neural implicit pipeline to reconstruct efficient monocular 3D human avatars.
- [Few-shot NeRF by Adaptive Rendering Loss Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17839): Regularizes NeRF training with adaptive rendering losses so few-shot view synthesis can recover geometry from sparse observations.
- [VR-Splatting: Foveated Radiance Field Rendering via 3D Gaussian Splatting and Neural Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17932): Combines 3DGS and neural points with foveated rendering to meet VR radiance-field latency requirements while preserving detail where users look.
- [An LLM Agent for Automatic Geospatial Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18792): GeoAgent wraps LLM planning around GIS function calls and spatial constraints for automatic geospatial data analysis workflows.
- [Dynamic 3D Gaussian Tracking for Graph-Based Neural Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18912): Tracks dynamic 3D Gaussian primitives and feeds their trajectories into graph-based neural dynamics models for scene-motion prediction.
- [SegLLM: Multi-round Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18923): Uses mask-aware multimodal memory across dialogue rounds so segmentation can reference prior masks, spatial relations, and hierarchical objects.
- [Sort-free Gaussian Splatting via Weighted Sum Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18931): Replaces sorted alpha blending in 3DGS with weighted-sum rendering to remove view-dependent sorting overhead.
- [Large Spatial Model: End-to-end Unposed Images to Semantic 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18956): Processes unposed RGB images directly into semantic radiance fields, avoiding a multi-stage SfM-to-reconstruction pipeline.
- [Where Am I and What Will I See: An Auto-Regressive Model for Spatial Localization and View Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18962): Autoregressively predicts camera localization and future views, linking spatial self-localization with what the agent should see next.
- [3D-Adapter: Geometry-Consistent Multi-View Diffusion for High-Quality 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18974): Adds a plug-in 3D-Adapter to pretrained multiview diffusion, feeding back 3D geometry during denoising for more consistent object generation.
- [PixelGaussian: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Reconstruction from Arbitrary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18979): Dynamically adapts Gaussian count and distribution to scene complexity for feed-forward 3DGS reconstruction from arbitrary input views.
- [DiffGS: Functional Gaussian Splatting Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19657): Trains a latent diffusion generator over Gaussian primitives, enabling functional 3DGS generation with arbitrary numbers of splats.
- [SCube: Instant Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction using VoxSplats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20030): Uses VoxSplats to reconstruct large-scale scene geometry, appearance, and semantics from sparse posed images.
- [Normal-GS: 3D Gaussian Splatting with Normal-Involved Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20593): Injects normal vectors into the 3DGS rendering pipeline so Gaussian optimization better preserves surface geometry.
- [COMPGS: Unleashing 2D Compositionality for Compositional Text-to-3D via Dynamically Optimizing 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20723): Uses dynamically optimized 3D Gaussians to transfer 2D compositional generation strengths into compositional text-to-3D scenes.
- [TV-3DG: Mastering Text-to-3D Customized Generation with Visual Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21299): Conditions customized text-to-3D generation on visual prompts so object identity and appearance can be preserved during 3D asset creation.
- [PF3plat: Pose-Free Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22128): Performs pose-free feed-forward 3DGS for novel-view synthesis from unposed images without per-scene optimization.
- [Epipolar-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting for Generalizable Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22817): Removes epipolar-line dependence in generalizable 3DGS, enabling efficient feed-forward novel-view synthesis from sparse inputs.
- [HelloMeme: Integrating Spatial Knitting Attentions to Embed High-Level and Fidelity-Rich Conditions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22901): Uses spatial knitting attention adapters in diffusion models to inject high-level conditions while preserving base-model generalization.
- [SceneComplete: Open-World 3D Scene Completion in Cluttered Real World Environments for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23643): Targets open-world 3D scene completion in cluttered real-world manipulation environments.
- [ImOV3D: Learning Open-Vocabulary Point Clouds 3D Object Detection from Only 2D Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24001): Learns open-vocabulary 3D object detection from only 2D images by constructing pseudo multimodal image-point-cloud representations.
- [DiffPano: Scalable and Consistent Text to Panorama Generation with Spherical Epipolar-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24203): Uses spherical epipolar-aware diffusion for scalable text-to-panorama generation with multiview consistency.
- [DELTA: Dense Efficient Long-range 3D Tracking for any video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24211): Tracks dense pixels in 3D across long videos, estimating motion for every point rather than sparse correspondences only.
- [NIMBA: Towards Robust and Principled Processing of Point Clouds With SSMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00151): Serializes point clouds into structure-preserving 1D sequences so state-space models can process 3D data efficiently.
- [Aquatic-GS: A Hybrid 3D Representation for Underwater Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00239): Builds a hybrid 3D representation for underwater scenes that separates objects from water-medium effects.
- [GAFusion: Adaptive Fusing LiDAR and Camera with Multiple Guidance for 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00340): Fuses LiDAR and camera features with LiDAR-guided global interaction and adaptive fusion for multimodal 3D object detection.
- [CityGaussianV2: Efficient and Geometrically Accurate Reconstruction for Large-Scale Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00771): Improves CityGaussian with filters and geometric controls that reduce Gaussian explosion while keeping large-scale reconstruction accurate.
- [One for All: Multi-Domain Joint Training for Point Cloud Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01584): Jointly trains point-cloud 3D detectors across domains so a single model can handle domain shifts instead of requiring dataset-specific detectors.
- [FewViewGS: Gaussian Splatting with Few View Matching and Multi-stage Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02229): Combines few-view feature matching with multi-stage 3DGS training to reconstruct scenes when only a small number of images are available.
- [Tencent Hunyuan3D-1.0: A Unified Framework for Text-to-3D and Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02293): Unifies text-to-3D and image-to-3D generation in the Hunyuan3D framework, covering both prompt-only and image-conditioned asset creation.
- [GenXD: Generating Any 3D and 4D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02319): Generates 3D and 4D scenes from flexible conditions, extending asset generation from static objects to time-varying scenes.
- [MVPaint: Synchronized Multi-View Diffusion for Painting Anything 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02336): Uses synchronized multiview diffusion and refinement to paint high-resolution seamless textures onto 3D objects.
- [Modeling Uncertainty in 3D Gaussian Splatting Through Continuous Semantic Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02547): Moves continuous semantic mapping from voxels to probabilistic ellipsoids, combining 3DGS structure with uncertainty-aware robotic maps.
- [GIS Copilot: towards an autonomous GIS agent for spatial analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03205): Integrates LLMs into QGIS-style GIS platforms so spatial-analysis code generation can use native geospatial tools.
- [Fine-Grained Spatial and Verbal Losses for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03405): Adds visual offset and language span losses for 3D visual grounding, improving referred-object localization in point-cloud scenes.
- [Object and Contact Point Tracking in Demonstrations Using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03555): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting to track objects and contact points in demonstrations, recovering interaction geometry useful for imitation data.
- [MVSplat360: Feed-Forward 360 Scene Synthesis from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04924): Introduces MVSplat360, a feed-forward sparse-observation model for 360-degree novel-view synthesis of real-world scenes.
- [CAD-MLLM: Unifying Multimodality-Conditioned CAD Generation With MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04954): Builds CAD-MLLM to generate parametric CAD models from multimodal inputs rather than only images or text.
- [ProEdit: Simple Progression is All You Need for High-Quality 3D Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05006): Uses difficulty-aware subtask scheduling and adaptive 3DGS training to break large scene generation into manageable reconstruction tasks.
- [StdGEN: Semantic-Decomposed 3D Character Generation from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05738): Decomposes single-image character generation by semantic parts so generated 3D characters preserve body structure and local appearance details.
- [GaussianSpa: An "Optimizing-Sparsifying" Simplification Framework for Compact and High-Quality 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06019): Alternates optimization and sparsification to impose strong Gaussian sparsity while preserving 3DGS scene quality.
- [SplatFormer: Point Transformer for Robust 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06390): Introduces SplatFormer, a point transformer designed to operate directly on Gaussian splat primitives.
- [Adaptive and Temporally Consistent Gaussian Surfels for Multi-View Dynamic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06602): Uses per-frame incremental optimization to reconstruct high-quality dynamic surfaces from multiview video.
- [CapeLLM: Support-Free Category-Agnostic Pose Estimation with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06869): Models unseen keypoint distributions and uncertainty during inference to refine category-level pose estimation.
- [Scaling Mesh Generation via Compressive Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07025): Tokenizes meshes with blocked and patchified tokens, enabling generation of meshes with more than eight thousand faces.
- [Edify 3D: Scalable High-Quality 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07135): Presents Edify 3D for high-quality 3D asset generation from image and text conditions.
- [SAMPart3D: Segment Any Part in 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07184): Segments any 3D object into multi-granularity semantic parts with SAMPart3D, avoiding predefined text part labels.
- [GUS-IR: Gaussian Splatting With Unified Shading for Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07478): Adds unified shading to Gaussian-splatting inverse rendering so geometry, material, and illumination can be recovered in one representation.
- [HiCoM: Hierarchical Coherent Motion for Streamable Dynamic Scene with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07541): Adds hierarchical coherent motion to dynamic 3DGS so non-uniform Gaussians learn frame-to-frame motion more efficiently.
- [ALOcc: Adaptive Lifting-Based 3D Semantic Occupancy and Cost Volume-Based Flow Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07725): Uses occlusion-aware adaptive lifting with depth denoising to improve 3D reconstruction under occluded views.
- [No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment via Graph Convolutional Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07728): Evaluates point-cloud quality without references by applying graph convolution over multiview 2D projections.
- [Efficient 3D Perception on Multi-Sweep Point Cloud with Gumbel Spatial Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07742): Adds a learnable Gumbel spatial-pruning layer that dynamically samples point-cloud tokens end to end.
- [Novel View Synthesis with Pixel-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07765): Performs novel-view synthesis directly with pixel-space diffusion, using generative image priors to predict unseen views.
- [Wavelet Latent Diffusion (Wala): Billion-Parameter 3D Generative Model with Compact Wavelet Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08017): Trains a billion-parameter 3D generative model over compact wavelet encodings, scaling 3D generation while keeping the latent representation manageable.
- [GaussianAnything: Interactive Point Cloud Latent Diffusion for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08033): Trains native 3D generation across text and image conditions, outperforming prior 3D methods on multiple datasets.
- [DG-SLAM: Robust Dynamic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Hybrid Pose Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08373): Builds DG-SLAM, a dynamic visual SLAM system grounded in 3D Gaussians for precise tracking and high-fidelity reconstruction.
- [Architect: Generating Vivid and Interactive 3D Scenes with Hierarchical 2D Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09823): Generates interactive 3D scenes through hierarchical 2D inpainting, supporting text, floor plans, or arranged layouts as starting points.
- [LLM Agents Grounded in Self-Reports Enable General-Purpose Simulation of Individuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10109): Off-axis for Spatial Model; it grounds LLM agents in self-reports to simulate individuals rather than contributing a 3D, geometry, or spatial-representation model.
- [DrivingSphere: Building a High-fidelity 4D World for Closed-loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11252): Builds a high-fidelity 4D driving world for closed-loop simulation, modeling dynamic traffic scenes rather than only static reconstruction.
- [GPS-Gaussian+: Generalizable Pixel-wise 3D Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Human-Scene Rendering from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11363): Generalizes pixel-wise 3D Gaussian splatting for real-time human-scene rendering from sparse camera views.
- [RoboGSim: A Real2Sim2Real Robotic Gaussian Splatting Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11839): Off-axis for Spatial Model; it is a robotics real-to-sim-to-real Gaussian-splatting simulator rather than a standalone spatial model.
- [SpatialDreamer: Self-supervised Stereo Video Synthesis from Monocular Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11934): Synthesizes stereo video from monocular input with self-supervision, using spatial consistency to infer the missing binocular view over time.
- [Beyond Gaussians: Fast and High-Fidelity 3D Splatting with Linear Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12440): Replaces Gaussian kernels with linear kernels in 3D splatting to sharpen high-frequency geometry and appearance.
- [Efficient Scene Modeling via Structure-Aware and Region-Prioritized 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12788): Prioritizes scene regions and structure-aware Gaussians to make high-fidelity 3D scene modeling more efficient.
- [PhysSplat: Efficient Physics Simulation for 3D Scenes via MLLM-Guided Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12789): Uses an MLLM to infer object physical properties and guide Gaussian-splat physics simulation for 3D scenes.
- [Generating 3D-Consistent Videos from Unposed Internet Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13549): Trains a scalable 3D-aware video model from unposed internet photos and video consistency without camera or 3D annotations.
- [Find any Part in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13550): Targets open-vocabulary 3D part localization, asking foundation-style models to find arbitrary parts in 3D objects or scenes.
- [FAST-Splat: Fast, Ambiguity-Free Semantics Transfer in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13753): Transfers semantics into Gaussian splatting quickly while avoiding ambiguous 2D-to-3D feature assignment.
- [Stereo Anything: Unifying Stereo Matching with Large-Scale Mixed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14053): Unifies stereo matching with large-scale mixed data for spatial perception.
- [Baking Gaussian Splatting Into Diffusion Denoiser for Fast and Scalable Single-Stage Image-to-3D Generation and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14384): Bakes Gaussian splatting into a diffusion denoiser so image-to-3D generation and reconstruction run as a single-stage scalable process.
- [NexusSplats: Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14514): Optimizes efficient in-the-wild 3DGS reconstruction under complex lighting and occlusion conditions.
- [TEXGen: a Generative Diffusion Model for Mesh Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14740): Generates mesh textures with a diffusion model, targeting coherent high-resolution surface appearance for 3D assets.
- [Dynamics-Aware Gaussian Splatting Streaming Towards Fast On-the-Fly 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14847): Streams 4D reconstruction with a three-stage dynamics-aware Gaussian splatting pipeline for fast on-the-fly updates.
- [Material Anything: Generating Materials for Any 3D Object via Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15138): Generates physically plausible materials for arbitrary 3D objects with diffusion, extending 3D asset creation beyond shape and texture alone.
- [When Spatial meets Temporal in Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15284): Studies how spatial representations interact with temporal modeling in action recognition, focusing on architectures that combine scene layout and motion cues.
- [ConsistentAvatar: Learning to Diffuse Fully Consistent Talking Head Avatar with Temporal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15436): Uses temporal guidance in diffusion to synthesize talking-head avatars whose geometry and appearance remain consistent across frames.
- [ZeroGS: Training 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15779): Trains 3DGS directly from hundreds of unposed, unordered images, removing the usual pose-preprocessing requirement.
- [PG-SLAM: Photorealistic and Geometry-Aware RGB-D SLAM in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15800): Extends RGB-D SLAM with photorealistic, geometry-aware Gaussian splatting for dynamic environments.
- [Generalizable Single-View Object Pose Estimation by Two-Side Generating and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15860): Estimates single-view object pose by generating and matching two-sided hypotheses, improving generalization to unseen objects and viewpoints.
- [Gaussian Scenes: Pose-Free Sparse-View Scene Reconstruction using Depth-Enhanced Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15966): Uses depth-enhanced diffusion priors to inpaint novel-view and depth-map artifacts for pose-free sparse-view scene reconstruction.
- [Boosting 3D object generation through PBR materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16080): Improves generated 3D objects by optimizing physically based rendering materials instead of only geometry or RGB textures.
- [UNOPose: Unseen Object Pose Estimation with an Unposed RGB-D Reference Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16106): Estimates unseen-object pose from an unposed RGB-D reference image.
- [MVGenMaster: Scaling Multi-View Generation from Any Image via 3D Priors Enhanced Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16157): Scales multi-view generation from a single image by injecting 3D priors into diffusion, improving consistency across generated views.
- [Event-boosted Deformable 3D Gaussians for Fast Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16180): Jointly models event thresholds and deformable 3D Gaussians so event streams improve fast dynamic reconstruction.
- [Functionality understanding and segmentation in 3D scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16310): Introduces Fun3DU for understanding and segmenting functional regions in 3D scenes.
- [Quadratic Gaussian Splatting: High Quality Surface Reconstruction with Second-Order Geometric Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16392): Replaces static Gaussian primitives with deformable quadratic surfaces to capture higher-quality surface geometry.
- [SplatFlow: Multi-View Rectified Flow Model for 3D Gaussian Splatting Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16443): Uses rectified flow to generate and edit 3D Gaussian splats directly from multiview conditions.
- [RoboSpatial: Teaching Spatial Understanding to 2D and 3D Vision-Language Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16537): Builds RoboSpatial, a large robotics dataset and training setup for teaching spatial understanding to 2D and 3D VLMs.
- [MICAS: Multi-grained In-Context Adaptive Sampling for 3D Point Cloud Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16773): Uses multi-grained in-context adaptive sampling to process point clouds with task-relevant local and global context.
- [UniPose: A Unified Multimodal Framework for Human Pose Comprehension, Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16781): Unifies human-pose comprehension, generation, and editing across image, text, and SMPL pose modalities.
- [DetailGen3D: Generative 3D Geometry Enhancement via Data-Dependent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16820): Enhances coarse 3D geometry with data-dependent flows and token matching that preserve global structure while adding local detail.
- [Open Vocabulary Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16833): Extends monocular 3D object detection to open-vocabulary categories, using language-aligned visual cues rather than a closed class set.
- [SAR3D: Autoregressive 3D Object Generation and Understanding via Multi-scale 3D VQVAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16856): Uses a multi-scale 3D VQ-VAE and autoregressive modeling for both 3D object generation and understanding.
- [PreF3R: Pose-Free Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Variable-length Image Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16877): Predicts 3D Gaussian splats feed-forward from variable-length unposed image sequences, avoiding camera-pose preprocessing and per-scene optimization.
- [MonoGSDF: Exploring Monocular Geometric Cues for Gaussian Splatting-Guided Implicit Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16898): Couples monocular geometric cues, Gaussian primitives, and an SDF branch for watertight implicit surface reconstruction.
- [Geometry Field Splatting with Gaussian Surfels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17067): Represents surfaces with Gaussian surfels and geometry fields so splatting can recover sharper surface structure during reconstruction.
- [Distilling Spectral Graph for Object-Context Aware Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17150): Distills spectral graph context so open-vocabulary segmentation can account for object-level context in complex scenes.
- [SelfSplat: Pose-Free and 3D Prior-Free Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17190): Performs generalizable 3DGS from unposed multiview images without camera poses or pretrained 3D priors.
- [Buffer Anytime: Zero-Shot Video Depth and Normal from Image Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17249): Estimates video depth and normals zero-shot by combining single-image priors with temporal-consistency constraints.
- [Distractor-free Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17605): Predicts distractor masks and feed-forward Gaussian splats to make generalizable 3DGS robust to cross-scene distractors.
- [DROID-Splat Combining End-to-End SLAM with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17660): Combines an end-to-end SLAM tracker with a 3DGS renderer to improve monocular tracking and scene synthesis.
- [3D-Mem: 3D Scene Memory for Embodied Exploration and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17735): Builds 3D-Mem, a compact scene-memory representation for embodied agents that preserves detailed spatial relations over time.
- [Symmetry Strikes Back: From Single-Image Symmetry Detection to 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17763): Detects 3D reflection symmetry from a single image with Reflect3D and uses it as a cue for single-image 3D generation.
- [HI-SLAM2: Geometry-Aware Gaussian SLAM for Fast Monocular Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17982): Uses monocular priors and grid-based scale alignment inside Gaussian SLAM for fast RGB-only scene reconstruction.
- [GLS: Geometry-aware 3D Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18066): Unifies 3DGS surface reconstruction and open-vocabulary segmentation with normal priors and geometry-aware language features.
- [Make-It-Animatable: An Efficient Framework for Authoring Animation-Ready 3D Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18197): Makes arbitrary 3D humanoid models animation-ready in under a second through data-driven rigging and skinning.
- [Textured Gaussians for Enhanced 3D Scene Appearance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18625): Adds texture-aware attributes to Gaussian splats so reconstructed scenes preserve richer view-dependent appearance.
- [RoMo: Robust Motion Segmentation Improves Structure from Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18650): Segments static and moving regions in video so structure-from-motion can estimate camera poses more robustly.
- [TAPTRv3: Spatial and Temporal Context Foster Robust Tracking of Any Point in Long Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18671): Improves long-video point tracking by querying features with both spatial and temporal context.
- [PCDreamer: Point Cloud Completion Through Multi-view Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19036): Completes partial point clouds by using multiview diffusion priors as guidance for plausible missing geometry.
- [HOT3D: Hand and Object Tracking in 3D from Egocentric Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19167): Releases HOT3D, an egocentric multiview RGB, eye-gaze, and 3D annotation dataset for hand-object tracking.
- [Video Depth without Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19189): Turns image depth models into temporally consistent video depth estimators without training a dedicated video model.
- [OMNI-DC: Highly Robust Depth Completion with Multiresolution Depth Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19278): Introduces multiresolution depth integration and Laplacian losses for robust zero-shot depth completion from sparse depth.
- [Multiview Equivariance Improves 3D Correspondence Understanding with Minimal Feature Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19458): Fine-tunes vision features with multiview equivariance and 3D correspondences to improve 3D correspondence understanding.
- [Diorama: Unleashing Zero-Shot Single-View 3D Indoor Scene Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19492): Models open-world indoor 3D scenes from a single RGB image using CAD objects without end-to-end training or manual annotations.
- [DisCoRD: Discrete Tokens to Continuous Motion via Rectified Flow Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19527): Decodes discrete motion tokens with rectified flow so generated human motion remains continuous and conditioning-aware.
- [Bootstraping Clustering of Gaussians for View-consistent 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19551): Bootstraps clusters of Gaussian primitives to obtain view-consistent 3D scene grouping and semantic understanding.
- [Tortho-Gaussian: Splatting True Digital Orthophoto Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19594): Generates true digital orthophoto maps by orthogonally splatting optimized anisotropic Gaussians.
- [TexGaussian: Generating High-quality PBR Material via Octree-based 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19654): Uses octree-based 3D Gaussian splatting to generate geometry-consistent physically based rendering materials.
- [PerLA: Perceptive 3D language assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19774): Builds PerLA, a 3D language assistant that keeps both local detail and global context in point-cloud representations.
- [GuardSplat: Efficient and Robust Watermarking for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19895): Embeds robust watermarks into 3D Gaussian splatting scenes while preserving rendering quality and resisting common scene transformations.
- [AerialGo: Walking-through City View Generation from Aerial Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00157): Generates ground-level walking-through city views from aerial images with multiview diffusion, avoiding direct street-level capture.
- [Hard-Label Black-Box Attacks on 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00404): Attacks 3D point-cloud classifiers in a hard-label black-box setting where only predicted labels are observable.
- [Instant3dit: Multiview Inpainting for Fast Editing of 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00518): Casts 3D object editing as multiview inpainting, enabling mesh, NeRF, or Gaussian-splat edits in about three seconds.
- [Speedy-Splat: Fast 3D Gaussian Splatting with Sparse Pixels and Sparse Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00578): Speeds up 3DGS by training and rendering with sparse pixels and sparse primitives rather than dense image and splat updates.
- [FiffDepth: Feed-Forward Transformation of Diffusion-Based Generators for Detailed Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00671): Transforms diffusion-based generators into an efficient feed-forward monocular-depth estimator with detailed real-world predictions.
- [DynSUP: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting from An Unposed Image Pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00851): Reconstructs dynamic Gaussian splats from an unposed image pair, estimating motion and geometry without camera-pose preprocessing.
- [Ref-GS: Directional Factorization for 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00905): Factorizes view-dependent lighting in 2D Gaussian splatting with directional encoding and spherical Mip-grids.
- [LSceneLLM: Enhancing Large 3D Scene Understanding Using Adaptive Visual Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01292): Uses adaptive visual preferences and a scene magnifier so 3D scene LLMs focus on task-relevant areas.
- [Articulate3D: Holistic Understanding of 3D Scenes as Universal Scene Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01398): Converts 3D scenes into universal scene descriptions that capture objects, layouts, attributes, and relations for holistic scene understanding.
- [Structured 3D Latents for Scalable and Versatile 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01506): Introduces structured 3D latent representations for scalable and versatile 3D generation.
- [6DOPE-GS: Online 6D Object Pose Estimation using Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01543): Uses Gaussian splatting for online 6D object pose estimation and tracking from a single RGB-D camera.
- [GFreeDet: Exploiting Gaussian Splatting and Foundation Models for Model-free Unseen Object Detection in the BOP Challenge 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01552): Exploits Gaussian splatting and foundation-model features for model-free unseen object detection, targeting BOP-style 6D pose and detection settings without object-specific templates.
- [SfM-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting via Hierarchical Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01553): Trains and merges regional Gaussian representations hierarchically, eliminating SfM preprocessing for video-based 3DGS.
- [3DSceneEditor: Controllable 3D Scene Editing with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01583): Edits Gaussian-splatting scenes with controllable operations so users can modify 3D content while preserving multi-view consistency.
- [Driving View Synthesis on Free-Form Trajectories with Generative Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01717): Synthesizes driving views along free-form camera trajectories by combining scene geometry with generative priors.
- [Horizon-Gs: Unified 3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale Aerial-To-Ground Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01745): Unifies aerial and ground-view Gaussian splatting for large scenes, bridging scale and viewpoint gaps in outdoor reconstruction.
- [Occam's LGS: A Simple Approach for Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01807): Embeds language features into Gaussian splats with a simplified pipeline for open-vocabulary 3D scene querying.
- [HDGS: Textured 2D Gaussian Splatting for Enhanced Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01823): Adds textured 2D Gaussian primitives for scene rendering, improving surface appearance without relying only on point-like 3D splats.
- [SparseLGS: Sparse View Language Embedded Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02245): Learns language-embedded Gaussian splats from sparse views so open-vocabulary scene understanding works with limited image coverage.
- [HumanRig: Learning Automatic Rigging for Humanoid Character in a Large Scale Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02317): Trains automatic rigging for humanoid characters on a large dataset, producing skeletons and skinning weights for animation-ready 3D assets.
- [RG-SAN: Rule-Guided Spatial Awareness Network for End-to-End 3D Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02402): Uses rule-guided spatial awareness to segment referred 3D objects end to end from language expressions.
- [AdvDreamer Unveils: Are Vision-Language Models Truly Ready for Real-World 3D Variations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03002): Uses 3D variation generation to stress-test vision-language models under realistic pose, shape, and viewpoint changes.
- [Align3R: Aligned Monocular Depth Estimation for Dynamic Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03079): Aligns monocular depth predictions across frames so dynamic videos receive temporally coherent depth estimates.
- [MultiGO: Towards Multi-level Geometry Learning for Monocular 3D Textured Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03103): Learns multi-level geometry from monocular images to reconstruct textured 3D humans with both body shape and surface detail.
- [Splats in Splats: Robust and Effective 3D Steganography Towards Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03121): Hides information inside Gaussian-splatting scenes with a steganographic scheme designed to remain robust after rendering and scene edits.
- [Volumetrically Consistent 3D Gaussian Rasterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03378): Revises Gaussian rasterization to enforce volumetric consistency, reducing rendering artifacts caused by inconsistent splat accumulation.
- [Skel3D: Skeleton Guided Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03407): Uses skeletal structure as guidance for novel-view synthesis, improving pose-aware rendering of articulated subjects.
- [NVComposer: Boosting Generative Novel View Synthesis with Multiple Sparse and Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03517): Composes sparse unposed images for generative novel-view synthesis, avoiding reliance on calibrated camera poses.
- [Feed-Forward Bullet-Time Reconstruction of Dynamic Scenes from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03526): Reconstructs dynamic scenes from monocular video in a feed-forward pass, enabling bullet-time views without per-scene optimization.
- [MIDI: Multi-Instance Diffusion for Single Image to 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03558): Uses multi-instance diffusion to expand one image into a 3D scene with multiple objects rather than a single isolated asset.
- [HybridGS: Decoupling Transients and Statics with 2D and 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03844): Separates transient image-specific objects into 2D Gaussians while keeping static scene content in 3D Gaussians, with multi-view regulated supervision and staged training.
- [3D Part Segmentation via Geometric Aggregation of 2D Visual Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04247): Projects multi-view VLM features back onto point clouds, then uses geometry-aware feature aggregation and clustering for zero-shot 3D part segmentation.
- [EmbodiedOcc: Embodied 3D Occupancy Prediction for Vision-Based Online Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04380): Maintains a global memory of semantic 3D Gaussians and refines local regions through deformable cross-attention as an embodied agent explores the scene.
- [SeeGround: See and Ground for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04383): Converts 3D scenes into query-aligned renderings and spatial text descriptions, using perspective adaptation and fusion alignment for zero-shot 3D grounding.
- [GaussianFormer-2: Probabilistic Gaussian Superposition for Efficient 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04384): Treats semantic Gaussians as local occupancy probability distributions and combines them with probabilistic superposition plus distribution-based initialization.
- [Florence-VL: Enhancing Vision-Language Models with Generative Vision Encoder and Depth-Breadth Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04424): Integrates Florence-2 generative vision features into MLLMs through depth-breadth fusion over layers and prompts, then trains with a staged multimodal recipe.
- [Monocular Dynamic Gaussian Splatting: Fast, Brittle, and Scene Complexity Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04457): Analyzes monocular dynamic 3DGS behavior, identifying how scene complexity and motion make fast optimization brittle for dynamic reconstruction.
- [Cubify Anything: Scaling Indoor 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04458): Scales indoor 3D object detection around cuboid-style object representations for broad category coverage in cluttered indoor scenes.
- [MegaSaM: Accurate, Fast, and Robust Structure and Motion from Casual Dynamic Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04463): Estimates structure and motion from casual dynamic videos with a fast pipeline built for robust camera and scene reconstruction under non-rigid motion.
- [DualPM: Dual Posed-Canonical Point Maps for 3D Shape and Pose Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04464): Reconstructs shape and pose with paired posed and canonical point maps, linking observed geometry to a normalized object frame.
- [Turbo3D: Ultra-fast Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04470): Accelerates text-to-3D generation with a fast 3D generation pipeline aimed at producing assets without long per-prompt optimization.
- [PaintScene4D: Consistent 4D Scene Generation from Text Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04471): Generates temporally consistent 4D scenes from text by coordinating scene content, geometry, and motion across time.
- [Stereo Anywhere: Robust Zero-Shot Deep Stereo Matching Even Where Either Stereo or Mono Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04472): Improves robust zero-shot stereo matching in cases where either stereo or monocular cues fail.
- [Pushing Rendering Boundaries: Hard Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04826): Replaces soft alpha blending with harder splat visibility behavior to sharpen Gaussian-splat rendering boundaries.
- [PanoDreamer: Optimization-Based Single Image to 360 3D Scene With Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04827): Expands a single image into a 360-degree 3D scene by optimizing panoramic geometry and appearance under diffusion priors.
- [Momentum-Gs: Momentum Gaussian Self-Distillation for High-Quality Large Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04887): Uses momentum self-distillation to stabilize Gaussian optimization and improve large-scene reconstruction quality.
- [BimArt: A Unified Approach for the Synthesis of 3D Bimanual Interaction with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05066): Synthesizes coordinated two-hand motions with articulated objects in 3D, unifying bimanual interaction and object articulation modeling.
- [Street Gaussians Without 3D Object Tracker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05548): Reconstructs street scenes with Gaussian splatting while avoiding a separate 3D object tracker for dynamic traffic participants.
- [Temporally Compressed 3D Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05700): Compresses dynamic 3DGS over time so changing scenes can be represented with less redundant per-frame Gaussian state.
- [SizeGS: Size-aware Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting via Mixed Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05808): Uses mixed-integer programming with size-aware decisions to prune or compress 3D Gaussians while preserving rendering quality.
- [GBR: Generative Bundle Refinement for High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting With Enhanced Mesh Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05908): Refines Gaussian splatting with a generative bundle procedure that also improves mesh reconstruction fidelity.
- [Generative Densification: Learning to Densify Gaussians for High-Fidelity Generalizable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06234): Learns when and where to add Gaussians, replacing hand-coded densification rules for generalizable high-fidelity 3D reconstruction.
- [Splatter-360: Generalizable 360° Gaussian Splatting for Wide-baseline Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06250): Builds generalizable Gaussian splats from wide-baseline panoramic images for full 360-degree scene rendering.
- [Advancing Extended Reality with 3D Gaussian Splatting: Innovations and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06257): Surveys how 3D Gaussian splatting changes extended-reality capture, rendering, compression, and interaction pipelines.
- [Omni-Scene: Omni-Gaussian Representation for Ego-Centric Sparse-View Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06273): Uses an omni-directional Gaussian representation for ego-centric sparse-view scene reconstruction.
- [PrEditor3D: Fast and Precise 3D Shape Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06592): Edits 3D shapes with a fast localized mechanism designed to preserve unaffected geometry while applying precise changes.
- [Gen-3Diffusion: Realistic Image-to-3D Generation via 2D & 3D Diffusion Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06698): Couples 2D and 3D diffusion priors so image-to-3D generation can improve both view realism and geometric consistency.
- [You See it, You Got it: Learning 3D Creation on Pose-Free Videos at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06699): Learns 3D asset creation from large-scale pose-free videos, avoiding the need for calibrated multi-view supervision.
- [MAtCha Gaussians: Atlas of Charts for High-Quality Geometry and Photorealism From Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06767): Represents sparse-view scenes with an atlas of Gaussian charts to improve geometry quality and photorealistic rendering.
- [Around the World in 80 Timesteps: A Generative Approach to Global Visual Geolocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06781): Frames global visual geolocation as an iterative generative process over world locations rather than direct coordinate regression.
- [MV-DUSt3R+: Single-Stage Scene Reconstruction from Sparse Views In 2 Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06974): Extends DUSt3R-style point-map prediction to reconstruct sparse-view scenes in a single stage within seconds.
- [Faster and Better 3D Splatting via Group Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07608): Trains groups of Gaussians jointly to speed up 3D splatting optimization while improving reconstruction quality.
- [Proc-Gs: Procedural Building Generation for City Assembly with 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07660): Combines procedural building generation with 3D Gaussians to assemble city-scale scenes from reusable structural components.
- [SimVS: Simulating World Inconsistencies for Robust View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07696): Trains view synthesis to handle real-world inconsistencies by simulating mismatched geometry, appearance, and observations.
- [SLGaussian: Fast Language Gaussian Splatting in Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08331): Embeds language features into sparse-view Gaussian splats for fast open-vocabulary scene representation.
- [Reloc3r: Large-Scale Training of Relative Camera Pose Regression for Generalizable, Fast, and Accurate Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08376): Trains relative camera-pose regression at scale for fast generalizable visual localization.
- [3D Mesh Editing Using Masked LRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08641): Uses masked large reconstruction models to edit selected regions of a 3D mesh while preserving the rest of the shape.
- [LIVE-GS: LLM Powers Interactive VR Experience with Physics-Aware Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09176): Combines LLM-driven interaction with physics-aware Gaussian splats to support editable and interactive VR scenes.
- [SLAM3R: Real-Time Dense Scene Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09401): Performs real-time dense scene reconstruction from monocular RGB video using learned 3D reconstruction in a SLAM-style pipeline.
- [FreeSplatter: Pose-free Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-view 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09573): Builds Gaussian splats from sparse unposed images, removing the usual dependency on known camera poses.
- [Feat2GS: Probing Visual Foundation Models with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09606): Converts visual foundation-model features into Gaussian-splat representations to test how well those features support 3D reconstruction.
- [Stereo4D: Learning How Things Move in 3D from Internet Stereo Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09621): Mines internet stereoscopic videos into world-consistent 4D point clouds with long-term motion tracks, then trains DUSt3R-style models to infer structure and 3D motion from real image pairs.
- [SplineGS: Robust Motion-Adaptive Spline for Real-Time Dynamic 3D Gaussians from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09982): Models monocular-video dynamics with motion-adaptive splines over 3D Gaussians for real-time dynamic rendering.
- [How to Spin an Object: First, Get the Shape Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10273): Improves object rotation and view synthesis by prioritizing accurate 3D shape recovery before appearance generation.
- [GaussianWorld: Gaussian World Model for Streaming 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10373): Uses a Gaussian world model to maintain streaming 3D occupancy predictions for online scene understanding.
- [SweetTok: Semantic-Aware Spatial-Temporal Tokenizer for Compact Video Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10443): Tokenizes video with semantic-aware spatio-temporal units to create compact discrete representations for visual generation or modeling.
- [Benchmarking and Learning Multi-Dimensional Quality Evaluator for Text-To-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11170): Learns a multi-dimensional evaluator for text-to-3D outputs, covering quality factors beyond a single perceptual score.
- [OccScene: Semantic Occupancy-based Cross-task Mutual Learning for 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11183): Uses semantic occupancy as a shared representation for cross-task mutual learning in 3D scene generation.
- [GaussianProperty: Integrating Physical Properties to 3D Gaussians with LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11258): Attaches physical properties to 3D Gaussians with large multimodal-model guidance, making splatted scenes more simulation-aware.
- [MOVIS: Enhancing Multi-Object Novel View Synthesis for Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11457): Improves indoor novel-view synthesis by modeling multiple objects rather than treating the room as a single undifferentiated scene.
- [EditSplat: Multi-View Fusion and Attention-Guided Optimization for View-Consistent 3D Scene Editing with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11520): Fuses multi-view cues and attention-guided optimization to edit Gaussian-splat scenes while keeping edits view-consistent.
- [Deformable Radial Kernel Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11752): Replaces fixed Gaussian kernels with deformable radial kernels to better fit scene geometry during splat rendering.
- [Wonderland: Navigating 3D Scenes From a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12091): Generates navigable 3D scenes from a single image so users can move through plausible extrapolated views.
- [PanSplat: 4K Panorama Synthesis with Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12096): Synthesizes 4K panoramas with a feed-forward Gaussian-splatting model rather than per-scene optimization.
- [Gaussian Billboards: Expressive 2D Gaussian Splatting with Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12734): Represents scenes with textured 2D Gaussian billboards, making splats more expressive for surface appearance.
- [CATSplat: Context-Aware Transformer with Spatial Guidance for Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from a Single-View Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12906): Uses a spatially guided context-aware transformer to infer generalizable Gaussian splats from a single image.
- [Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Satellite Image Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13047): Applies Gaussian splatting to satellite photogrammetry for efficient 3D reconstruction and rendering from overhead imagery.
- [GaussTR: Foundation Model-Aligned Gaussian Transformer for Self-Supervised 3D Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13193): Aligns a Gaussian transformer with foundation-model features for self-supervised 3D spatial understanding.
- [Turbo-GS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Fitting for High-Quality Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13547): Speeds up 3D Gaussian fitting for radiance fields while retaining high-quality scene reconstruction.
- [DragScene: Interactive 3D Scene Editing with Single-view Drag Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13552): Lets users edit 3D scenes from single-view drag instructions by translating 2D interaction into consistent 3D changes.
- [GAGS: Granularity-Aware Feature Distillation for Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13654): Distills language features into Gaussian splats at multiple granularities for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding.
- [UA-MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Model Predictive Control for Motorized LiDAR Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13873): Applies uncertainty-aware model predictive control to motorized LiDAR odometry.
- [GraphAvatar: Compact Head Avatars with GNN-Generated 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13983): Generates compact 3D-Gaussian head avatars with a graph neural network over facial structure.
- [Prompting Depth Anything for 4K Resolution Accurate Metric Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14015): Adapts Depth Anything with prompting to produce accurate metric depth at 4K resolution.
- [MegaSynth: Scaling Up 3D Scene Reconstruction with Synthesized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14166): Scales 3D scene reconstruction by training on synthesized data designed to broaden geometry and appearance coverage.
- [MegaPairs: Massive Data Synthesis For Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14475): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it synthesizes VLM-generated image-text retrieval pairs for universal multimodal retrieval rather than learning 3D geometry or scene representations.
- [IDOL: Instant Photorealistic 3D Human Creation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14963): Creates a photorealistic 3D human from one image with an instant reconstruction and generation pipeline.
- [Generative Multiview Relighting for 3D Reconstruction under Extreme Illumination Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15211): Generates multiview relit images to make 3D reconstruction robust under severe illumination changes.
- [Scaling 4D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15212): Studies how larger data and model capacity improve 4D scene representations for dynamic spatial modeling.
- [GCA-3D: Towards Generalized and Consistent Domain Adaptation of 3D Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15491): Adapts 3D generators across domains with consistency constraints so generated shapes remain stable after transfer.
- [3D Shape Tokenization via Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15618): Tokenizes 3D shapes through latent flow matching, producing compact shape codes for generative modeling.
- [IRGS: Inter-Reflective Gaussian Splatting with 2D Gaussian Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15867): Adds 2D Gaussian ray tracing to model inter-reflections in Gaussian-splat scenes.
- [Topology-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting: Leveraging Persistent Homology for Optimized Structural Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16619): Uses persistent homology to regularize 3D Gaussian splats toward topologically coherent structure.
- [TAR3D: Creating High-Quality 3D Assets Via Next-Part Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16919): Generates 3D assets through next-part prediction, building complex shapes as sequences of parts.
- [GSemSplat: Generalizable Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting from Uncalibrated Image Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16932): Learns semantic Gaussian splats from uncalibrated image pairs, combining generalizable reconstruction with scene semantics.
- [LangSurf: Language-Embedded Surface Gaussians for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17635): Places language embeddings on surface-aligned Gaussians for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding.
- [GaussianPainter: Painting Point Cloud into 3D Gaussians with Normal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17715): Converts point clouds into 3D Gaussians using normal guidance to preserve surface orientation and geometry.
- [FaceLift: Learning Generalizable Single Image 3D Face Reconstruction From Synthetic Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17812): Trains single-image 3D face reconstruction on synthetic heads to improve generalization across identities and appearances.
- [DepthLab: From Partial to Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18153): Completes partial depth or geometry observations into fuller spatial structure for downstream 3D reconstruction.
- [3DGraphLLM: Combining Semantic Graphs and Large Language Models for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18450): Couples semantic 3D scene graphs with LLM reasoning to answer spatial queries over indoor scenes.
- [3DEnhancer: Consistent Multi-View Diffusion for 3D Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18565): Uses consistent multi-view diffusion to enhance 3D assets or reconstructions without breaking cross-view geometry.
- [Orient Anything: Learning Robust Object Orientation Estimation from Rendering 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18605): Learns object orientation from rendered 3D models, improving robustness across categories and viewpoints.
- [PartGen: Part-level 3D Generation and Reconstruction with Multi-View Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18608): Generates and reconstructs 3D objects at part level with multi-view diffusion models.
- [Multi-scale Latent Point Consistency Models for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19413): Builds 3D shapes with multi-scale latent point consistency, improving coherent point-based generation.
- [4D Gaussian Splatting: Modeling Dynamic Scenes with Native 4D Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20720): Represents dynamic scenes with native 4D Gaussian primitives instead of attaching motion to static 3D splats.
- [Prometheus: 3D-Aware Latent Diffusion Models for Feed-Forward Text-to-3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.21117): Uses 3D-aware latent diffusion for feed-forward text-to-3D scene generation.
- [PERSE: Personalized 3D Generative Avatars from A Single Portrait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.21206): Generates personalized 3D avatars from a single portrait, combining identity preservation with 3D avatar synthesis.
- [DyFusion: Cross-Attention 3D Object Detection with Dynamic Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/tla.2024.10412035): Uses cross-attention and dynamic fusion to combine multimodal cues for 3D object detection.
- [Is 3DGS Useful?: Comparing the Effectiveness of Recent Reconstruction Methods in VR](https://doi.org/10.1109/ismar62088.2024.00021): Compares recent 3D reconstruction methods inside VR to test whether 3DGS improves immersive reconstruction quality rather than only standard novel-view metrics.
- [Multi-Attribute Interactions Matter for 3D Visual Grounding](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01633): Models object attributes and their interactions for 3D visual grounding, addressing cases where language queries depend on multiple spatial and semantic cues.
- [Video Encoding Enhancement via Content-Aware Spatial and Temporal Super-Resolution](https://doi.org/10.23919/eusipco63174.2024.10714942): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it targets video coding quality through content-aware spatial and temporal super-resolution rather than 3D or spatial-reasoning model design.
- [MIX3D: A Mixed Representation for Communication-Efficient Distributed 3DGS Training](https://doi.org/10.1109/msn63567.2024.00060): Uses a mixed 3DGS representation to reduce communication overhead when training Gaussian splatting models across distributed devices.
- [Baking Neural Radiance Fields for Real-Time View Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3381001): Converts trained NeRFs into baked scene representations for real-time novel-view synthesis while preserving radiance-field view dependence.
- [Joint Semantic Segmentation using representations of LiDAR point clouds and camera images](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2024.102370): Fuses LiDAR point-cloud and camera-image representations for joint semantic segmentation, aligning range geometry with image appearance.
- [Parameter Efficient Self-Supervised Geospatial Domain Adaptation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.02630): Adapts geospatial vision models across domains with parameter-efficient self-supervision instead of full retraining on every remote-sensing distribution.
- [GauSPU: 3D Gaussian Splatting Processor for Real-Time SLAM Systems](https://doi.org/10.1109/micro61859.2024.00114): Designs a dedicated 3DGS processor for real-time SLAM pipelines, accelerating Gaussian splatting operations used in mapping and rendering.
- [LiDeNeRF: Neural radiance field reconstruction with depth prior provided by LiDAR point cloud](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.isprsjprs.2024.01.017): Conditions NeRF reconstruction on LiDAR-derived depth priors so sparse or ambiguous image observations recover more reliable scene geometry.
- [Point Cloud-Based Control Barrier Function Regression for Safe and Efficient Vision-Based Control](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610647): Learns control barrier functions from point clouds, linking spatial perception to safety constraints for vision-based robot control.
- [Memory-enhanced spatial-temporal encoding framework for industrial anomaly detection system](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2024.123718): Uses memory-augmented spatial-temporal encoders to detect industrial anomalies from structured sensor or visual streams.
- [Neural 3D Scene Reconstruction With Indoor Planar Priors](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3379833): Injects indoor planar priors into neural 3D reconstruction so walls, floors, and other dominant planes stabilize scene geometry.
- [Using a Large Language Model to turn Explorations of Virtual 3D-Worlds into Interactive Narrative Experiences](https://doi.org/10.1109/cog60054.2024.10645583): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it uses an LLM to convert virtual-world exploration traces into interactive narratives rather than improving spatial perception or 3D reconstruction.
- [Illumination Enlightened Spatial-temporal Inconsistency for Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme57554.2024.10687905): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it detects deepfakes via illumination-aware spatial-temporal inconsistencies in video frames, closer to media forensics than 3D spatial modeling.
- [GaussianHand: Real-Time 3D Gaussian Rendering for Hand Avatar Animation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2024.3516778): Represents articulated hand avatars with 3D Gaussians for real-time rendering and animation.
- [SPMHand: Segmentation-Guided Progressive Multi-Path 3D Hand Pose and Shape Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2024.3355652): Uses segmentation guidance and progressive multi-path estimation to recover 3D hand pose and shape from images.
- [High-quality Approximation of Scientific Data using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://doi.org/10.1109/ldav64567.2024.00018): Applies 3D Gaussian splatting to approximate large scientific data fields with high-quality interactive visualization.
- [LLM Acceleration on FPGAs: A Comparative Study of Layer and Spatial Accelerators](https://doi.org/10.1109/concapan63470.2024.10933896): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it compares FPGA layer and spatial accelerator designs for LLM inference, not spatial reasoning or 3D representation.
- [PanoSLAM: Panoptic 3D Scene Reconstruction via Gaussian SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00352): Unifies Gaussian SLAM with semantic and instance lifting so RGB-D videos produce panoptic 3D scene reconstructions.
- [STORM: Spatio-Temporal Reconstruction Model for Large-Scale Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00602): Reconstructs large outdoor dynamic scenes with a spatio-temporal model that separates static structure from moving content across long sequences.
- [GET3DGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2024.3511342): generates 3D Gaussians from point deformation fields, extending Gaussian-based spatial generation.
- [Adaptive CLIP for open-domain 3D model retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2024.103989): Adapts CLIP-style representations for open-domain 3D model retrieval, adding a retrieval-oriented spatial representation method alongside 3D grounding and asset-understanding models.
- [A Comprehensive Framework for 3D Occupancy Estimation in Autonomous Driving](https://doi.org/10.1109/tiv.2024.3403134): Builds an autonomous-driving 3D occupancy framework that predicts volumetric scene occupancy for downstream perception.
- [GeoRGS: Geometric Regularization for Real-Time Novel View Synthesis From Sparse Inputs](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2024.3447113): Regularizes sparse-input novel-view synthesis with geometric constraints so real-time rendering preserves scene structure.
- [RNNPose: 6-DoF Object Pose Estimation via Recurrent Correspondence Field Estimation and Pose Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3360181): Estimates 6-DoF object pose by recurrently updating correspondence fields and optimizing pose, targeting real-world pose recovery.
- [Spatial-Temporal Knowledge Transfer for Dynamic Constrained Multiobjective Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/tevc.2024.3449142): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it transfers spatial-temporal knowledge for dynamic constrained multiobjective optimization rather than 3D perception or spatial representation.
- [A Transformer-based multi-modal fusion network for 6D pose estimation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2024.102227): Uses a transformer fusion network to combine multimodal cues for 6D object pose estimation.
- [MH6D: Multi-Hypothesis Consistency Learning for Category-Level 6-D Object Pose Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnnls.2024.3360712): Learns multiple pose hypotheses with consistency constraints for category-level 6D object pose estimation.
- [Hierarchically Recognizing Vector Graphics and A New Chart-Based Vector Graphics Dataset](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3394298): Off-axis for Spatial Model unless diagram understanding is in scope: it recognizes vector graphics hierarchically and releases a chart-based vector-graphics dataset.
- [Match Normalization: Learning-Based Point Cloud Registration for 6D Object Pose Estimation in the Real World](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3355198): Normalizes learned point-cloud matches before registration to improve real-world 6D object pose estimation.
- [AWARDistill: Adaptive and robust 3D object detection in adverse conditions through knowledge distillation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2024.126032): Distills robust 3D object detectors for adverse conditions, adapting teacher knowledge to degraded perception settings.
- [Learning Spherical Radiance Field for Efficient 360° Unbounded Novel View Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2024.3409052): Learns spherical radiance fields for efficient 360-degree unbounded novel-view synthesis.
- [DVMNet: Computing Relative Pose for Unseen Objects Beyond Hypotheses](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01936): Computes relative pose for unseen objects without relying only on a finite set of pose hypotheses.
- [HomuGAN: A 3D-Aware GAN With the Method of Cylindrical Spatial-Constrained Sampling](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2024.3520423): Adds cylindrical spatial-constrained sampling to a 3D-aware GAN to improve geometry-aware image synthesis.
- [Gaussian Building Mesh (GBM): Extract a Building's 3D Mesh with Google Earth and Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00625): Reconstructs building meshes from Google Earth imagery by combining Gaussian splatting with mesh extraction for urban-scale geometry.
- [D3-Human](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01589): reconstructs dynamic disentangled digital humans from monocular video, extending dynamic human spatial representation.
- [PG-SAG: Parallel Gaussian Splatting for Fine-Grained Large-Scale Urban Buildings Reconstruction via Semantic-Aware Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01677): Groups urban-building Gaussians by semantic regions and reconstructs them in parallel to scale fine-grained 3DGS to large city scenes.
- [HaWoR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02973): reconstructs world-space hand motion from egocentric videos.
- [ProTracker: Probabilistic Integration for Robust and Accurate Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03220): Integrates point-track evidence probabilistically to improve long-range tracking robustness and accuracy across video frames.
- [DoubleDiffusion: Combining Heat Diffusion with Denoising Diffusion for Texture Generation on 3D Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03397): Combines heat diffusion over mesh surfaces with denoising diffusion priors to generate coherent textures directly on 3D meshes.
- [MoDec-GS: Global-to-Local Motion Decomposition and Temporal Interval Adjustment for Compact Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03714): Decomposes motion for compact dynamic 3D Gaussian splatting, improving efficient time-varying spatial representation.
- [FatesGS: Fast and Accurate Sparse-View Surface Reconstruction using Gaussian Splatting with Depth-Feature Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04628): Improves sparse-view surface reconstruction with Gaussian splatting and depth-feature consistency.
- [Segs-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05242): strengthens 3D Gaussian splatting SLAM with structural cues and appearance embeddings.
- [Arc2Avatar: Generating Expressive 3D Avatars from a Single Image via ID Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05379): Generates expressive 3D avatars from a single image with identity guidance, extending single-view human spatial generation.
- [Consistent Flow Distillation for Text-to-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05445): distills flow consistency for text-to-3D generation.
