- [Locality-Aware Gaussian Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05757): compresses Gaussian splatting for fast and high-quality rendering with locality-aware structure.
- [SkeletonDiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06035): Uses nonisotropic Gaussian diffusion aligned with skeleton kinematics for realistic 3D human motion prediction.
- [F3D-Gaus: Feed-forward 3D-aware Generation on ImageNet with Cycle-Consistent Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06714): Trains feed-forward ImageNet-scale 3D-aware generation with cycle-consistent Gaussian splatting to avoid per-scene optimization.
- [ActiveGAMER: Active GAussian Mapping through Efficient Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06897): Uses 3D Gaussian Splatting and rendering-based information gain for active mapping, selecting informative viewpoints while reconstructing complex environments in real time.
- [PoAct: Policy and Action Dual-Control Agent for Generalized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07054): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it is an LLM agent framework that switches reasoning policies and code-action spaces, closer to Agent Harness than 3D modeling.
- [Skip Mamba Diffusion for Monocular 3D Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07260): Uses a Mamba-based diffusion design for monocular 3D semantic scene completion, filling dense scene geometry and semantics from one view.
- [UnCommon Objects in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07574): Primarily a Bench/Data candidate: uCO3D releases high-resolution 360-degree object videos with camera poses, depth, point clouds, captions, and 3DGS reconstructions for 3D learning.
- [3UR-LLM: An End-to-End Multimodal Large Language Model for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07819): Connects 3D scene representations to a multimodal LLM for end-to-end 3D scene understanding.
- [Object-Centric 2D Gaussian Splatting: Background Removal and Occlusion-Aware Pruning for Compact Object Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08174): Uses object masks and occlusion-aware pruning to reconstruct compact object-specific 2DGS or mesh models instead of full scenes.
- [LLaVA-ST: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Fine-Grained Spatial-Temporal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08282): Adds fine-grained spatial-temporal localization to multimodal LLM understanding.
- [VINGS-Mono](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08286): combines visual-inertial monocular SLAM with Gaussian splatting for large-scene reconstruction.
- [Omni-RGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08326): Uses token marks to unify image and video region-level understanding, strengthening model-native visual grounding.
- [Predicting 4D Hand Trajectory from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08329): Recovers hand trajectories over three-dimensional space and time from monocular video, extending pose prediction to 4D motion.
- [GS-LIVO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08672): fuses LiDAR, inertial, and visual sensing for real-time odometry with Gaussian mapping.
- [SLC$^{2}$-SLAM: Semantic-Guided Loop Closure Using Shared Latent Code for NeRF SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08880): Uses semantic guidance and shared latent codes to add loop closure to NeRF SLAM, improving global map consistency.
- [CityDreamer4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08983): Composes unbounded 4D cities from separate static-scene and dynamic-object representations.
- [AugRefer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09428): Improves 3D visual grounding through cross-modal augmentation and spatial-relation referring.
- [Mitigating Hallucinations on Object Attributes using Multiview Images and Negative Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10011): Reduces object-attribute hallucinations by using multiview evidence and negative instructions to align visual-language outputs with observed 3D object properties.
- [CLIP-PCQA: Exploring Subjective-Aligned Vision-Language Modeling for Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10071): Aligns point-cloud quality assessment with language descriptions through CLIP-style retrieval, making no-reference 3D quality scoring more subjective-aware.
- [GauSTAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10283): performs Gaussian surface tracking and reconstruction for dynamic surface modeling.
- [Decoupling Appearance Variations with 3D Consistent Features in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10788): Models local appearance changes outside the Gaussian field with 3D-consistent features, reducing floaters and color distortions while keeping rendering efficient.
- [HAC++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12255): targets 100x compression for 3D Gaussian splatting while preserving practical scene representation.
- [DARB-Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12369): generalizes splatting with decaying anisotropic radial basis functions.
- [Parallel Sequence Modeling via Generalized Spatial Propagation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12381): Off-axis but spatially relevant: it proposes a 2D spatial propagation attention mechanism that preserves image-grid structure and accelerates very large image generation.
- [GoDe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13558): uses Gaussians on demand for progressive level of detail and scalable 3DGS compression.
- [Micro-macro Wavelet-based Gaussian Splatting for 3D Reconstruction from Unconstrained Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14231): Uses micro-macro wavelet decomposition in Gaussian splatting to reconstruct 3D scenes from unconstrained image collections.
- [Relightable Full-Body Gaussian Codec Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14726): Builds relightable full-body Gaussian avatars, adding whole-body controllability to spatial human representation generation.
- [HuGDiffusion: Generalizable Single-Image Human Rendering via 3D Gaussian Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15008): Uses 3D Gaussian diffusion to render humans from a single image, targeting generalizable novel views of human geometry and appearance.
- [LinPrim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16312): uses linear primitives for differentiable volumetric rendering.
- [SliceOcc: Indoor 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction with Vertical Slice Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16684): Represents indoor occupancy as vertical slices, improving semantic 3D occupancy prediction from monocular or sparse observations.
- [3D-MoE: A Mixture-of-Experts Multi-modal LLM for 3D Vision and Pose Diffusion via Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16698): Uses a mixture-of-experts multimodal LLM for 3D vision and pose diffusion.
- [StructuredField: Unifying Structured Geometry and Radiance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18152): Unifies structured geometric primitives with radiance fields so explicit structure and neural rendering share one scene representation.
- [Deformable Beta Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18630): introduces deformable beta splatting for dynamic 3D representation.
- [Drag Your Gaussian: Effective Drag-Based Editing with Score Distillation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18672): Enables drag-based editing of 3D Gaussian splats with score distillation, adding direct manipulation to spatial scene editing.
- [RLS3: RL-Based Synthetic Sample Selection to Enhance Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models for Indoor Autonomous Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18880): Uses an RL agent to synthesize informative indoor spatial-reasoning samples for fine-tuning vision-language models.
- [OmniPhysGS: 3D Constitutive Gaussians for General Physics-Based Dynamics Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18982): OmniPhysGS targets general Physics-Based Dynamics Generation with 3D Constitutive Gaussians.
- [Lifting by Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00173): provides a simple and flexible Gaussian-based method for 3D instance segmentation.
- [WonderHuman: Hallucinating Unseen Parts in Dynamic 3D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01045): Reconstructs dynamic 3D humans by hallucinating unobserved body regions, improving completeness under occlusion and limited views.
- [CleanPose: Category-Level Object Pose Estimation via Causal Learning and Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01312): Combines front-door causal adjustment with residual knowledge distillation to reduce spurious correlations in category-level object pose estimation.
- [Diff9D: Diffusion-Based Domain-Generalized Category-Level 9-DoF Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02525): Uses diffusion modeling to estimate category-level 9-DoF object pose with stronger domain generalization.
- [Articulate AnyMesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02590): Models open-vocabulary articulated 3D objects, extending spatial generation toward language-aligned articulated assets.
- [GARAD-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03228): applies 3D Gaussian splatting to real-time anti-dynamic SLAM, extending dynamic-scene mapping models.
- [Dress-1-to-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03449): turns a single image into simulation-ready 3D outfits with diffusion priors and differentiable physics.
- [GCE-Pose: Global Context Enhancement for Category-level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04293): Enhances category-level object pose estimation with global context cues that disambiguate object geometry and orientation.
- [sshELF: Single-Shot Hierarchical Extrapolation of Latent Features for 3D Reconstruction from Sparse-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04318): Extrapolates latent features hierarchically in a single shot to reconstruct 3D objects or scenes from sparse views.
- [DreamDPO: Aligning Text-to-3D Generation with Human Preferences via Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04370): Aligns text-to-3D generation with human preferences using DPO.
- [GaussRender: Learning 3D Occupancy with Gaussian Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05040): Learns 3D occupancy through Gaussian rendering, using differentiable splatting signals to supervise volumetric scene structure.
- [PINGS: Gaussian Splatting Meets Distance Fields within a Point-Based Implicit Neural Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05752): Combines Gaussian splatting with distance fields inside a point-based implicit neural map for geometry-aware scene reconstruction.
- [TripoSG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06608): Uses large-scale rectified-flow models for high-fidelity 3D shape synthesis, adding a spatial generation model line.
- [Articulate That Object Part (ATOP): 3D Part Articulation via Text and Motion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07278): Personalizes 3D part articulation with text and motion conditions, adding language-controlled movable-object modeling.
- [MeshSplats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07754): initializes Gaussian splatting from meshes to improve mesh-based neural rendering.
- [Pippo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07785): Generates high-resolution multi-view human representations from one image, extending single-view-to-3D human spatial generation.
- [TranSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07840): uses surface embeddings to guide 3D Gaussian splatting for transparent-object manipulation.
- [DenseSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09111): densifies 3D Gaussian splatting SLAM with a neural radiance prior for denser scene reconstruction.
- [ConsistentDreamer: View-Consistent Meshes Through Balanced Multi-View Gaussian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09278): Balances fixed multi-view priors with Gaussian optimization and SDS losses so image-to-3D meshes stay view-consistent.
- [Human-LLM Coevolution: Evidence from Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09606): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it analyzes changing LLM-associated word frequencies in arXiv abstracts, not spatial capability or 3D modeling.
- [RigAnything: Template-Free Autoregressive Rigging for Diverse 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09615): Generates rigs for diverse 3D assets without templates through autoregressive modeling, extending spatial assets toward animation-ready structure.
- [Text-guided Sparse Voxel Pruning for Efficient 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10392): Prunes sparse voxels under text guidance for efficient 3D visual grounding, linking language queries with compact spatial evidence.
- [Occlusion-Aware Text-Image-Point Cloud Pretraining for Open-World 3D Object Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10674): Pretrains text, image, and point-cloud encoders on occlusion-aware partial point clouds, then uses DuoMamba to reduce open-world 3D recognition cost.
- [MARS: Mesh AutoRegressive Model for 3D Shape Detailization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11390): Autoregressively refines meshes for 3D shape detailization, adding fine geometry after coarse shape generation.
- [3D Gaussian Inpainting with Depth-Guided Cross-View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11801): Uses depth-guided cross-view constraints to make 3D Gaussian inpainting geometrically and texturally consistent.
- [MagicArticulate: Make Your 3D Models Articulation-Ready](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12135): Makes static 3D models articulation-ready, adding a spatial-asset preparation method for interactive and embodied use.
- [PUGS: Zero-Shot Physical Understanding with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12231): Reconstructs objects as 3D Gaussian splats, improves shape quality with geometry-aware regularization and region-aware contrastive loss, then propagates physical properties through Gaussian features and volume integration for zero-shot mass, friction, and hardness prediction.
- [Not-So-Optimal Transport Flows for 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12456): Replaces costly equivariant optimal-transport flows with precomputed approximate OT pairings to scale point-cloud generation to larger shapes.
- [Textured 3D Regenerative Morphing with 3D Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14316): Uses a 3D diffusion prior for regenerative morphing so textured 3D assets can be edited while preserving coherent geometry.
- [CrossOver: 3D Scene Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15011): Aligns 3D scene representations across modalities so language, image, and point-cloud evidence refer to consistent scene content.
- [OpenGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15633): performs RGB-only Gaussian Splatting SLAM for unbounded outdoor scenes using pointmap regression.
- [Dragen3D: Multiview Geometry Consistent 3D Gaussian Generation with Drag-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16475): Adds drag-based controls to multiview-consistent 3D Gaussian generation so users can steer geometry while maintaining cross-view coherence.
- [Efficient 4D Gaussian Stream with Low Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16575): Uses low-rank adaptation to stream dynamic 4D Gaussian representations more efficiently across time.
- [Dr. Splat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16652): Registers language embeddings directly to 3D Gaussians for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding.
- [PointSea: Point Cloud Completion via Self-structure Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17053): Completes point clouds by augmenting their self-structure, using internal geometric regularities rather than only external shape priors.
- [V-HOP: Visuo-Haptic 6D Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17434): Combines visual and haptic observations to track 6D object pose during physical interaction.
- [LAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17796): Builds a one-shot large avatar model for animatable Gaussian heads, extending controllable 3D avatar generation.
- [The NeRF Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19125): Adds codebook-aided watermarking to Neural Radiance Fields for copyright protection of 3D neural content.
- [Accurate Volumetric Rendering in 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19318): revisits whether 3D Gaussian splatting requires accurate volumetric rendering.
- [Interactable Replicas via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19459): Reconstructs interactable replicas of complex articulated objects, linking spatial reconstruction with action-ready 3D assets.
- [GenPC: Zero-shot Point Cloud Completion via 3D Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19896): Uses pretrained 3D generative priors for zero-shot point-cloud completion, avoiding task-specific completion training.
- [Avat3r](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20220): Builds a large animatable Gaussian reconstruction model for high-fidelity 3D head avatars, extending spatial avatar generation.
- [Sparse Time-Variant 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20378): uses sparse time-variant attributes for efficient monocular dynamic-scene Gaussian splatting.
- [FunHOI: Annotation-Free 3D Hand-Object Interaction Generation via Functional Text Guidanc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20805): Generates 3D hand-object interactions from functional text guidance without requiring paired HOI annotations.
- [Spatial Reasoning with Denoising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.21075): adds denoising Models to spatial Reasoning.
- [Scalable Real2Sim: Physics-Aware Asset Generation Via Robotic Pick-and-Place Setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00370): Automates simulation-ready asset generation by recovering geometry and physical properties through robotic interaction.
- [GaussianSeal: Rooting Adaptive Watermarks for 3D Gaussian Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00531): Embeds adaptive watermarks into 3D Gaussian generation models, targeting provenance and protection for generated Gaussian assets.
- [Contribution-Adaptive 3DGS Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00881): Balances high-quality rendering and geometric reconstruction in 3D Gaussian splatting with contribution-adaptive regularization.
- [Kiss3DGen: Repurposing Image Diffusion Models for 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01370): Repurposes image diffusion models to generate, edit, and enhance 3D objects through multi-view bundle images and normal maps.
- [MeshPad: Interactive Sketch-Conditioned Artist-Reminiscent Mesh Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01425): Conditions mesh generation and editing on artist-style sketches so users can steer 3D geometry with drawn controls.
- [OpenGS-SLAM: Open-Set Dense Semantic SLAM with 3D Gaussian Splatting for Object-Level Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01646): Builds open-set object-level semantic SLAM by injecting 2D foundation-model labels into 3D Gaussian maps and rendering label maps for efficient scene updates.
- [MUSt3R: Multi-view Network for Stereo 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01661): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for spatial reasoning and spatial intelligence. Core idea: DUSt3R introduced a novel paradigm in geometric computer vision by proposing a model that can provide dense and unconstrained Stereo 3D Reconstruction of arbitrary image collections with no prior information about camera calibration nor viewpoint poses.
- [Why Is Spatial Reasoning Hard for VLMs? An Attention Mechanism Perspective on Focus Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01773): Analyzes why VLMs struggle with spatial relations by studying attention focus areas.
- [Union of Experts: Adapting Hierarchical Routing to Equivalently Decomposed Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02495): Off-axis for Spatial Model: it decomposes transformers into hierarchically routed experts and extends MoE routing to attention blocks.
- [Beyond Existance: Fulfill 3D Reconstructed Scenes with Pseudo Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04037): Adds pseudo details to incomplete 3D reconstructed scenes, filling missing fine structure after coarse reconstruction.
- [How to Move Your Dragon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04257): generates text-conditioned motion for large-vocabulary object categories.
- [S2Gaussian: Sparse-View Super-Resolution 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04314): Reconstructs high-resolution 3D Gaussian scenes from sparse low-resolution views through staged Gaussian upsampling and refinement.
- [Transferable foundation models for geometric tasks on point cloud representations: geometric neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04649): Pretrains geometric neural operators on point clouds to estimate surface metrics, curvature, geometric PDE solutions, and shape deformations.
- [GrainPaint: A multi-scale diffusion-based generative model for microstructure reconstruction of large-scale objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04776): Off-axis but spatially generative: it uses multi-scale denoising diffusion inpainting to reconstruct large material microstructures beyond fixed generation windows.
- [Spectral Informed Mamba for Robust Point Cloud Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04953): Incorporates spectral information into Mamba-style sequence modeling for robust point-cloud classification and segmentation.
- [Taming Video Diffusion Prior with Scene-Grounding Guidance for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05082): Guides sparse-input 3D Gaussian splatting with video diffusion priors and scene grounding.
- [GaussianCAD: Robust Self-Supervised CAD Reconstruction from Three Orthographic Views Using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05161): Reconstructs CAD geometry from three orthographic views with self-supervised 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [EvolvingGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05162): Uses evolving 3D Gaussian representations for high-fidelity streamable volumetric video.
- [SeeLe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05168): Accelerates real-time Gaussian splatting through a unified framework, adding a deployment-oriented route for interactive 3D scene representations.
- [SecureGS: Boosting the Security and Fidelity of 3D Gaussian Splatting Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06118): Protects 3DGS assets with decoupled Gaussian encryption and neural decoding so hidden messages preserve rendering fidelity and point-cloud structure security.
- [StreamGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06235): performs online generalizable Gaussian-splatting reconstruction from unposed image streams.
- [Localization Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06287): Shows that a small set of LVLM attention heads can localize visually grounded text references without extra grounding modules.
- [OV-SCAN: Semantically Consistent Alignment for Novel Object Discovery in Open-Vocabulary 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06435): Aligns open-vocabulary 3D detection features semantically so novel object discovery stays consistent across modalities.
- [SP3D: Boosting Sparsely-Supervised 3D Object Detection via Accurate Cross-Modal Semantic Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06467): Uses LMM-generated cross-modal semantic prompts and dynamic pseudo-label clustering to improve 3D detection under sparse annotation.
- [Vector Quantized Feature Fields for Fast 3D Semantic Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06469): Compresses semantic feature fields with vector quantization to accelerate 2D-to-3D semantic lifting.
- [Online Dense Point Tracking with Streaming Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06471): Tracks dense points online with streaming memory so long videos retain correspondences without storing all frames.
- [AxisPose: Model-Free Matching-Free Single-Shot 6D Object Pose Estimation via Axis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06660): Directly predicts 6D object pose from a single view by generating latent object axes, avoiding CAD models, matching, SfM, and PnP.
- [REArtGS: Reconstructing and Generating Articulated Objects via 3D Gaussian Splatting with Geometric and Motion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06677): Combines 3D Gaussian splatting with geometry and motion constraints for articulated-object reconstruction and generation.
- [SOGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07476): uses second-order anchors to improve advanced 3D Gaussian splatting representations.
- [POp-GS: Next Best View in 3D-Gaussian Splatting with P-Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07819): Selects next-best views for 3D Gaussian splatting using P-optimality, targeting more informative image acquisition during reconstruction.
- [FunGraph: Functionality Aware 3D Scene Graphs for Language-Prompted Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07909): Augments 3D scene graphs with affordance-relevant functional parts so language prompts can ground interactive scene elements.
- [7DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07946): unifies spatial, temporal, and angular Gaussian splatting for richer 4D-style scene representation.
- [S3R-GS: Streamlining the Pipeline for Large-Scale Street Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08217): Removes unnecessary transforms, projections, and distant-content rendering from street-scene 3DGS to reduce large-scale reconstruction cost.
- [HRAvatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08224): Builds high-quality relightable Gaussian head avatars, adding controllable 3D human representation to spatial generation.
- [Twinner: Shining Light on Digital Twins in a Few Snaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08382): Builds digital-twin reconstructions from only a few captures, emphasizing quick scene or object digitization under limited views.
- [WildSeg3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08407): Segments arbitrary 3D objects in the wild from 2D images, adding an open-world 3D parsing route.
- [PCGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08511): progressively compresses 3D Gaussian splatting representations.
- [SAS: Segment Any 3D Scene with Integrated 2D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08512): Aligns multiple 2D open-vocabulary models through text, estimates their category capabilities, and distills fused priors into 3D scene segmentation.
- [High-Quality 3D Head Reconstruction from Any Single Portrait Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08516): Uses identity and expression-aware multiview diffusion plus a high-quality portrait dataset to reconstruct 3D heads from arbitrary single portraits.
- [Dual-Domain Homogeneous Fusion with Cross-Modal Mamba and Progressive Decoder for 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08992): Fuses image and LiDAR features in both BEV and voxel domains with cross-modal Mamba and progressive decoding for 3D detection.
- [Online Language Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09447): adds online language-aligned Gaussian splatting for open-vocabulary spatial scene representations.
- [CleverDistiller: Simple and Spatially Consistent Cross-modal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09878): Distills cross-modal knowledge with spatial consistency constraints so dense predictions respect geometry across modalities.
- [PanoGen++: Domain-Adapted Text-Guided Panoramic Environment Generation for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09938): Fine-tunes diffusion models with LoRA for panoramic inpainting and outpainting, generating VLN environments from text.
- [TGP: Two-modal occupancy prediction with 3D Gaussian and sparse points for 3D Environment Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09941): Combines 3D Gaussian sets with sparse points to predict semantic occupancy while retaining volumetric detail and point-level spatial precision.
- [GaussHDR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10143): learns unified 3D and 2D local tone mapping for high-dynamic-range Gaussian splatting.
- [VicaSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10286): Jointly estimates cameras and reconstructs 3D Gaussian scenes from unposed video frames in a single feed-forward pass.
- [MaterialMVP: Illumination-Invariant Material Generation via Multi-View PBR Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10289): Uses multi-view PBR diffusion for illumination-invariant material generation, adding material control to 3D spatial assets.
- [4D LangSplat: 4D Language Gaussian Splatting via Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10437): Extends language Gaussian splatting to 4D scenes with multimodal large language models, linking dynamic spatial representations with language grounding.
- [Unifying 2D and 3D Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10745): Unifies 2D and 3D vision-language understanding with a shared grounding decoder and 2D-to-3D lifting strategy for spatial perception.
- [GaussianIP: Identity-Preserving Realistic 3D Human Generation via Human-Centric Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11143): Uses a human-centric diffusion prior to generate realistic 3D humans while preserving identity in Gaussian-based representations.
- [TreeMeshGPT: Artistic Mesh Generation with Autoregressive Tree Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11629): Generates artistic meshes with autoregressive tree sequencing, extending structured mesh synthesis for spatial assets.
- [VGGT: Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11651): Introduces the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer for feed-forward 3D reconstruction and spatial perception.
- [Swift4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12307): uses adaptive divide-and-conquer Gaussian splatting for compact dynamic-scene reconstruction.
- [GS-I3: Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction from Illumination-Inconsistent Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12335): Uses CNN tone-mapping correction and normal compensation from single-view and multi-view normals to reconstruct surfaces under inconsistent illumination.
- [SPC-GS: Gaussian Splatting with Semantic-Prompt Consistency for Indoor Open-World Free-view Synthesis from Sparse Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12535): Uses scene-layout Gaussian initialization from video-generated view changes and SAM2 semantic-prompt consistency for sparse-input indoor open-world free-view synthesis.
- [MTGS: Multi-Traversal Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12552): Model candidate for spatial and 3D reasoning: Develops multi-traversal Gaussian splatting for 3D modeling.
- [KISS-SLAM: A Simple, Robust, and Accurate 3D LiDAR SLAM System With Enhanced Generalization Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12660): Keeps LiDAR-only SLAM minimal with scan-to-map odometry, local-map matching, and pose-graph optimization without IMU or GNSS.
- [ProtoDepth: Unsupervised Continual Depth Completion with Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12745): Learns domain-specific prototype sets and descriptors on a frozen depth-completion backbone to reduce forgetting across indoor and outdoor domains.
- [PASTA: Part-Aware Sketch-to-3D Shape Generation with Text-Aligned Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12834): Combines sketch input with vision-language text part priors and ISG-Net graph convolutions for controllable part-aware 3D shape generation.
- [RGBAvatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12886): Maps tracked 3D morphable-model parameters to compact Gaussian blendshape bases for real-time photorealistic head-avatar reconstruction.
- [AR-1-to-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12929): Predicts nearby novel views first and feeds them through stacked local and LSTM global encoders to generate farther views with better 3D consistency.
- [TFDM: Time-Variant Frequency-Based Point Cloud Diffusion with Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13004): Reorders point clouds along space-filling curves for latent Mamba diffusion and uses a time-varying frequency encoder to sharpen late-stage details.
- [DeGauss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13176): Separates foreground dynamic and background static Gaussians with probabilistic masks for self-supervised distractor-free scene reconstruction from cluttered videos.
- [Generative Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13272): Parameterizes video-diffusion features as 3D Gaussian fields that render multi-view images or upsample into radiance fields for more consistent 3D scenes.
- [Infinite Mobility: Scalable High-Fidelity Synthesis of Articulated Objects via Procedural Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13424): Uses procedural generation to synthesize high-fidelity articulated objects with physical properties and mesh quality suitable for training generative models.
- [Amodal3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13439): Extends a foundation 3D generator with mask-weighted cross-attention and occlusion-aware attention to recover full objects from partial 2D observations.
- [SALAD Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13836): Models joints, frames, and words in skeleton-aware latent diffusion and reuses cross-attention maps for zero-shot text-driven motion editing.
- [2D-to-3D Scene Segmentation in 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14029): Augments 3D Gaussians with contrastive instance features and a learnable object codebook for end-to-end 2D-to-3D scene segmentation.
- [DUNE Universal Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14405): Co-distills heterogeneous 2D, 3D, and human-perception teachers into one encoder for classification, segmentation, depth, 3D understanding, and relocalization.
- [Bolt3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14445): Samples 3D scene representations in under seven seconds with latent diffusion trained on dense-reconstruction-derived multiview-consistent geometry and appearance.
- [SuperPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14558): Uses a three-level-conditioned diffusion framework with spatial-mix fusion to jointly complete, upsample, denoise, and colorize point clouds.
- [GIVEPose: Gradual Intra-class Variation Elimination for RGB-based Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15110): Uses an intra-class-variation-free consensus map alongside NOCS to improve RGB-only category-level 6D object pose estimation.
- [DeepMesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15265): Pretrains improved mesh tokenization and applies preference alignment so autoregressive mesh generation favors detailed topology and human-preferred geometry.
- [Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion-Guided Refinement of 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15742): Iteratively refines single-image Gaussian scene reconstructions with a latent video diffusion prior, Fourier-style transfer, and semantic uncertainty maps.
- [Acc3D: Accelerating Single Image to 3D Diffusion Models via Edge Consistency Guided Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15975): Distills image-to-3D diffusion with edge-consistency regularization and adversarial augmentation to reduce sampling cost while improving generation detail.
- [Animating the Uncaptured](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15996): Renders a static humanoid mesh, drives a video diffusion model with a text motion prompt, and transfers the generated motion back through SMPL optimization.
- [OccluGaussian: Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Splatting for Large Scene Reconstruction and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16177): Clusters cameras by co-visibility for occlusion-aware large-scene 3DGS partitions and culls region-invisible Gaussians for faster rendering.
- [Generalized Few-shot 3D Point Cloud Segmentation with Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16282): Filters noisy 3D VLM pseudo-labels with prototype guidance, infills unlabeled regions, and mixes few-shot samples into scenes for novel-class point-cloud segmentation.
- [Vecset Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16302): Accelerates Vecset Diffusion with progressive flow distillation for few-step DiT sampling and a sparse locality-aware vecset decoder.
- [Gaussian Graph Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16338): Builds Gaussian graphs across multi-view pixel-aligned Gaussian groups, using message passing and pooling to reduce redundant splats in feed-forward reconstruction.
- [NuiScene: Exploring Efficient Generation of Unbounded Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16375): Encodes outdoor scene chunks as uniform vector sets and trains an explicit outpainting model for faster coherent unbounded 3D scene generation.
- [Syncity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16420): Combines pretrained 3D object generators with 2D image generators in a tile-by-tile context-aware pipeline for optimization-free text-to-3D world creation.
- [Vision-Language Embodiment for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16535): Embeds camera intrinsics and language depth priors into monocular depth estimation so RGB features are grounded by scene-depth interactions.
- [iFlame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16653): Interleaves full and linear attention in an hourglass autoregressive transformer, plus KV-cache compression, for efficient high-resolution mesh generation.
- [GAIR: Location-aware self-supervised contrastive pre-training with geo-aligned implicit representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16683): Extends ViT remote-sensing features with neural implicit local interpolation and contrastively aligns overhead, street-view, and geolocation embeddings.
- [Cross-Modal and Uncertainty-Aware Agglomeration for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16707): Distills CLIP, DINOv2, and Stable Diffusion features into 3D scene understanding with deterministic uncertainty weighting for open-vocabulary segmentation.
- [4D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16710): Separates static and dynamic Gaussian sets, models dynamic transformations with control points and an MLP, and supervises motion with rendered optical flow.
- [RigGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16822): Extracts sparse skeleton nodes from video-driven Gaussian deformations and learns skinning plus pose-dependent detail for editable articulated-object rendering.
- [Optimized Minimal 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16924): Selects distinct Gaussians, uses compact attribute representations and sub-vector quantization, and renders minimal 3DGS scenes at high frame rates with lower storage.
- [DroneSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16964): Removes drone-scene dynamic distractors with local-global segmentation thresholds and improves 3DGS using MVS predictions plus voxel-guided optimization.
- [Instant Gaussian Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16979): Streams free-viewpoint video by predicting Gaussian motion from multi-view 2D motion features and refining key frames to limit temporal error accumulation.
- [3D Neural Operator-Based Flow Surrogates around 3D geometries: Signed Distance Functions and Derivative Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17289): Tests DeepONet variants on complex-geometry 3D flow simulations and uses SDF geometry plus derivative constraints for physics-consistent surrogates.
- [Pow3R: Empowering Unconstrained 3D Reconstruction with Camera and Scene Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17316): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for spatial reasoning and spatial intelligence. Core idea: We present Pow3r, a novel large 3D vision regression model that is highly versatile in the input modalities it accepts.
- [Is there anything left? Measuring semantic residuals of objects removed from 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17574): Defines metrics for semantic residues after object removal from 3DGS scenes and refines removal with spatial and semantic consistency.
- [MotionDiff: Training-Free Zero-Shot Interactive Motion Editing via Flow-Assisted Multi-View Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17695): Estimates multi-view optical flows from user motion priors and feeds them to training-free diffusion for zero-shot, multi-view-consistent scene motion editing.
- [Co-op: Correspondence-based Novel Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17731): Matches semi-dense RGB-to-template correspondences and refines probabilistic flow through differentiable PnP for fast CAD-model-based 6DoF pose estimation.
- [GS-LTS: 3D Gaussian Splatting-Based Adaptive Modeling for Long-Term Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17733): Detects indoor scene changes, collects robot observations with a rule policy, and edits 3D Gaussians for long-term map updates.
- [SymmCompletion: High-Fidelity and High-Consistency Point Cloud Completion with Symmetry Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18007): Estimates local symmetry transformations and uses a symmetry-guidance transformer to complete point clouds while preserving observed geometry.
- [SceneSplat: Gaussian Splatting-Based Scene Understanding with Vision-Language Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18052): SceneSplat adds vision-Language Pretraining to gaussian Splatting-Based Scene Understanding.
- [PanoGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18107): Models 3D language features with pyramid tri-planes, groups Gaussian primitives via language-guided graph cuts, and clusters super-primitives for panoptic open-vocabulary scenes.
- [MLLM-For3D: Adapting Multimodal Large Language Model for 3D Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18135): Turns 2D MLLM pseudo masks into 3D reasoning segmentation with spatial-consistency filtering and token-for-query text alignment.
- [SimMotionEdit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18211): Jointly trains motion editing and motion-similarity prediction in a Diffusion Transformer to improve text-aligned 3D human-motion edits.
- [NeRFPrior: Learning Neural Radiance Field as a Prior for Indoor Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18361): Trains a scene-specific NeRF prior to supply geometry and color cues for SDF surface reconstruction with multi-view and confidence-weighted depth consistency.
- [DashGaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18402): Treats 3DGS fitting as progressive frequency optimization and schedules rendering resolution with primitive growth to cut redundant training cost.
- [UniPCGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18541): Unifies lossless and lossy point-cloud geometry compression with uneven lossless coding, rate modulation, and dynamic sparse convolution.
- [Any6D: Model-free 6D Pose Estimation of Novel Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18673): Uses one RGB-D anchor image, joint object alignment, and render-and-compare pose refinement to estimate pose and scale for unseen objects.
- [Hardware-Rasterized Ray-Based 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18682): Derives hardware-rasterized shaders for ray-based Gaussian splatting and alias-free rendering for high-frame-rate VR and mixed-reality novel view synthesis.
- [GS-Marker: Generalizable and Robust Watermarking for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18718): Embeds watermarks into 3DGS with a 3D encoder, distortion layers, adaptive marker control, and a 2D rendering decoder.
- [RomanTex: Decoupling 3D-Aware Rotary Positional Embedded Multi-Attention Network for Texture Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19011): Combines multiview diffusion priors with a 3D-aware rotary-position multi-attention network, decoupled attention blocks, and geometry-related CFG to reduce seams, ghosting, and back-view errors in mesh texture synthesis.
- [Open-Vocabulary Functional 3D Scene Graphs for Real-World Indoor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19199): Predicts 3D scene graphs that include objects, interactive elements, and functional relations by injecting VLM and LLM functional knowledge.
- [DeClotH: Decomposable 3D Cloth and Human Body Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19373): Separates cloth and body reconstruction from one image using cloth and human templates plus a cloth-specific diffusion prior.
- [COB-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19443): Splits ambiguous Gaussians using semantic boundary gradients while jointly optimizing semantic masks and visual texture to sharpen 3DGS object boundaries.
- [GaussianUDF: Inferring Unsigned Distance Functions through 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19458): GaussianUDF uses 3D Gaussian Splatting to realize inferring Unsigned Distance Functions.
- [MM-LINS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19506): Detects LiDAR-inertial degeneracy, stores sleeping maps, reinitializes active maps, and fuses overlapping maps through scan-context constraints.
- [PartRM: Modeling Part-Level Dynamics with Large Cross-State Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19913): Fine-tunes large 3D Gaussian reconstruction models on PartDrag-4D with drag embeddings and two-stage motion-then-appearance learning for part dynamics.
- [EVolSplat: Efficient Volume-based Gaussian Splatting for Urban View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20168): EVolSplat targets urban View Synthesis with efficient Volume-based Gaussian Splatting.
- [Synthetic-to-Real Self-supervised Robust Depth Estimation via Learning with Motion and Structure Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20211): Transfers motion-structure priors from synthetic adverse weather to real data using cost-volume knowledge and consistency-reweighted pseudo-labels.
- [HierRelTriple: Guiding Indoor Layout Generation with Hierarchical Relationship Triplet Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20289): Injects object-region, object-object, and corner-corner geometric triplets into diffusion denoising for indoor layouts with fewer collisions.
- [ARMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20663): Uses OmniRig mesh-skeleton-skinning data and an autoregressive graph-token model with latent diffusion to predict joints and connectivity.
- [PhysGen3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20746): Estimates object shapes, poses, physical properties, and lighting from one image, then simulates controllable physics-grounded 3D scenes.
- [VoxRep: Enhancing 3D Spatial Understanding in 2D Vision-Language Models via Voxel Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21214): Slices voxel grids into ordered 2D images so a standard VLM can infer object identity, color, and location semantics from 3D space.
- [GenFusion: Closing the Loop between Reconstruction and Generation via Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21219): Uses a reconstruction-driven video diffusion model and cyclical restoration-frame fusion to expand sparse 3D reconstruction into more complete generated views.
- [Frequency-Aware Gaussian Splatting Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21226): Decomposes 3D Gaussians into Laplacian-pyramid frequency bands with residual colors, enabling level-of-detail streaming and foveated rendering.
- [RainyGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21442): Combines 3DGS rendering with physics-based raindrop and shallow-water simulation to synthesize controllable real-time rain effects in open scenes.
- [SparseFlex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21732): Uses a sparse Flexicubes-style isosurface representation and frustum-aware voxel training for high-resolution differentiable mesh reconstruction and generation.
- [Biomechanically Accurate Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21751): Trains a transformer to estimate biomechanically constrained skeleton parameters from one image using iteratively refined pseudo labels.
- [$\mathbf{X}^{\mathbf{2}}$-Gaussian: 4D Radiative Gaussian Splatting for Continuous-Time Tomographic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21779): Models continuous-time 4D CT as dynamic radiative Gaussian splats and learns patient-specific breathing cycles without external gating.
- [Stream and Query-guided Feature Aggregation for Efficient and Effective 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22087): Keeps dense voxel fidelity while aggregating temporal streams and injecting query features for dynamic-object-aware real-time 3D occupancy prediction.
- [ABC-GS: Alignment-Based Controllable Style Transfer for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22218): Aligns content and style features through segmentation masks, feature-alignment style loss, depth loss, and Gaussian regularization for controllable 3DGS stylization.
- [AH-GS: Augmented 3D Gaussian Splatting for High-Frequency Detail Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22324): Enriches Scaffold-GS features and feature-map losses so structurally complex regions learn higher-frequency scene detail.
- [MVSAnywhere: Zero-Shot Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22430): Combines monocular and multi-view cues with adaptive cost volumes to estimate zero-shot depth across domains and unknown depth ranges.
- [TranSplat: Lighting-Consistent Cross-Scene Object Transfer with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22676): Relights Gaussian surfel objects by analytically transferring spherical-harmonic appearance from source to target environment maps without GS retraining.
- [DSO: Aligning 3D Generators with Simulation Feedback for Physical Soundness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22677): DSO adds simulation Feedback for Physical Soundness to aligning 3D Generators.
- [FreeSplat++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22986): Uses low-cost cross-view aggregation, triplet fusion, floater removal, and depth-regularized fine-tuning for whole-scene feed-forward 3DGS reconstruction.
- [CityGS-$\mathcal{X}$: A Scalable Architecture for Efficient and Geometrically Accurate Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23044): CityGS-$\mathcal{X}$ targets efficient and Geometrically Accurate Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction with a Scalable Architecture.
- [NeuralGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23162): Clusters Gaussians and fits their attributes with tiny MLP neural fields, shrinking 3DGS storage by about 91 times.
- [ReasonGrounder: LVLM-Guided Hierarchical Feature Splatting for Open-Vocabulary 3D Visual Grounding and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23297): Uses LVLM-interpreted instructions, hierarchical Gaussian feature fields, SAM masks, and multi-view CLIP embeddings for occlusion-aware 3D grounding.
- [HiPART](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23331): Generates dense hierarchical 2D poses from sparse skeletons with autoregressive tokens before 2D-to-3D lifting, improving occluded 3D human pose estimation.
- [Spatial Condition-Based 3DGS Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23337): Predicts anchor-based Gaussian attributes from grid-captured spatial conditions and compresses residuals with an instance-aware hyperprior.
- [Gaussian Blending Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23625): Identifies Gaussian blending as an edge-GPU bottleneck and proposes intra-row shading plus a dedicated rendering engine for AR and VR.
- [Uni-Render](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23644): Uses a reconfigurable accelerator that switches neural-rendering dataflows across pipelines to support real-time on-device rendering.
- [DiET-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24210): Combines event double-integral constraints with a diffusion prior in two-stage 3DGS training to recover sharp novel views from motion-blurred inputs.
- [StochasticSplats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24366): Replaces sorted alpha compositing with an unbiased Monte Carlo volume-rendering estimator, trading samples for quality without depth sorting.
- [Easi3R: Estimating Disentangled Motion from DUSt3R Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24391): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for spatial reasoning and spatial intelligence. Core idea: Recent advances in DUSt3R have enabled robust estimation of dense point clouds and camera parameters of static scenes, leveraging Transformer network architectures and direct supervision on large-scale 3D datasets.
- [SonarSplat: Novel View Synthesis of Imaging Sonar via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00159): Represents sonar scenes as acoustic-reflectance and saturation Gaussians and rasterizes range-azimuth images with azimuth streak modeling.
- [LITA-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00219): Extracts illumination-invariant physical priors, renders light-agnostic structure, and progressively denoises reference-free 3DGS under adverse lighting.
- [Scene4U: Hierarchical Layered 3D Scene Reconstruction from Single Panoramic Image for Your Immerse Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00387): Decomposes a panorama into semantic layers with open-vocabulary segmentation and an LLM, repairs occlusions, then optimizes layered 3DGS for free exploration.
- [Coca-Splat: Collaborative Optimization for Camera Parameters and 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00639): Jointly optimizes camera parameters and 3D Gaussians with deformable Transformer queries, camera-aware cross-attention, and ray constraints for pose-free sparse views.
- [DropGaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00773): Randomly removes Gaussians during sparse-view 3DGS training so remaining primitives receive stronger gradients and reduce overfitting.
- [Improved Visual-Spatial Reasoning via R1-Zero-Like Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00883): Trains Qwen2-VL with GRPO on VSI-100k, keeping a KL penalty, to activate video-based visual-spatial reasoning in smaller MLLMs.
- [Explorable INR: An Implicit Neural Representation for Ensemble Simulation Enabling Efficient Spatial and Parameter Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00904): Uses implicit neural representations, probabilistic affine uncertainty propagation, and gradient-based parameter search for point-query ensemble simulation exploration.
- [SuperDec: 3D Scene Decomposition with Superquadric Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00992): Decomposes objects into compact superquadric sets and scales to scenes through instance segmentation for robotic and editable-generation uses.
- [Luminance-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01503): adapts 3D Gaussian Splatting to challenging lighting with view-adaptive curve adjustment.
- [High-fidelity 3D Object Generation from Single Image with RGBN-Volume Gaussian Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01512): Introduces GS-RGBN, a structured RGB-normal volume Gaussian reconstruction model that reduces single-image 3D generation artifacts from multiview diffusion ambiguity.
- [FlowR: Flowing from Sparse to Dense 3D Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01647): Trains a multiview flow-matching model that maps renderings from sparse 3D reconstructions toward dense-capture renderings, producing consistent generated views that improve Gaussian-splatting novel-view synthesis.
- [VideoScene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01956): Distills video diffusion priors into one-step 3D scene generation, connecting video generation with spatial scene construction.
- [Refining CLIP's Spatial Awareness: A Visual-Centric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02328): Improves CLIP spatial awareness from a visual-centric perspective to reduce relation-insensitivity.
- [Taylor Point-Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02454): uses Taylor-series-inspired local structure fitting for few-shot point-cloud semantic segmentation.
- [GEOPARD: Geometric Pretraining for Articulation Prediction in 3D Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02747): Uses geometric pretraining for 3D articulation prediction, strengthening model-side understanding of movable shapes.
- [MD-ProjTex: Texturing 3D Shapes with Multi-Diffusion Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02762): MD-ProjTex adds multi-Diffusion Projection to texturing 3D Shapes.
- [Scene Splatter: Momentum 3D Scene Generation from Single Image with Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02764): Uses video diffusion priors for momentum-based 3D scene generation from a single image, linking image-conditioned generation with spatial scene synthesis.
- [Octree Adaptive Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02817): Uses octree-based adaptive tokenization to make autoregressive 3D shape generation more efficient.
- [Detection Based Part-level Articulated Object Reconstruction from Single RGBD Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03177): Reconstructs part-level articulated objects from a single RGB-D image, extending spatial perception toward movable object structure.
- [HumanDreamer-X: Photorealistic Single-image Human Avatars Reconstruction via Gaussian Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03536): Unifies multiview human generation and 3D Gaussian reconstruction so single-image avatars keep consistent limbs, geometry, and texture.
- [WildGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03886): performs monocular Gaussian-splatting SLAM in dynamic environments.
- [View2CAD: Reconstructing View-Centric CAD Models from Single RGB-D Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04000): Introduces a view-centric B-rep representation with visibility limits and uncertainty so CAD geometry can be reconstructed from a single RGB-D observation without hallucinating hidden surfaces.
- [On the Spatial Structure of Mixture-of-Experts in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04444): Analyzes how transformer mixture-of-experts layers form spatial structure, using expert organization as the model-side object of study.
- [PanoDreamer: Consistent Text to 360-Degree Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05152): Uses an LLM-guided warp-refine pipeline to compose 360-degree panoramas, lift them to point clouds, and generate coherent text- or image-conditioned 3D scenes.
- [InteractVLM: 3D Interaction Reasoning from 2D Foundational Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05303): Fine-tunes VLMs to infer 3D human-object contact from single in-the-wild images, strengthening interaction-aware spatial reasoning.
- [View-Dependent Deformation Fields for 2D Editing of 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05544): Lifts user-specified 2D view edits into smooth view-dependent deformation fields for meshes or 3D Gaussian splats, enabling layered non-realistic 3D object authoring across viewpoints.
- [QEMesh: Employing A Quadric Error Metrics-Based Representation for Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05720): Extends PoNQ quadric-error surface patches into a latent diffusion mesh generator with multi-decoder VAE heads for normals, QEM matrices, and occupancy.
- [Micro-splatting: Multistage Isotropy-informed Covariance Regularization Optimization for High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05740): Reduces 3DGS model size with trace-based covariance regularization and gradient-guided densification that keeps high-frequency regions detailed while leaving smooth areas sparse.
- [HiMoR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06210): uses hierarchical motion representations for monocular deformable Gaussian reconstruction.
- [PromptHMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06397): makes human mesh recovery promptable for more controllable human 3D reconstruction.
- [MonoPlace3D: Learning 3D-Aware Object Placement for 3D Monocular Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06801): Learns scene-aware synthetic object placement, including position, size, and orientation, to improve monocular 3D detector augmentation.
- [IAAO: Interactive Affordance Learning for Articulated Objects in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06827): Learns interactive affordances for articulated objects in 3D environments, connecting spatial object structure with action possibilities.
- [HoloPart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07943): Generates 3D part-level amodal segmentations, strengthening part-aware spatial representation and generation.
- [Detect Anything 3D in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07958): Targets open-world 3D detection, strengthening model-native spatial localization in unconstrained 3D scenes.
- [ChildlikeSHAPES: Semantic Hierarchical Region Parsing for Animating Figure Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08022): ChildlikeSHAPES targets animating Figure Drawings with semantic Hierarchical Region Parsing.
- [FMLGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08581): builds fast multilevel language-embedded Gaussians for part-level interactive spatial agents.
- [MASH: Masked Anchored SpHerical Distances for 3D Shape Representation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09149): Represents shapes as local spherical-distance functions anchored on surface patches, enabling differentiable point-cloud conversion and 3D generative modeling.
- [Text To 3D Object Generation For Scalable Room Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09328): Builds text-conditioned 3D indoor assets by adapting text-to-image, multiview diffusion, and NeRF meshing, then inserts them into floor-plan scenes for synthetic data generation.
- [DropoutGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09491): uses random dropout regularization to improve sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting rendering.
- [TextSplat: Text-Guided Semantic Fusion for Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09588): Adds text-guided semantic fusion to feed-forward Gaussian Splatting so sparse-view reconstructions align geometry with fine-grained semantic cues.
- [OctGPT: Octree-based Multiscale Autoregressive Models for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09975): Builds octree-based multiscale autoregressive models for 3D shape generation, adding a structured 3D generation architecture.
- [EBAD-Gaussian: Event-driven Bundle Adjusted Deblur Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10012): Jointly reconstructs sharp 3D Gaussians and exposure-time camera trajectories from event streams plus severely motion-blurred RGB images.
- [LL-Gaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10331): uses Gaussian splatting for low-light scene reconstruction and enhancement in novel-view synthesis.
- [Art3D: Training-Free 3D Generation from Flat-Colored Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10466): Art3D reconstructs or generates training-free 3d generation from flat-colored illustration.
- [NormalCrafter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11427): Uses video diffusion priors to estimate temporally consistent surface normals, adding a geometry-focused spatial perception method.
- [DM-OSVP++: One-Shot View Planning Using 3D Diffusion Models for Active RGB-Based Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11674): Adds one-shot view planning with 3D diffusion models for active object reconstruction.
- [CoMotion 3D Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12186): models concurrent multi-person 3D motion, extending human spatial-motion generation.
- [One Model to Rig Them All: Diverse Skeleton Rigging with UniRig](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12451): Unifies diverse skeleton rigging, supporting controllable 3D objects and avatars for spatial generation.
- [CM3AE: A Unified RGB Frame and Event-Voxel/-Frame Pre-training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12576): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [ARAP-GS: Drag-driven As-Rigid-As-Possible 3D Gaussian Splatting Editing with Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12788): Applies as-rigid-as-possible deformation directly to 3D Gaussians and uses a diffusion super-resolution prior during optimization to preserve multi-view appearance after drag edits.
- [TSGS Transparent Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12799): adds normal and de-lighting priors to Gaussian splatting for transparent surface reconstruction.
- [CAGE-GS: High-fidelity Cage Based 3D Gaussian Splatting Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12800): Learns deformation cages for 3DGS scenes and updates Gaussian covariances with Jacobian-based transformations to preserve texture during user-shaped deformation.
- [AAA-Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12811): Adds anti-aliasing and artifact suppression to 3D Gaussian rendering, improving visual fidelity of spatial scene representations.
- [Rethinking Temporal Fusion with a Unified Gradient Descent View for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12959): Rethinking Temporal Fusion with a Unified Gradient Descent adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [HiScene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13072): Generates hierarchical 3D scenes from isometric views, decomposing rooms into editable object-level structure.
- [ST4RTrack: Simultaneous 4D Reconstruction and Tracking in the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13152): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for spatial reasoning and spatial intelligence. Core idea: Dynamic 3D reconstruction and point tracking in videos are typically treated as separate tasks, despite their deep connection.
- [AerialMegaDepth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13157): learns aerial-ground reconstruction and view synthesis from pseudo-synthetic city-scale data plus real ground imagery.
- [EDGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13204): eliminates iterative densification in 3D Gaussian Splatting with dense correspondence-based initialization.
- [Compile Scene Graphs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13617): Trains multimodal LLMs with supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to output structured scene graphs instead of unconstrained text.
- [HoLa B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14257): generates boundary-representation geometry with a holistic latent representation.
- [Seurat: From Moving Points to Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14687): Infers monocular video depth from tracked moving points, adding a spatial-modeling entry for motion-derived geometry.
- [TAPIP3D: Tracking Any Point in Persistent 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14717): TAPIP3D contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [MoBGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15122): applies dynamic 3D Gaussian splatting to motion-deblur and reconstruct blurry monocular videos.
- [Instance-Adaptive Keypoint Learning with Local-to-Global Geometric Aggregation for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15134): combines its base model with Local-to-Global Geometric Aggregation for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation to improve instance-adaptive keypoint learning.
- [FaceCraft4D: Animated 3D Facial Avatar Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15179): Builds a single-image 4D facial avatar pipeline with 3D-GAN shape initialization, depth-guided multiview texture warping, video priors, and consistency-aware 4D training.
- [DRAWER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15278): Reconstructs and articulates objects with environmental realism, extending interactive spatial asset modeling.
- [PointLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16023): adapts point-cloud models through low-rank adaptation with token selection.
- [HUG: Hierarchical Urban Gaussian Splatting with Block-Based Reconstruction for Large-Scale Aerial Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16606): HUG adds block-Based Reconstruction for Large-Scale Aerial Scenes to hierarchical Urban Gaussian Splatting.
- [Physics-based super-resolved simulation of 3D elastic wave propagation adopting scalable diffusion transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17308): uses diffusion-based generation or distillation to control geometry, views, placement, or 3D content synthesis.
- [Gaussian Meets Surfel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17545): combines Gaussian and surfel representations for ultra-fast high-fidelity radiance-field rendering.
- [Casual3DHDR: High Dynamic Range 3D Gaussian Splatting from Casually Captured Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17728): Casual3DHDR targets high Dynamic Range 3D Gaussian Splatting from casually Captured Videos.
- [iVR-GS: Inverse Volume Rendering for Explorable Visualization via Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17954): iVR-GS uses editable 3D Gaussian Splatting for inverse Volume Rendering for Explorable Visualization.
- [Unify3D: An Augmented Holistic End-to-end Monocular 3D Human Reconstruction via Anatomy Shaping and Twins Negotiating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18215): Reconstructs clothed humans end-to-end from one RGB image using anatomy-shaped implicit features and a twin U-Net negotiation module, supported by comic-style augmentation and 3D scan data.
- [LaRI: Layered Ray Intersections for Single-view 3D Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18424): Uses layered ray intersections for single-view 3D geometric reasoning, strengthening spatial inference from sparse visual evidence.
- [Vysics: Object Reconstruction Under Occlusion by Fusing Vision and Contact-Rich Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18719): Fuses RGB-D reconstruction, robot proprioception, and contact-inferred "physible" geometry to recover rigid objects under heavy occlusion.
- [4DGS-CC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18925): Compresses 4D Gaussian splatting data through contextual coding over temporal and spatial quantized features.
- [Sketch2Anim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19189): transfers sketch storyboards into 3D animation.
- [Point2Quad: Generating Quad Meshes From Point Clouds via Face Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19545): Point2Quad uses face Prediction for generating Quad Meshes From Point Clouds.
- [STCOcc: Sparse Spatial-Temporal Cascade Renovation for 3D Occupancy and Scene Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19749): Uses occupied-state-guided sparse attention, cascade refinement, and long-term temporal modeling for 3D occupancy and scene-flow prediction.
- [SpatialReasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20024): A 3D spatial-reasoning method that trains or structures models to reason explicitly over 3D spatial relations.
- [LIRM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20026): Builds a large inverse-rendering model for shape, material, and view-dependent radiance reconstruction, extending spatial scene-representation models.
- [Sparse2DGS: Geometry-Prioritized Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20378): Initializes 2D Gaussian Splatting with learned multiview-stereo points and geometry-prioritized enhancement to reconstruct surfaces from sparse views.
- [GSFeatLoc: Visual Localization Using Feature Correspondence on 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20379): GSFeatLoc uses feature Correspondence on 3D Gaussian Splatting for visual Localization.
- [LMM4Gen3DHF: Benchmarking and Evaluating Multimodal 3D Human Face Generation with LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20466): LMM4Gen3DHF combines its base model with LMMs to improve benchmarking and evaluating multimodal 3d human face generation.
- [GauSS-MI: Gaussian Splatting Shannon Mutual Information for Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21067): Uses Gaussian-splatting Shannon mutual information to guide active 3D reconstruction and viewpoint selection.
- [GarmentDiffusion: 3D Garment Sewing Pattern Generation with Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21476): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, verifier, synthetic-data, or generation coverage.
- [Compensating Spatiotemporally Inconsistent Observations for Online Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01235): Improves online dynamic 3DGS by learning and subtracting observation errors that cause temporal inconsistency, raising both rendering quality and stability for live video reconstruction.
- [GENMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01425): builds a generalist model for human motion.
- [Learning Multi-frame and Monocular Prior for Estimating Geometry in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01737): Proposes MMP, a feed-forward model that predicts dynamic pointmaps over multiple monocular video frames without costly test-time global optimization.
- [Co3Gesture: Towards Coherent Concurrent Co-speech 3D Gesture Generation with Interactive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01746): Builds a concurrent two-person co-speech 3D gesture dataset and an interactive diffusion framework for coherent conversational gesture generation.
- [Discrete Spatial Diffusion: Intensity-Preserving Diffusion Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01917): Models discrete spatial scientific fields with a continuous-time jump process that preserves particle counts during both forward and reverse diffusion.
- [HybridGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01938): compresses 3D Gaussian splatting with dual-channel sparse representation and a point-cloud encoder.
- [SparSplat: Fast Multi-View Reconstruction with Generalizable 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02175): Extends generalizable sparse-view reconstruction with MVS-based 2D Gaussian parameter regression for fast multiview geometry and novel-view synthesis.
- [Real-time Spatial Retrieval Augmented Generation for Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02271): develops real-time spatial retrieval augmented generation for urban environments.
- [MetaScenes: Towards Automated Replica Creation for Real-world 3D Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02388): Automates replica creation from real-world 3D scans, extending spatial reconstruction toward reusable digital-scene assets.
- [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Discrete-Time Gaussian Process Mixtures for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03296): develops the unreasonable effectiveness of discrete-time gaussian process mixtures for robot policy learning.
- [Position: Foundation Models Need Digital Twin Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03798): Classification concern: a position paper arguing that foundation models need digital-twin representations to encode spatial-temporal dynamics and causal physical structure.
- [CAD-Llama: Leveraging Large Language Models for Computer-Aided Design Parametric 3D Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04481): Uses LLMs to generate parametric CAD models, connecting natural language with structured 3D design.
- [PrimitiveAnything: Human-Crafted 3D Primitive Assembly Generation with Auto-Regressive transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04622): Generates human-crafted 3D primitive assemblies with an autoregressive transformer, adding compositional structure to 3D asset generation.
- [WIR3D: Visually-Informed and Geometry-Aware 3D Shape Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04813): WIR3D contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Generating Physically Stable and Buildable Brick Structures from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05469): Generates physically stable, buildable brick structures from text, adding physical constraints to language-conditioned 3D generation.
- [DiffusionSfM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05473): predicts structure and motion using ray-origin and endpoint diffusion.
- [Steepest Descent Density Control for 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05587): controls Gaussian density through steepest descent to make 3D Gaussian splatting more compact.
- [DiffLocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06166): Generates 3D hair from a single image with diffusion models, adding a fine-grained human-asset line to spatial generation.
- [Virtualized 3D Gaussians: Flexible Cluster-based Level-of-Detail System for Real-Time Rendering of Composed Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06523): Creates a Nanite-like level-of-detail system for composed 3DGS scenes by building hierarchical Gaussian clusters offline and selecting perceptible clusters online for real-time rendering.
- [CMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07003): Uses controllable multiview diffusion for part-wise 3D editing and progressive generation from image conditions.
- [GIFStream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07539): builds 4D Gaussian immersive video with feature streams, extending dynamic spatial representations.
- [ADC-GS: Anchor-Driven Deformable and Compressed Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08196): Compresses dynamic 3DGS with canonical-space anchors, temporal-significance refinement, coarse-to-fine deformation, and rate-distortion optimization instead of per-Gaussian motion fields.
- [ACT-R: Adaptive Camera Trajectories for Single View 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08239): ACT-R adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [FOCI: Trajectory Optimization on Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08510): Optimizes robot trajectories directly over Gaussian-splat representations with a Field Overlap Collision Integral instead of conservative bounding-box collision checks.
- [VRSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10144): Adapts Gaussian splatting for fast and robust virtual-reality rendering, addressing view-consistency and latency in immersive spatial scenes.
- [SpatialCrafter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11992): Uses video diffusion priors for scene reconstruction from limited observations, connecting generative video models with spatial world construction.
- [Spatial-LLaVA: Enhancing Large Language Models with Spatial Referring Expressions for Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12194): Spatial-LLaVA combines its base model with Spatial Referring Expressions for Visual Understanding to improve enhancing large language models.
- [LLaVA-4D: Embedding SpatioTemporal Prompt into LMMs for 4D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12253): Embeds spatiotemporal prompts into large multimodal models for 4D scene understanding, extending spatial modeling beyond static 3D scenes.
- [Visuospatial Cognitive Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12312): makes spatial or geometric structure explicit in the model objective, representation, or evaluation target.
- [TACOcc:Target-Adaptive Cross-Modal Fusion with Volume Rendering for 3D Semantic Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12693): Aligns camera and LiDAR features for 3D semantic occupancy with target-scale adaptive bidirectional retrieval, then uses 3DGS volume rendering supervision to recover surface detail.
- [MVAR: Visual Autoregressive Modeling with Scale and Spatial Markovian Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12742): Reduces visual autoregressive generation cost by imposing scale and spatial Markov assumptions so tokens condition on local multiscale neighborhoods.
- [UniHM: Universal Human Motion Generation with Object Interactions in Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12774): UniHM adds object Interactions in Indoor Scenes to universal Human Motion Generation.
- [Hybrid 3D-4D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Dynamic Scene Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13215): Represents static regions with 3D Gaussians and dynamic elements with 4D Gaussians to lower memory and compute in dynamic scene rendering.
- [Surrogate Modeling of 3D Rayleigh-Benard Convection with Equivariant Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13569): Combines a G-steerable equivariant convolutional autoencoder with an equivariant ConvLSTM surrogate for 3D Rayleigh-Benard convection fields.
- [Building spatial world models from sparse transitional episodic memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13696): targets building spatial world models from sparse transitional episodic memories.
- [MGStream: Motion-aware 3D Gaussian for Streamable Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13839): Separates motion-related Gaussians from static Gaussians and applies rigid deformation plus motion masks for streamable dynamic scene reconstruction.
- [MATHDance: Mamba-Transformer Architecture with Uniform Tokenization for High-Quality 3D Dance Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14222): Generates music-conditioned 3D dance with uniform motion tokenization and a hybrid Mamba-Transformer architecture for choreographic consistency.
- [Sparc3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14521): Uses sparse representations for high-resolution 3D shape modeling, extending scalable spatial asset generation.
- [MonoSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from Monocular Depth Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15185): Adapts monocular depth foundation-model features into multiview representations for generalizable Gaussian reconstruction on novel scenes.
- [R3GS: Gaussian Splatting for Robust Reconstruction and Relocalization in Unconstrained Image Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15294): Uses CNN and hash-grid anchor features plus transient-object visibility maps to reconstruct and relocalize with unconstrained image collections.
- [Mesh-RFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16761): Applies fine-grained reinforcement fine-tuning to mesh generation, adding an RL-based route for controllable 3D asset synthesis.
