- [Temporal Differential Fields for 4D Motion Modeling via Image-to-Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17333): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [Reflectance Prediction-Based Knowledge Distillation for Robust 3D Object Detection in Compressed Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17442): targets robust 3D Object Detection in Compressed Point Clouds with reflectance Prediction-Based Knowledge Distillation.
- [CGS-GAN: 3D Consistent Gaussian Splatting GANs for High Resolution Human Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17590): Stabilizes Gaussian-splatting GAN training for human heads while avoiding camera-conditioned identity drift across generated viewpoints.
- [Seek-CAD: A Self-refined Generative Modeling for 3D Parametric CAD Using Local Inference via DeepSeek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17702): Seek-CAD uses deepSeek for a Self-refined Generative Modeling for 3D Parametric CAD Using Local Inference.
- [GRE Suite: Geo-localization Inference via Fine-Tuned Vision-Language Models and Enhanced Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18700): Builds geolocalization reasoning data, a VLM reasoning model, and GREval for fine-grained visual and contextual location inference.
- [VPGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18992): uses voxel-based progressive 3D Gaussian SLAM for large-scale scenes.
- [Triangle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19175): uses triangle splatting for real-time radiance-field rendering, adding an efficient spatial representation route.
- [Improving Novel view synthesis of 360$^\circ$ Scenes in Extremely Sparse Views by Jointly Training Hemisphere Sampled Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19264): Uses DUSt3R poses and point clouds to synthesize hemisphere-sampled 360-degree training views, reducing sparse-view 3DGS overfitting for four-view novel-view synthesis.
- [Absolute Coordinates Make Motion Generation Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19377): Revisits global absolute joint coordinates for text-to-motion diffusion, simplifying motion representation while improving controllable spatial motion generation.
- [ParticleGS: Particle-Based Dynamics Modeling of 3D Gaussians for Prior-free Motion Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20270): ParticleGS targets prior-free Motion Extrapolation with particle-Based Dynamics Modeling of 3D Gaussians.
- [VLM-3R: Vision-Language Models Augmented with Instruction-Aligned 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20279): Augments VLMs with instruction-aligned 3D reconstruction for spatial understanding.
- [Category-Agnostic Neural Object Rigging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20283): learns category-agnostic rigging for animation-ready 3D objects.
- [DIPO: Dual-State Images Controlled Articulated Object Generation Powered by Diverse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20460): Uses paired dual-state images to control articulated-object generation, adding a data-driven route for movable 3D assets.
- [CityGo: Lightweight Urban Modeling and Rendering with Proxy Buildings and Residual Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21041): Combines textured proxy buildings with residual Gaussians to model large aerial urban scenes efficiently for edge-friendly rendering.
- [Uni3D-MoE: Scalable Multimodal 3D Scene Understanding via Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21079): Uni3D-MoE uses mixture of Experts for scalable Multimodal 3D Scene Understanding.
- [3D Question Answering via only 2D Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22143): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [UP-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22335): uses adaptively structured Gaussian SLAM with uncertainty prediction in dynamic environments.
- [CLIPGaussian: Universal and Multimodal Style Transfer Based on Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22854): Optimizes color and geometry directly on Gaussian primitives for text- or image-guided style transfer across images, videos, 3D objects, and 4D scenes without retraining a large generator.
- [Hierarchical Sparse Transform Coding for 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22908): compresses 3D Gaussian splatting with hierarchical sparse transform coding.
- [Pose-free 3D Gaussian splatting via shape-ray estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22978): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [SpatialSplat: Efficient Semantic 3D from Sparse Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23044): SpatialSplat reconstructs or generates efficient semantic 3d from sparse unposed images.
- [Zero-P-to-3: Zero-Shot Partial-View Images to 3D Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23054): Tackles 3D reconstruction from partial-view image arcs by generating coherent unseen views beyond the observed angular range.
- [Diffusion-Based Generative Models for 3D Occupancy Prediction in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23115): Recasts visual 3D occupancy prediction as diffusion-based generative modeling to improve robustness to noise, occlusion, and complex driving geometry.
- [LODGE: Level-of-Detail Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting with Efficient Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23158): LODGE adds efficient Rendering to level-of-Detail Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting.
- [UniTEX: Universal High Fidelity Generative Texturing for 3D Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23253): Replaces UV-only texture inpainting with continuous 3D texture functions predicted by a transformer Large Texturing Model, then adapts DiT priors with LoRA for high-fidelity multiview texture synthesis.
- [Holistic Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction via Mixed Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23280): Optimizes large scenes without divide-and-conquer partitioning by decoding camera pose and Gaussian attributes from a holistic view-aware representation.
- [Radiant Triangle Soup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23642): adds soft connectivity forces for 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis.
- [Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23678): Grounds reinforcement learning for visual reasoning in spatial evidence and attention.
- [Spatial-MLLM: Boosting MLLM Capabilities in Visual-based Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23747): Boosts multimodal large language models for visual spatial intelligence.
- [Rooms from Motion: Un-posed Indoor 3D Object Detection as Localization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23756): Rooms from Motion targets pose estimation with geometry-aware features, keypoints, or spatial-shape constraints.
- [NUC-Net: Non-Uniform Cylindrical Partition Network for Efficient LiDAR Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24634): NUC-Net targets efficient LiDAR Semantic Segmentation with non-Uniform Cylindrical Partition Network.
- [Tackling View-Dependent Semantics in 3D Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24746): modifies Gaussian-splatting representation, optimization, or rendering to improve spatial reconstruction quality or efficiency.
- [TC-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24796): accelerates Gaussian splatting with tensor cores, improving real-time spatial rendering efficiency.
- [AdaHuman: Animatable Detailed 3D Human Generation with Compositional Multiview Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24877): Generates animatable single-image human avatars with pose-conditioned 3D joint diffusion and compositional 3DGS refinement for local body detail.
- [Probabilistic Spatial Interpolation of Sparse Data using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00033): Uses diffusion models to fill sparse, uncertain geophysical observations while representing distributions over plausible spatial initial conditions.
- [Diff-SPORT: Diffusion-based Sensor Placement Optimization and Reconstruction of Turbulent flows in urban environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00214): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, verifier, synthetic-data, or generation coverage.
- [Adaptive Voxelization for 3DGS Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00271): uses adaptive voxelization for transform coding of 3D Gaussian splatting data.
- [Pro3D-Editor : A Progressive-Views Perspective for Consistent and Precise 3D Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00512): Edits 3D content through progressive views to improve consistency and precision.
- [SatDreamer360: Multiview-Consistent Generation of Ground-Level Scenes from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00600): SatDreamer360 targets multiview-Consistent Generation of Ground-Level Scenes from satellite Imagery.
- [GeoChain: Multimodal Chain-of-Thought for Geographic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00785): GeoChain targets geographic Reasoning with multimodal Chain-of-Thought.
- [GSCodec Studio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01822): Provides a modular framework for compressing 3D and 4D Gaussian splats, supporting practical spatial scene representation.
- [MoDA: Modulation Adapter for Fine-Grained Visual Grounding in Instructional MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01850): MoDA targets fine-Grained Visual Grounding in Instructional MLLMs with modulation Adapter.
- [RobustSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02751): decouples densification and dynamics for transient-free 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [PBR-SR: Mesh PBR Texture Super Resolution from 2D Image Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02846): PBR-SR targets mesh PBR Texture Super Resolution from 2D Image Priors.
- [Self-Supervised Spatial Correspondence Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03148): Learns RGB-depth and cross-modal pixel correspondences through cycle-consistent contrastive random walks without aligned multimodal supervision.
- [Multi-Spectral Gaussian Splatting with Neural Color Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03407): Models thermal, near-infrared, and RGB views in one 3DGS framework using neural color representations without cross-modal camera calibration.
- [Rex-Thinker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04034): Uses chain-of-thought reasoning for grounded object referring, connecting visual grounding with explicit reasoning.
- [FlexGS: Train Once, Deploy Everywhere with Many-in-One Flexible 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04174): FlexGS adds many-in-One Flexible 3D Gaussian Splatting to train Once, Deploy Everywhere.
- [Handle-based Mesh Deformation Guided By Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04562): uses vision Language Model to realize handle-based Mesh Deformation Guided.
- [OpenMaskDINO3D : Reasoning 3D Segmentation via Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04837): OpenMaskDINO3D uses large Language Model for reasoning 3D Segmentation.
- [Video World Models with Long-term Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05284): Introduces Video World Models with Long-term Spatial Memory, a model-side contribution for Spatial.
- [FreeTimeGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05348): introduces free Gaussian primitives for anytime dynamic scene reconstruction.
- [Layered Motion Fusion: Lifting Motion Segmentation to 3D in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05546): contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [On-the-fly Reconstruction for Unposed NVS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05558): reconstructs large-scale scenes on the fly for novel-view synthesis from unposed images.
- [BecomingLit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06271): Builds relightable Gaussian avatars with hybrid neural shading, extending controllable 3D human representation generation.
- [Vid2Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06440): Reconstructs appearance, geometry, and physics from video for mesh-free simulation, linking spatial perception with simulation-ready world modeling.
- [Basis Transformers for Multi-Task Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06926): targets multi-Task Tabular Regression with basis Transformers.
- [Drive Any Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07489): Uses 4D latent diffusion to animate meshes from monocular video, connecting video motion priors with editable spatial assets.
- [Squeeze3D: Your 3D Generation Model is Secretly an Extreme Neural Compressor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07932): Uses pretrained 3D generative priors as extreme neural compressors by mapping encoded meshes, point clouds, or radiance fields into compact latent codes.
- [UA-Pose: Uncertainty-Aware 6D Object Pose Estimation and Online Object Completion with Partial References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07996): Estimates 6D pose from partial RGB-D or single-image references while completing uncertain object geometry online.
- [Dynamic View Synthesis as an Inverse Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08004): Performs training-free dynamic view synthesis by rewriting video-diffusion noise initialization with K-order recursive noise and visibility-aware stochastic latent modulation.
- [iTACO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08334): Reconstructs interactable digital twins of articulated objects from casual RGB-D video, extending spatial assets toward action-ready object models.
- [STREAMINGGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09070): combines voxel-based streaming, memory optimization, and architectural support for 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [UFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09278): unifies dense image correspondence and optical flow with a simple transformer-based flow-regression model.
- [UniForward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09378): Reconstructs 3D scenes and semantic fields through feed-forward Gaussian splatting from uncalibrated sparse-view images.
- [Gaussian Herding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09534): frames global Gaussian reduction for 3DGS from an optimal-transport perspective.
- [SemanticSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09565): Predicts language-aware Gaussian fields from sparse views for feed-forward 3D scene reconstruction and semantic understanding.
- [PD$^{2}$GS: Part-Level Decoupling and Continuous Deformation of Articulated Objects via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09663): Decouples articulated-object parts and deformation with Gaussian splatting, adding a fine-grained dynamic 3D representation.
- [Accurate and efficient zero-shot 6D pose estimation with frozen foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09784): Introduces FreeZeV2, a training-free 6D pose estimator that reuses frozen foundation-model features for novel RGB-D objects.
- [Hearing Hands: Generating Sounds from Physical Interactions in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09989): Hearing Hands targets generating Sounds from physical Interactions in 3D Scenes.
- [DGS-LRM: Real-Time Deformable 3D Gaussian Reconstruction From Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09997): DGS-LRM reconstructs or generates real-time deformable 3d gaussian reconstruction from monocular videos.
- [DySS: Dynamic Queries and State-Space Learning for Efficient 3D Object Detection from Multi-Camera Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10242): Uses a state-space model over multi-camera video features plus dynamic query merge, remove, and split operations for efficient BEV 3D object detection.
- [Perceptual-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12400): adds scene-adaptive perceptual densification to Gaussian splatting.
- [Efficient multi-view training for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12727): Adapts 3DGS to multi-view mini-batch training with lower-overhead rasterization, 3D distance-aware D-SSIM, and multi-view adaptive density control.
- [Metropolis-Hastings Sampling for 3D Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12945): Reformulates Gaussian densification and pruning as Metropolis-Hastings sampling driven by multiview photometric errors and opacity scores.
- [WildCAT3D: Appearance-Aware Multi-View Diffusion in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13030): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [GS-2DGS: Geometrically Supervised 2DGS for Reflective Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13110): Adds geometric supervision to 2D Gaussian splatting for reflective-object reconstruction, improving spatial reconstruction under view-dependent appearance.
- [MT-PCR: Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Network with Spatial Serialization for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13183): Serializes irregular point clouds and combines Mamba with Transformer layers to register high-resolution clouds with lower sequence cost.
- [Micro-macro Gaussian Splatting with Enhanced Scalability for Unconstrained Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13516): Decomposes unconstrained scene Gaussians into global, refined, and intrinsic components with micro-macro projection and wavelet sampling.
- [Dive3D: Diverse Distillation-based Text-to-3D Generation via Score Implicit Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13594): Dive3D uses score Implicit Matching for diverse Distillation-based Text-to-3D Generation.
- [Test3R: Learning to Reconstruct 3D at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13750): Applies self-supervised test-time learning to 3D reconstruction, improving cross-pair geometric consistency from image triplets with little adaptation overhead.
- [Particle-Grid Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15680): Learns deformable object models from RGB-D videos, adding a dynamics-aware spatial modeling route.
- [Co-VisiON: Co-Visibility ReasONing on Sparse Image Sets of Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16805): Co-VisiON is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [Scene-R1: Video-Grounded Large Language Models for 3D Scene Reasoning without 3D Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17545): Scene-R1 targets 3D Scene Reasoning without 3D Annotations with video-Grounded Large Language Models.
- [Relation3D: Enhancing Relation Modeling for Point Cloud Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17891): Adds a model entry for Spatial, because it enhances point-cloud relation modeling for 3D instance segmentation.
- [Auto-Regressive Surface Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18017): Models surface cutting autoregressively, adding a structured mesh-construction primitive for 3D generation.
- [2D Triangle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18575): supports direct differentiable mesh training through 2D triangle splatting.
- [MCN-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18678): combines collaborative neural SLAM with hybrid implicit neural scene representations.
- [GRAND-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18885): uses local optimization to make large-scale multi-agent Gaussian SLAM globally consistent.
- [PrITTI: Primitive-based Generation of Controllable and Editable 3D Semantic Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19117): PrITTI adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [HoliGS: Holistic Gaussian Splatting for Embodied View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19291): Uses holistic deformable Gaussian splatting for embodied view synthesis from long monocular videos, separating static background and time-varying articulated objects.
- [DreamAnywhere: Object-Centric Panoramic 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20367): Generates object-centric panoramic 3D scenes, extending spatial generation toward controllable full-scene synthesis.
- [EditP23: 3D Editing via Propagation of Image Prompts to Multi-View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20652): Propagates a user-edited image prompt through a pretrained multiview diffusion latent flow for mask-free, feed-forward, 3D-consistent editing.
- [PhysRig](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20936): Replaces simple linear blend skinning with differentiable physics-based rigging for more realistic articulated-object deformation.
- [Geometry and Perception Guided Gaussians for Multiview-consistent 3D Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21152): Combines geometric and perceptual guidance in a training-free Gaussian reconstruction pipeline for single-image, multiview-consistent 3D objects.
- [GroundFlow: A Plug-in Module for Temporal Reasoning on 3D Point Cloud Sequential Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21188): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [Curve-Aware Gaussian Splatting for 3D Parametric Curve Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21401): Optimizes 3D parametric curves directly from multiview edge maps by pairing curve geometry with a differentiable Gaussian rendering representation.
- [DeOcc-1-to-3: 3D De-Occlusion from a Single Image via Self-Supervised Multi-View Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21544): Performs single-image 3D de-occlusion with self-supervised multi-view diffusion, improving spatial reconstruction under missing evidence.
- [Fine-Grained Preference Optimization for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21656): Uses fine-grained spatial preferences to improve VLM judgments of relations, positions, and 3D layouts.
- [PhotonSplat: 3D Scene Reconstruction and Colorization from SPAD Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21680): Reconstructs and colorizes scenes from noisy SPAD binary images using 3D Gaussian splatting, spatial filtering, generative or reference colorization, and a SPAD multiview dataset.
- [RoomCraft: Controllable and Complete 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22291): Generates controllable and complete 3D indoor scenes, extending language- or condition-guided spatial scene synthesis.
- [STD-GS: Exploring Frame-Event Interaction for SpatioTemporal-Disentangled Gaussian Splatting to Reconstruct High-Dynamic Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23157): STD-GS targets spatioTemporal-Disentangled Gaussian Splatting to Reconstruct High-Dynamic Scene with exploring Frame-Event Interaction.
- [Endo-4DGX: Robust Endoscopic Scene Reconstruction and Illumination Correction with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23308): Adds illumination-adaptive 4D Gaussian Splatting to reconstruct dynamic endoscopic scenes under low light and over-exposure.
- [WAVE: Warp-Based View Guidance for Consistent Novel View Synthesis Using a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23518): WAVE adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [Puzzles: Unbounded Video-Depth Augmentation for Scalable End-to-End 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23863): Synthesizes unbounded posed video-depth augmentation from a single image or clip to scale training data for end-to-end 3D reconstruction.
- [GaVS: 3D-Grounded Video Stabilization via Temporally-Consistent Local Reconstruction and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23957): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [MILo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.24096): adds mesh-in-the-loop constraints to Gaussian splatting for detailed and efficient surface reconstruction.
- [LLaVA-SP: Enhancing Visual Representation with Visual Spatial Tokens for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00505): Adds six spatial visual tokens and a convolutional projector to strengthen local patch relationships in MLLM visual representations.
- [LOD-GS: Level-of-Detail-Sensitive 3D Gaussian Splatting for Detail Conserved Anti-Aliasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00554): Dynamically predicts filtering strength for each Gaussian primitive so anti-aliasing preserves detail instead of over-smoothing.
- [A LoD of Gaussians: Unified Training and Rendering for Ultra-Large Scale Reconstruction with External Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01110): A LoD of Gaussians adds external Memory to unified Training and Rendering for Ultra-Large Scale Reconstruction.
- [PLOT: Pseudo-Labeling via Video Object Tracking for Scalable Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02393): Uses video object tracking to aggregate pseudo-LiDAR evidence across adjacent frames, generating scalable pseudo labels for monocular 3D object detection without multi-view setups, extra sensors, camera poses, or domain-specific training.
- [SIU3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02705): jointly performs scene understanding and 3D reconstruction beyond simple feature alignment.
- [LiteReality: Graphics-Ready 3D Scene Reconstruction from RGB-D Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02861): Reconstructs graphics-ready 3D scenes from RGB-D scans, connecting spatial reconstruction with renderable scene assets.
- [Point3R: Streaming 3D Reconstruction with Explicit Spatial Pointer Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02863): Adds explicit spatial pointer memory for streaming 3D reconstruction, relevant to persistent spatial representation.
- [S3PO-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03737): uses global scale-consistent 3D Gaussian pointmaps for outdoor monocular SLAM.
- [Height-Fidelity Dense Global Fusion for Multi-Modal 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04369): Preserves LiDAR height information during camera-LiDAR fusion and feeds height-aware features into Hybrid Mamba blocks for efficient multimodal 3D object detection.
- [Sat2City: 3D City Generation from a Single Satellite Image with Cascaded Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04403): Generates 3D city structures from a single satellite image through cascaded latent diffusion.
- [PRISM: Pointcloud Reintegrated Inference via Segmentation and Cross-Attention for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04633): Learns manipulation policies directly from raw point clouds and robot states using segmentation embeddings and cross-attention.
- [InterGSEdit: Interactive 3D Gaussian Splatting Editing with 3D Geometry-Consistent Attention Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04961): Lets users choose key views, selects semantically consistent reference views with CLIP, unprojects cross-attention maps into a 3D geometry-consistent attention prior, and fuses 3D-constrained and 2D diffusion attention for consistent 3DGS edits.
- [MoDiT: Learning Highly Consistent 3D Motion Coefficients with Diffusion Transformer for Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05092): MoDiT adds diffusion Transformer for Talking Head Generation to learning Highly Consistent 3D Motion Coefficients.
- [SPADE: Spatial-Aware Denoising Network for Open-Vocabulary Panoptic Scene Graph Generation with Long- and Local-Range Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05798): Uses denoising-style spatial structure preservation plus long- and local-range context reasoning for open-vocabulary panoptic scene graphs.
- [High-Fidelity and Generalizable Neural Surface Reconstruction with Sparse Feature Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05952): Predicts sparse voxel occupancies and feature volumes from posed images, enabling higher-resolution generalizable neural surface reconstruction.
- [OmniPart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06165): Generates part-aware 3D assets through semantic decoupling and structural cohesion, adding structured spatial generation coverage.
- [3D-Generalist: Self-Improving Vision-Language-Action Models for Crafting 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06484): 3D-Generalist develops self-improving vision-language-action models for crafting 3d worlds.
- [MK-Pose: Category-Level Object Pose Estimation via Multimodal-Based Keypoint Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06662): Fuses RGB images, point clouds, and category text through self-supervised keypoint learning for category-level 6D pose estimation.
- [A Neural Representation Framework with LLM-Driven Spatial Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06719): adds lLM-Driven Spatial Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary 3D Visual Grounding to a Neural Representation Framework.
- [LangSplatV2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07136): Builds high-dimensional 3D language Gaussian splatting at high frame rates, linking language-aligned 3D scenes with efficient spatial representation.
- [Occlusion-Aware Temporally Consistent Amodal Completion for 3D Human-Object Interaction Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08137): targets 3D Human-Object Interaction Reconstruction with occlusion-Aware Temporally Consistent Amodal Completion.
- [From One to More: Contextual Part Latents for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08772): Adds contextual part latents for 3D generation, strengthening part-aware spatial asset synthesis beyond whole-object generation.
- [PoseLLM: Enhancing Language-Guided Human Pose Estimation with MLP Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09139): Replaces linear vision-language projection with an MLP connector so LLM-guided pose estimation captures richer spatial-textual keypoint relations.
- [OpenHuman4D: Open-Vocabulary 4D Human Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09880): OpenHuman4D contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [ScaffoldAvatar: High-Fidelity Gaussian Avatars with Patch Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10542): ScaffoldAvatar adds patch Expressions to high-Fidelity Gaussian Avatars.
- [Spatial Reasoners for Continuous Variables in Any Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10768): targets continuous Variables in Any Domain with spatial Reasoners.
- [HPR3D: Hierarchical Proxy Representation for High-Fidelity 3D Reconstruction and Controllable Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11971): Represents 3D objects with sparse hierarchical proxy nodes that store local shape and texture features, enabling compact reconstruction, neural interpolation, and direct drag editing.
- [PhysX-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12465): Generates physically grounded 3D assets, extending spatial generation toward assets with usable physical properties.
- [Wavelet-GS: 3D Gaussian Splatting with Wavelet Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12498): Wavelet-GS adds wavelet Decomposition to 3D Gaussian Splatting.
- [MindJourney: Test-Time Scaling with World Models for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12508): Uses world models for test-time scaling in spatial reasoning, expanding candidate spatial rollouts before selecting answers.
- [Diffuman4D: 4D Consistent Human View Synthesis From Sparse-View Videos With Spatio-Temporal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13344): Diffuman4D adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [AutoPartGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13346): Generates and discovers 3D parts autoregressively, adding a part-structured route for 3D asset generation.
- [Leveraging the Spatial Hierarchy: Coarse-to-fine Trajectory Generation via Cascaded Hybrid Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13366): Synthesizes human mobility trajectories with a coarse-to-fine cascaded hybrid diffusion model that follows spatial hierarchy in urban movement data.
- [PCR-GS: COLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting via Pose Co-Regularizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13891): PCR-GS uses pose Co-Regularizations for cOLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting.
- [Adaptive 3D Gaussian Splatting Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14432): adapts 3D Gaussian splatting representations for video streaming.
- [Adaptive 3DGS Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14454): uses saliency-aware tiling and meta-learning-based bitrate adaptation for streaming 3D Gaussian splats.
- [Stereo-GS: Multi-View Stereo Vision Model for Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14921): Builds a generalizable multi-view stereo model for 3D Gaussian splatting reconstruction.
- [ObjectGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15454): makes Gaussian splatting object-aware for scene reconstruction and understanding.
- [HOLa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15542): Adapts VLM features with low-rank decomposition for zero-shot human-object interaction detection, adding a spatial-relation model line beyond 3D-only grounding.
- [SurfaceSplat: Connecting Surface Reconstruction and Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15602): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [Dens3R: A Foundation Model for 3D Geometry Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16290): Introduces a foundation model for 3D geometry prediction.
- [VGGT-Long](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16443): extends VGGT-style reconstruction to kilometer-scale long RGB sequences through chunking, looping, and alignment.
- [Spatial 3D-LLM : Exploring Spatial Awareness in 3D Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16524): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [STQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17522): enhances spatial-temporal attributes for G-PCC compressed dynamic point clouds.
- [Perspective-Invariant 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17665): Introduces cross-platform LiDAR adaptation for vehicle, quadruped, and drone 3D object detection with perspective-invariant representations.
- [Ultra3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17745): Improves efficient high-fidelity 3D generation through part attention, extending structured spatial asset generation.
- [LMM-Det](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18300): Adapts large multimodal models for object detection, strengthening model-native localization and spatial grounding.
- [SaLF: Sparse Local Fields for Multi-Sensor Rendering in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18713): Builds sparse local fields for real-time rendering of cameras and LiDAR, decoupling scene representation from pinhole-only 3DGS assumptions.
- [Neural Shell Texture Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20200): preserves more detail with fewer primitives for efficient 3D surface texture representation.
- [BANG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21493): Divides 3D assets through generative exploded dynamics, adding a structured editing method for spatial assets.
- [SLTarch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21499): makes point-based neural rendering more scalable by addressing workload imbalance and memory irregularity.
- [PanoSplatt3R: Leveraging Perspective Pretraining for Generalized Unposed Wide-Baseline Panorama Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21960): PanoSplatt3R adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [Ov3R: Open-Vocabulary Semantic 3D Reconstruction from RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22052): Ov3R adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [DepR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22825): Uses depth-guided instance-level diffusion for compositional single-view scene reconstruction.
- [LIVE-GS: Online LiDAR-Inertial-Visual State Estimation and Globally Consistent Mapping with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23273): Integrates LiDAR, inertial, and visual state estimation with 3DGS mapping to improve global consistency in texture-poor or long-range scenes.
- [iLRM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23277): Scales feed-forward 3D reconstruction through iterative refinement, reducing attention bottlenecks for large 3D reconstruction models.
- [3D-R1: Enhancing Reasoning in 3D VLMs for Unified Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23478): Builds Scene-30K synthetic 3D chain-of-thought data, then applies GRPO-style RLHF with perception, semantic-similarity, and format rewards plus dynamic view selection to strengthen 3D VLM scene reasoning.
- [XSpecMesh: Quality-Preserving Auto-Regressive Mesh Generation Acceleration via Multi-Head Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23777): Applies speculative decoding to accelerate autoregressive mesh generation while preserving quality.
- [Gaussian Variation Field Diffusion for High-Fidelity Video-to-4D Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23785): Synthesizes high-fidelity 4D scenes from video through Gaussian variation-field diffusion, extending spatial generation to time-varying 4D content.
- [PointGauss: Point Cloud-Guided Multi-Object Segmentation for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00259): PointGauss targets gaussian Splatting with point Cloud-Guided Multi-Object Segmentation.
- [Occlusion-Robust Stylization for Drawing-Based 3D Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00398): targets drawing-Based 3D Animation with occlusion-Robust Stylization.
- [Sel3DCraft: Interactive Visual Prompts for User-Friendly Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00428): Turns text-to-3D prompting into an interactive visual workflow with retrieval-generation candidates, multiview MLLM scoring, and visual analytics for defect-driven prompt refinement.
- [DPoser-X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00599): Uses a diffusion prior for robust 3D whole-body human pose modeling and inverse pose tasks.
- [DreamSat-2.0: Towards a General Single-View Asteroid 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01079): Classification concern: benchmarks Hunyuan-3D, Trellis-3D, and Ouroboros-3D on spacecraft and asteroid single-view reconstruction rather than introducing a new model.
- [DELTAv2: Accelerating Dense 3D Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01170): DELTAv2 adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [SPFSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01171): predicts 3D Gaussians and camera poses from sparse unposed views in a self-supervised pose-free pipeline.
- [MeshLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01242): Enables LLMs to progressively understand and generate 3D meshes, directly connecting language models with structured spatial assets.
- [DisCo3D: Distilling Multi-View Consistency for 3D Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01684): Distills multiview consistency constraints for 3D scene editing, reducing slow iterative refinement and cross-view artifacts.
- [VDEGaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02129): enhances 4D Gaussian splatting for dynamic urban-scene modeling with video diffusion priors.
- [Unified Category-Level Object Detection and Pose Estimation from RGB Images Using 3D Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02157): Uses neural mesh prototypes with learned features and multi-model RANSAC to combine RGB object detection and category-level 3D pose estimation.
- [PRIMU: Uncertainty Estimation for Novel Views in Gaussian Splatting from Primitive-Based Representations of Error and Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02443): PRIMU targets uncertainty Estimation for Novel Views in Gaussian Splatting from primitive-Based Representations of Error and Coverage.
- [Uni3R: Unified 3D Reconstruction and Semantic Understanding via Generalizable Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03643): Unifies 3D reconstruction and semantic understanding from unposed multi-view images with generalizable Gaussian splatting.
- [La La LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03691): Generates large-scale layouts from LiDAR data, extending spatial scene generation to sensor-grounded outdoor structure.
- [One Model for All: Unified Try-On and Try-Off in Any Pose via LLM-Inspired Bidirectional Tweedie Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04559): One Model for All uses lLM-Inspired Bidirectional Tweedie Diffusion for unified Try-On and Try-Off in Any Pose.
- [Occupancy Learning with Spatiotemporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04705): adds spatiotemporal Memory to occupancy Learning.
- [MagicHOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05506): leverages 3D priors for hand-object reconstruction from short monocular videos.
- [GMF-Drive: Gated Mamba Fusion with Spatial-Aware BEV Representation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06113): GMF-Drive combines its base model with Spatial-Aware BEV Representation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving to improve gated mamba fusion.
- [Fading the Digital Ink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07263): Attacks 3DGS watermarking systems in a universal black-box setting, exposing provenance risks for neural 3D content.
- [DIP-GS: Deep Image Prior For Gaussian Splatting Sparse View Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07372): Injects a deep-image-prior representation into 3DGS so coarse-to-fine optimization can recover sparse-view scenes without pretrained depth or generative models.
- [Dream4D: Lifting Camera-Controlled I2V towards Spatiotemporally Consistent 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07769): Dream4D adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Mem4D: Decoupling Static and Dynamic Memory for Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07908): Mem4D targets dynamic Scene Reconstruction with decoupling Static and Dynamic Memory.
- [CD-TVD: Contrastive Diffusion for 3D Super-Resolution with Scarce High-Resolution Time-Varying Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08173): Combines contrastive pretraining with diffusion super-resolution to recover 3D time-varying scientific simulation fields from scarce high-resolution samples.
- [ReferSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08252): performs referring segmentation directly in 3D Gaussian splatting scenes.
- [GaussianUpdate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08867): updates 3D Gaussian splatting scenes continually as environments change.
- [Learning Spatial Decay for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09525): Adds content-aware spatial decay to ViT self-attention through a Context-Aware Gating mechanism for spatially structured vision tasks.
- [TRACE: Learning 3D Gaussian Physical Dynamics from Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09811): Models dynamic 3D Gaussian points as rigid particles with learned translation-rotation dynamics and physical parameters, enabling future-frame extrapolation and part clustering from video.
- [PERSONA: Personalized Whole-Body 3D Avatar with Pose-Driven Deformations from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09973): Generates pose-rich videos from a single human image, then optimizes a personalized 3D avatar with balanced sampling and geometry-weighted losses to preserve identity under pose-driven deformation.
- [EntropyGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10227): designs efficient entropy coding for 3D Gaussian splatting, extending spatial representation compression.
- [Multi-Sample Anti-Aliasing and Constrained Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10507): Integrates adaptive multisample anti-aliasing with under-reconstruction and gradient-differential constraints to sharpen 3DGS details.
- [AddressVLM: Cross-view Alignment Tuning for Image Address Localization using Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10667): Aligns street-view and satellite-view cues through image grafting and automatic labels so LVLMs can answer fine-grained address-localization questions.
- [Chem3DLLM: 3D Multimodal Large Language Models for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10696): Builds a protein-conditioned multimodal LLM with reversible 3D molecular tokenization so text, proteins, ligands, and conformations share one generation space.
- [Puppeteer: Rig and Animate Your 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10898): Rigs and animates 3D models, connecting static spatial asset generation with controllable motion-ready representations.
- [Vision-Only Gaussian Splatting for Collaborative Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10936): Shares sparse semantic Gaussian primitives across connected vehicles, fusing object-centric geometry and semantics for vision-only collaborative 3D occupancy prediction.
- [ComplicitSplat: Downstream Models are Vulnerable to Blackbox Attacks by 3D Gaussian Splat Camouflages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11854): ComplicitSplat uses 3D Gaussian Splat Camouflages to improve downstream models are vulnerable to blackbox attacks.
- [4DNeX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13154): contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [InnerGS: Internal Scenes Rendering via Factorized 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13287): InnerGS uses factorized 3D Gaussian Splatting for internal Scenes Rendering.
- [MeshCoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14879): Trains a multimodal LLM to convert point clouds into executable Blender programs, making 3D shapes editable through structured code.
- [GaussianArt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14891): Unifies geometry and motion modeling for articulated objects, extending spatial assets toward manipulable dynamic structure.
- [Hybrelighter: Combining Deep Anisotropic Diffusion and Scene Reconstruction for On-device Real-time Relighting in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14930): Hybrelighter targets on-device Real-time Relighting in Mixed Reality with combining Deep Anisotropic Diffusion and Scene Reconstruction.
- [Collaborative Multi-Modal Coding for High-Quality 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15228): Uses collaborative multimodal coding to improve 3D generation quality, fitting spatial-generation model coverage.
- [Deep Equilibrium Convolutional Sparse Coding for Hyperspectral Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15553): targets hyperspectral Image Denoising with deep Equilibrium Convolutional Sparse Coding.
- [HAMSt3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16433): adds human-aware reasoning to multi-view stereo 3D reconstruction.
- [Towards Open-Vocabulary Multimodal 3D Object Detection with Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16812): adds attributes to towards Open-Vocabulary Multimodal 3D Object Detection.
- [Random-phase Wave Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17480): uses translucent primitives and random-phase wave splatting for computer-generated holography.
- [TinyGiantVLM: A Lightweight Vision-Language Architecture for Spatial Reasoning under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17595): Uses RGB-depth global and regional encoders plus MoE fusion to perform lightweight VLM spatial reasoning in warehouse-scale logistics scenes.
- [ObjFiller-3D: Consistent Multi-view 3D Inpainting via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18271): Optimizes dense 360-degree view sequences with video-diffusion components so 3D object inpainting stays coherent across viewpoints.
- [Efficient Implicit Neural Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19140): collects efficiency tricks for implicit neural point-cloud representations.
- [SAT: Supervisor Regularization and Animation Augmentation for Two-process Monocular Texture 3D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19688): SAT targets two-process Monocular Texture 3D Human Reconstruction with supervisor Regularization and Animation Augmentation.
- [Global Motion Corresponder for 3D Point-Based Scene Interpolation Under Large Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20136): targets 3D Point-Based Scene Interpolation Under Large Motion with global Motion Corresponder.
- [ARGS: Advanced Regularization on Aligning Gaussians over the Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21344): Improves SuGaR-style mesh and visual reconstruction with rank regularization against needle-like Gaussians and surface-coherence regularization.
- [Adaptive Point-Prompt Tuning: Fine-Tuning Heterogeneous Foundation Models for 3D Point Cloud Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00374): Adds adaptive point-prompt tuning for 3D point-cloud foundation models for the Spatial Understanding Model section.
- [On the Influence of Shape, Texture and Color for Learning Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00765): targets learning Semantic Segmentation with on the Influence of Shape, Texture and Color.
- [Towards Integrating Multi-Spectral Imaging with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00989): Studies RGB and multispectral 3DGS optimization strategies and recommends joint spectral-band modeling inside Gaussian spherical-harmonics color components for consistent geometry.
- [3DGS Point-Cloud Compression and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02232): compresses and communicates 3D Gaussian splatting point clouds efficiently.
- [Category-Aware 3D Object Composition with Disentangled Texture and Shape Multi-view Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02357): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [Tera: Rethinking Text-Guided Realistic 3D Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02466): Tera adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [ContraGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03775): compresses Gaussian-splatting reconstruction with trainable codebooks, adding an efficient spatial-reconstruction model line.
- [SSGaussian: Semantic-Aware and Structure-Preserving 3D Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04379): SSGaussian contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [PropVG: End-To-End Proposal-Driven Visual Grounding with Multi-Granularity Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04833): Reintroduces proposal generation into end-to-end visual grounding and adds multi-granularity discrimination for robust referred-object localization.
- [UniView: Enhancing Novel View Synthesis From A Single Image By Unifying Reference Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04932): Retrieves similar-object references with an MLLM and injects them through an adapter to reduce ambiguity in single-image novel-view synthesis.
- [WinT3R: Window-Based Streaming Reconstruction with Camera Token Pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05296): WinT3R adds camera Token Pool to window-Based Streaming Reconstruction.
- [DVLO4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06023): uses sparse spatio-temporal fusion for deep visual-LiDAR odometry, extending spatial localization and mapping models.
- [P3-SAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06784): Extends segment-anything-style part segmentation to native 3D assets, adding a model-side spatial parsing primitive.
- [MEGS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07021): improves memory-efficient Gaussian splatting through spherical Gaussians and unified pruning.
- [AEOS: Active Environment-aware Optimal Scanning Control for UAV LiDAR-Inertial Odometry in Complex Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09141): Combines model-predictive control and reinforcement learning for active UAV LiDAR scanning, improving odometry and mapping under occlusion.
- [HD-MoE: Hybrid and Dynamic Parallelism for Mixture-of-Expert LLMs with 3D Near-Memory Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09420): HD-MoE combines its base model with 3D Near-Memory Processing to improve hybrid and dynamic parallelism for mixture-of-expert llms.
- [Chameleon: Taming Dynamic Operator Sequences for Memory-Intensive LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11076): Chameleon targets memory-Intensive LLM Training with taming Dynamic Operator Sequences.
- [WildSmoke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11114): reconstructs dynamic 3D smoke assets from a single in-the-wild video, extending spatial generation to fluid-like time-varying content.
- [SVR-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11116): uses spatially variant regularization for probabilistic masks in 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [OpenVDB 3D Gaussian Ray Marching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11377): builds 3D Gaussian modeling and ray marching for OpenVDB scientific-visualization datasets.
- [MemGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13536): makes Gaussian-splatting SLAM more memory-efficient for real-time spatial mapping.
- [DSpAST: Disentangled Representations for Spatial Audio Reasoning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13927): Learns disentangled spatial-audio embeddings for event type, direction, and distance with a lightweight SpatialAST extension used by LLM-based spatial-audio reasoning systems.
- [Plug-and-Play PDE Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting: Toward High-Quality Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13938): Plug-and-Play PDE Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [GenCAD-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15246): Generates CAD programs through multimodal latent-space alignment and synthetic dataset balancing, linking spatial reasoning with structured 3D design generation.
- [LaySPA / LLMs as Layout Designers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16891): Uses reinforcement learning and hybrid spatial rewards to teach LLM-based agents content-aware graphic layout generation.
- [SemanticGarment: Semantic-Controlled Generation and Editing of 3D Gaussian Garments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16960): SemanticGarment modifies Gaussian-splatting representation, optimization, or rendering to improve spatial reconstruction quality or efficiency.
- [HyRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17083): combines hybrid radiance fields for memory-efficient, high-quality novel-view synthesis.
- [PMRT: A Training Recipe for Fast, 3D High-Resolution Aerodynamic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17182): PMRT targets fast, 3D High-Resolution Aerodynamic Prediction with a Training Recipe.
- [Point-RTD: Replaced Token Denoising for Pretraining Transformer Models on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17207): Point-RTD targets pretraining Transformer Models on Point Clouds with replaced Token Denoising.
- [4D-MoDe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17506): uses motion-decoupled 4D Gaussian compression for editable and scalable volumetric streaming.
- [4DGCPro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17513): provides hierarchical 4D Gaussian compression for progressive volumetric video streaming.
- [VideoArtGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17647): Builds articulated-object digital twins from monocular video, connecting video observations with actionable 3D spatial representations.
- [Evict3R: Training-Free Token Eviction for Memory-Bounded Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17650): Evict3R targets memory-Bounded Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers with training-Free Token Eviction.
- [ProDyG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17864): reconstructs dynamic scenes from monocular videos with progressive Gaussian splatting.
- [Differentiable Light Transport with Gaussian Surfels via Adapted Radiosity for Efficient Relighting and Geometry Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18497): Uses Gaussian surfels and adapted radiosity in spherical-harmonics space to make differentiable global illumination efficient for relighting and geometry reconstruction.
- [DeblurSplat: SfM-free 3D Gaussian Splatting with Event Camera for Robust Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18898): DeblurSplat adds event Camera for Robust Deblurring to sfM-free 3D Gaussian Splatting.
- [Lyra: Generative 3D Scene Reconstruction via Video Diffusion Model Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19296): Lyra uses video Diffusion Model Self-Distillation for generative 3D Scene Reconstruction.
- [PolGS: Polarimetric Gaussian Splatting for Fast Reflective Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19726): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [SeHDR: Single-Exposure HDR Novel View Synthesis Via 3D Gaussian Bracketing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20400): Builds HDR 3DGS from single-exposure LDR multiview images by estimating bracketed Gaussians with shared geometry and fusing them through differentiable neural exposure fusion.
- [ARMesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20824): Generates meshes autoregressively through next-level-of-detail prediction, improving structured 3D asset synthesis.
- [Hunyuan3D-Omni](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21245): Unifies controllable 3D asset generation, extending spatial model coverage for text- and condition-driven 3D synthesis.
- [MusicWeaver: Composer-Style Structural Editing and Minute-Scale Coherent Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21714): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it targets composer-style structural editing and long-form music generation rather than 3D geometry or spatial reasoning.
- [SRHand: Super-Resolving Hand Images and 3D Shapes via View/Pose-aware Neural Image Representations and Explicit 3D Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21859): SRHand uses view/Pose-aware Neural Image Representations and Explicit 3D Meshes for super-Resolving Hand Images and 3D Shapes.
- [PartSAM: A Scalable Promptable Part Segmentation Model Trained on Native 3D Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21965): Builds a promptable part-segmentation model on native 3D data, adding a reusable 3D parsing primitive for spatial models.
- [Large Material Gaussian Model for Relightable 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22112): Extends large Gaussian reconstruction models to generate PBR material attributes, including albedo, roughness, and metallic maps, for relightable 3D assets.
- [Rigidity-Aware 3D Gaussian Deformation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22222): Guides single-image 3D Gaussian deformation with Gaussian-to-pixel matching and rigid-part segmentation so sparse 2D cues produce coherent object motion.
- [ARSS: Taming Decoder-only Autoregressive Visual Generation for View Synthesis From Single View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23008): ARSS develops taming decoder-only autoregressive visual generation for view synthesis from single view.
- [Hermes: A Multi-Scale Spatial-Temporal Hypergraph Network for Stock Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23668): Classification concern: models inter-industry lead-lag and multiscale stock correlations with spatial-temporal hypergraphs, which is temporal-finance forecasting rather than 3D spatial modeling.
- [RPG360: Robust 360 Depth Estimation with Perspective Foundation Models and Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23991): Combines perspective foundation models and graph optimization for robust 360-degree depth estimation.
- [UniLat3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25079): Unifies geometry and appearance latents for single-stage 3D generation, adding a compact representation route for spatial asset synthesis.
- [DepthLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25413): estimates metric depth from vision-language models.
- [SAGE: Spatial-visual Adaptive Graph Exploration for Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25723): SAGE develops spatial-visual adaptive graph exploration for visual place recognition.
- [GaussEdit: Adaptive 3D Scene Editing With Text and Image Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26055): Edits 3DGS scenes with text and image prompts using ROI selection, adaptive global-local optimization, category regularization, and image-to-image texture refinement.
- [3Diface: Synthesizing and Editing Holistic 3D Facial Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26233): Generates and edits holistic speech-driven 3D facial animation with a fully convolutional diffusion model, speaking-style personalization, and sparsely guided motion diffusion.
- [TTT3R: 3D Reconstruction as Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26645): TTT3R uses test-time training to adapt the reconstruction state while streaming or processing new observations.
- [Has the Two-Decade-Old Prophecy Come True? Artificial Bad Intelligence Triggered by Merely a Single-Bit Flip in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00490): Off-axis for this spatial model page; it studies single-bit-flip failures in LLMs rather than a spatial architecture, world model, or 3D training signal.
- [UniVerse Radiance Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01669): reuses scene priors from video diffusion models for robust radiance-field reconstruction.
- [ROGR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03163): Applies generative relighting to relightable 3D objects, extending controllable spatial asset editing.
- [Spatial-ViLT: Enhancing Visual Spatial Reasoning through Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03441): Spatial-ViLT uses multi-Task Learning to realize enhancing Visual Spatial Reasoning.
- [Joint Learning of Pose Regression and Denoising Diffusion with Score Scaling Sampling for Category-Level 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04125): combines its base model with Score Scaling Sampling for Category-Level 6D Pose Estimation to improve joint learning of pose regression and denoising diffusion.
- [PAINET: A Principled Efficient Transformer for 3D Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04233): PAINET targets 3D Dynamics Modeling with a Principled Efficient Transformer.
- [DoRAN: Stabilizing Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation via Noise Injection and Auxiliary Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04331): DoRAN uses noise Injection and Auxiliary Networks for stabilizing Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation.
- [OKVIS2-X: Open Keyframe-Based Visual-Inertial SLAM Configurable With Dense Depth or LiDAR, and GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04612): OKVIS2-X adds dense Depth or LiDAR, and GNSS to open Keyframe-Based Visual-Inertial SLAM Configurable.
- [HoloScene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05560): Builds simulation-ready interactive 3D worlds from a single video, linking spatial reconstruction with interactive world modeling.
- [UniFField: A Generalizable Unified Neural Feature Field for Visual, Semantic, and Spatial Uncertainties in Any Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06754): Builds a generalizable uncertainty-aware neural feature field that combines visual, semantic, and geometric features for robotic scene reasoning.
- [Splat the Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08491): represents radiance fields with splattable neural primitives.
- [SpatialLadder: Progressive Training for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08531): Uses progressive training to improve spatial reasoning in vision-language models.
- [ARTDECO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08551): targets efficient and high-fidelity on-the-fly 3D reconstruction with structured scene representations.
- [Mono4DEditor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09438): edits 4D scenes from monocular video with language-embedded Gaussians, linking text control to dynamic spatial scene editing.
- [LTGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09881): builds long-term Gaussian scene chronology from sparse view updates.
- [Color3D: Controllable and Consistent 3D Colorization with Personalized Colorizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10152): Color3D adds personalized Colorizer to controllable and Consistent 3D Colorization.
- [WorldMirror: Universal 3D World Reconstruction with Any-Prior Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10726): Reconstructs universal 3D worlds with any-prior prompting, extending spatial modeling across heterogeneous cues.
- [REACT3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11340): Recovers articulations for interactive physical 3D scenes, connecting reconstruction with action-ready scene understanding.
- [MaterialRefGS: Reflective Gaussian Splatting with Multi-view Consistent Material Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11387): Enforces multiview-consistent material maps and physically based environment modeling for reflective Gaussian-splatting novel-view synthesis.
- [ACE-G: Improving Generalization of Scene Coordinate Regression Through Query Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11605): ACE-G uses query Pre-Training to realize improving Generalization of Scene Coordinate Regression.
- [NV3D: Leveraging Spatial Shape Through Normal Vector-based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11632): NV3D uses normal Vector-based 3D Object Detection to realize leveraging Spatial Shape.
- [PhySIC: Physically Plausible 3D Human-Scene Interaction and Contact from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11649): Reconstructs metrically consistent humans, scenes, and contact maps from a single image, improving physically plausible 3D human-scene interaction modeling.
- [Prompt-Guided Spatial Understanding with RGB-D Transformers for Fine-Grained Object Relation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11996): combines its base model with RGB-D Transformers for Fine-Grained Object Relation Reasoning to improve prompt-guided spatial understanding.
- [AngularFuse: A Closer Look at Angle-based Perception for Spatial-Sensitive Multi-Modality Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12260): AngularFuse targets spatial-Sensitive Multi-Modality Image Fusion with a Closer Look at Angle-based Perception.
- [Scene Coordinate Reconstruction Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12387): Reinterprets scene-coordinate regression probabilistically and injects reconstruction priors, including point-cloud diffusion priors, to prevent degenerate scene geometry.
- [Uncertainty-Aware Dynamic 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12768): Models uncertainty in dynamic Gaussian splatting for monocular 4D reconstruction, improving temporal 3D reconstruction reliability.
- [Kinematic Kitbashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13048): Composes kinematic structures for 3D assets, adding a procedural route for controllable articulated-object generation.
- [CymbaDiff: Structured Spatial Diffusion for Sketch-based 3D Semantic Urban Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13245): CymbaDiff develops structured spatial diffusion for sketch-based 3d semantic urban scene generation.
- [Spatial Preference Rewarding for MLLMs Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14374): develops spatial preference rewarding for mllms spatial understanding.
- [SaLon3R: Structure-aware Long-term Generalizable 3D Reconstruction from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15072): Uses compact anchor primitives and saliency-aware Gaussian quantization for long-term, online generalizable 3D reconstruction from unposed image streams.
- [Neuro-Symbolic Spatial Reasoning in Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15841): makes spatial or geometric structure explicit in the model objective, representation, or evaluation target.
- [Skyfall-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15869): Synthesizes immersive 3D urban scenes from satellite imagery, adding a city-scale spatial generation route grounded in overhead observations.
- [MNO: Multiscale Neural Operator for 3D Computational Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16071): Solves 3D CFD on unstructured point clouds with global attention, local graph attention, and micro point-wise attention modules.
- [Where, Not What: Compelling Video LLMs to Learn Geometric Causality for 3D-Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17034): Trains Video LLMs toward geometric causality for 3D grounding, shifting spatial understanding from object identity toward where-and-why relations.
- [M2H: Multi-Task Learning with Efficient Window-Based Cross-Task Attention for Monocular Spatial Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17363): M2H adds efficient Window-Based Cross-Task Attention for Monocular Spatial Perception to multi-Task Learning.
- [PAGE-4D: Disentangled Pose and Geometry Estimation for 4D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17568): PAGE-4D targets 4D Perception with disentangled Pose and Geometry Estimation.
- [Re-Activating Frozen Primitives for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19653): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [OmniMotion-X: Versatile Multimodal Whole-Body Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19789): OmniMotion-X is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [AnyPcc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20331): compresses arbitrary point clouds with a single universal model.
- [Sketch2BIM: A Multi-Agent Human-AI Collaborative Pipeline to Convert Hand-Drawn Floor Plans to 3D BIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20838): Classification concern: a human-in-the-loop multi-agent pipeline converts floor-plan sketches to JSON layouts and BIM scripts, making it closer to an Agent Harness entry than a pure model.
- [Topology Sculptor, Shape Refiner: Discrete Diffusion Model for High-Fidelity 3D Meshes Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21264): Uses discrete diffusion for high-fidelity 3D mesh generation, adding a topology-aware mesh synthesis route.
- [WorldGrow: Generating Infinite 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21682): Generates unbounded 3D worlds, fitting spatial world-generation models rather than isolated object reconstruction.
- [Moving Beyond Diffusion: Hierarchy-to-Hierarchy Autoregression for fMRI-to-Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22335): targets fMRI-to-Image Reconstruction with moving Beyond Diffusion: Hierarchy-to-Hierarchy Autoregression.
- [GeoDiffusion: A Training-Free Framework for Accurate 3D Geometric Conditioning in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22337): Uses a class-specific 3D object prior, keypoint correlations, and viewpoint-consistent rendering to condition image generation on accurate 3D geometry without training.
- [ReconViaGen: Towards Accurate Multi-view 3D Object Reconstruction via Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23306): Injects reconstruction priors into diffusion-based 3D generation, improving cross-view feature connections and controlling iterative denoising so generated invisible geometry and texture stay consistent with observed multiview inputs.
- [TIRE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23605): Performs subject-driven 3D and 4D generation through tracking, inpainting, and resplatting for identity-preserving texture infilling.
- [Concerto: Joint 2D-3D Self-Supervised Learning Emerges Spatial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23607): Combines 3D self-distillation with 2D-3D joint embedding so self-supervised learning produces spatial features for 3D scene perception and open-world spatial understanding.
- [TurboPortrait3D: Single-step diffusion-based fast portrait novel-view synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23929): Refines feed-forward portrait 3D avatar renders with a single-step diffusion model trained for multiview consistency, giving low-latency portrait novel-view synthesis.
- [FreeArt3D: Training-Free Articulated Object Generation using 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25765): Provides a training-free 3D-diffusion route for articulated-object generation, expanding controllable spatial asset synthesis.
- [FullPart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26140): Generates 3D object parts at full resolution, extending part-aware spatial asset generation beyond coarse decomposed shapes.
- [DC4GS: Directional Consistency-Driven Adaptive Density Control for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26921): DC4GS targets 3D Gaussian Splatting with directional Consistency-Driven Adaptive Density Control.
- [Causal Masking on Spatial Data: An Information-Theoretic Case for Learning Spatial Datasets with Unimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27009): Studies causal masking for learning spatial datasets with unimodal language models, adding a model-side route for representing spatial data through language-model training objectives.
- [HiT 3D Shape Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27088): Learns unsupervised hierarchical transformer abstractions of 3D shapes, adding coarse-to-fine structure to spatial models.
- [HiGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27148): Builds hierarchical generative scenes through multi-step associative semantic spatial composition, strengthening structured spatial generation coverage.
- [Spatial-SSRL: Enhancing Spatial Understanding via Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27606): Uses self-supervised reinforcement learning to improve spatial understanding.
- [Sketch-to-Layout: Sketch-Guided Multimodal Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27632): Sketch-to-Layout is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [Object-Aware 4D Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00248): adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Diff4Splat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00503): Uses latent dynamic reconstruction models for controllable 4D scene generation, extending spatial generation to time-varying scenes.
- [Kinematify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01294): Synthesizes open-vocabulary high-degree-of-freedom articulated objects, extending 3D generation toward movable structured assets.
- [Object-Centric 3D Gaussian Splatting for Strawberry Plant Reconstruction and Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02207): Applies object-centric 3DGS to multiview strawberry plant reconstruction so non-destructive phenotyping can measure morphology and canopy traits.
- [FastGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04283): accelerates 3D Gaussian Splatting training to roughly 100 seconds, improving practical spatial-scene representation.
- [Efficient representation of 3D spatial data for defense-related applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05109): Classification concern: a comparative analysis of point clouds, voxels, meshes, NeRFs, and 3DGS for defense geospatial data, closer to survey or systems context than a new model.
- [Splatography: Sparse multi-view dynamic Gaussian Splatting for filmmaking challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05152): Splatography targets filmmaking challenges with sparse multi-view dynamic Gaussian Splatting.
- [How Many Tokens Do 3D Point Cloud Transformer Architectures Really Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05449): Introduces gitmerge3D, a globally informed graph token-merging method that removes up to 90-95% of point-cloud transformer tokens while preserving 3D task performance.
- [ALIGN: A Vision-Language Framework for High-Accuracy Accident Location Inference through Geo-Spatial Neural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06316): ALIGN targets high-Accuracy Accident Location Inference through Geo-Spatial Neural Reasoning with a Vision-Language Framework.
- [Robust and High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting: Fusing Pose Priors and Geometry Constraints for Texture-Deficient Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06765): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [ConeGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06810): uses pixel-cone-guided densification for improved Gaussian-splatting reconstruction with fewer primitives.
- [RaLD: Generating High-Resolution 3D Radar Point Clouds with Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07067): RaLD adds latent Diffusion to generating High-Resolution 3D Radar Point Clouds.
- [Sparse4DGS: 4D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-Frame Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07122): Sparse4DGS targets sparse-Frame Dynamic Scene Reconstruction with 4D Gaussian Splatting.
- [Omni-View: Unlocking How Generation Facilitates Understanding in Unified 3D Model based on Multiview images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07222): Uses multiview images to unify 3D generation and understanding, showing how generation can support spatial representation learning.
- [DIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07409): Generates diverse 3D motions for arbitrary objects from a single image using video-model priors and compact trajectory latents.
- [SasMamba: A Lightweight Structure-Aware Stride State Space Model for 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08872): Adds model-side coverage for spatial and 3D capability, focusing on state-space modeling for 3D human pose.
- [LARM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11563): Introduces a large articulated-object reconstruction model, adding a foundation-style route for movable 3D assets.
- [SRSplat: Feed-Forward Super-Resolution Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12040): Reconstructs high-resolution 3D scenes from sparse low-resolution views by combining MLLM and diffusion reference galleries with internal texture cues.
- [LSS3D: Learnable Spatial Shifting for Consistent and High-Quality 3D Generation from Single-Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12202): LSS3D develops learnable spatial shifting for consistent and high-quality 3d generation from single-image.
- [TR-Gaussians: High-fidelity Real-time Rendering of Planar Transmission and Reflection with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13009): TR-Gaussians adds 3D Gaussian Splatting to high-fidelity Real-time Rendering of Planar Transmission and Reflection.
- [Cognitive Maps in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13371): Mechanistically analyzes spatial planning representations in language models. Core idea: test whether LMs form cognitive-map-like structures that support planning over spatial relations.
- [Part-X-MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13647): Builds a part-aware 3D multimodal large language model, connecting MLLM reasoning with structured 3D asset understanding.
- [PhysX-Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13648): Generates simulation-ready physical 3D assets from one image. Core idea: turn single-view visual evidence into structured assets with physical properties for spatial and embodied simulation.
- [Gaussian See, Gaussian Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14848): transfers semantic 3D motion from multiview video into Gaussian-splatting reconstruction, strengthening dynamic spatial asset editing.
- [Gaussian Blending: Rethinking Alpha Blending in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15102): Replaces scalar alpha blending in 3DGS with spatially varying Gaussian Blending, reducing zoom-dependent blur and staircase artifacts without extra memory.
- [A Decade of Systems for Human Data Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15585): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it surveys human-data-interaction systems rather than proposing a 3D, geometric, or spatial-reasoning model.
- [FlashMesh: Faster and Better Autoregressive Mesh Synthesis via Structured Speculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15618): Uses structured speculation to accelerate and improve autoregressive mesh synthesis.
- [Extending Test-Time Scaling: A 3D Perspective with Context, Batch, and Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15738): Extends test-time scaling to 3D tasks through context, batch, and turn dimensions.
- [Temporally Coherent VGGT Semantic SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16282): builds memory-efficient temporally coherent 3D maps with VGGT for semantic SLAM.
- [LLaVA3: Representing 3D Scenes like a Cubist Painter to Boost 3D Scene Understanding of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16454): Improves VLM 3D scene understanding without fine-tuning by reconstructing multiview object views and packing them into cubist-style omnidirectional visual representations.
- [PartUV: Part-Based UV Unwrapping of 3D Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16659): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [Mesh RAG: Retrieval Augmentation for Autoregressive Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16807): Uses retrieval augmentation for autoregressive mesh generation, linking RAG-style conditioning with structured 3D asset synthesis.
