- [Radar2Shape: 3D Shape Reconstruction from High-Frequency Radar using Multiresolution Signed Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17484): Radar2Shape uses multiresolution Signed Distance Functions for 3D Shape Reconstruction from High-Frequency Radar.
- [Novel View Synthesis from A Few Glimpses via Test-Time Natural Video Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17932): Uses pretrained video diffusion priors to synthesize pseudo views from a few glimpses, then densifies 3D Gaussian Splatting supervision through an uncertainty-aware reconstruction-generation feedback loop.
- [RAISECity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18005): Uses a multimodal agent framework for reality-aligned city-scale 3D world generation.
- [MimiCAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18370): Uses correspondence-aware cascade transformers for category-free 3D pose transfer across structurally different characters.
- [NeAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18600): Couples neural assets with rendering in one stack, adding a model-side route for editable 3D asset and scene generation.
- [RigAnyFace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18601): Scales neural facial auto-rigging to diverse and disconnected mesh topologies with unlabeled data.
- [Any4D: Open-Prompt 4D Generation from Natural Language and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18746): Any4D reconstructs or generates open-prompt 4d generation from natural language and images.
- [DetAny4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18814): Builds an open-set 4D detection framework over streaming RGB video with a geometry-aware spatiotemporal decoder, extending 3D detection toward temporally stable dynamic-scene understanding.
- [NVGS: Neural Visibility for Occlusion Culling in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19202): Learns viewpoint-dependent Gaussian visibility with a shared MLP and uses neural occlusion culling inside an instanced rasterizer to reduce VRAM and rendering cost.
- [V2-SAM Cross-View Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20886): combines SAM2 with multi-prompt experts for cross-view object correspondence.
- [Detail Enhanced Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale Volumetric Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21697): targets large-Scale Volumetric Capture with detail Enhanced Gaussian Splatting.
- [MoE3D: Mixture of Experts meets Multi-Modal 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22103): Uses a mixture-of-experts 3D understanding design to route multimodal scene signals through specialized expert capacity for object, relation, and spatial reasoning.
- [Controllable 3D Object Generation with Single Image Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22194): Conditions 3D object generation on a single reference image while exposing controls for shape and appearance, turning image prompts into editable 3D assets.
- [IE-SRGS: An Internal-External Knowledge Fusion Framework for High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22233): Fuses internal scene statistics with external priors to super-resolve 3D Gaussian splatting scenes, targeting sharper high-frequency geometry and appearance.
- [HMR3D: Hierarchical Multimodal Representation for 3D Scene Understanding with Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22961): Builds hierarchical 3D, image, and language representations so an LVLM can reason over object-level and scene-level spatial structure.
- [Action-guided generation of 3D functionality segmentation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23230): Introduces SynthFun3D, which retrieves part-annotated 3D assets and arranges plausible scenes from action descriptions to synthesize functionality-segmentation masks.
- [MAC-Ego3D: Multi-Agent Gaussian Consensus for Real-Time Collaborative Ego-Motion and Photorealistic 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09723): MAC-Ego3D develops multi-agent gaussian consensus for real-time collaborative ego-motion and photorealistic 3d reconstruction.
- [DenseScan: Advancing 3D Scene Understanding with 2D Dense Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00226): Generates dense multi-level 3D scene descriptions from multi-view 2D images with MLLMs, turning 2D annotation pipelines into semantic supervision for 3D understanding.
- [Binary-Gaussian: Compact and Progressive Representation for 3D Gaussian Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00944): Encodes per-Gaussian semantic categories with coarse-to-fine binary codes, reducing memory while supporting progressive multi-granularity 3DGS segmentation.
- [EGG-Fusion: Efficient 3D Reconstruction with Geometry-aware Gaussian Surfel on the Fly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01296): EGG-Fusion adds geometry-aware Gaussian Surfel on the Fly to efficient 3D Reconstruction.
- [SPARK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01629): Uses VLM knowledge for sim-ready part-level articulated reconstruction, connecting 3D perception with action-ready spatial assets.
- [Masking Matters: Unlocking the Spatial Reasoning Capabilities of LLMs for 3D Scene-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02487): Replaces causal decoding masks with 3D Spatial Language Instruction Masks so order-agnostic objects and instruction tokens can attend in task-specific spatial patterns.
- [LATTICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03052): Scales high-fidelity 3D generation, adding a broad spatial asset-generation model line rather than a single-object reconstruction trick.
- [Flux4D: Flow-based Unsupervised 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03210): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [ShelfGaussian: Shelf-Supervised Open-Vocabulary Gaussian-based 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03370): Trains a multi-modal Gaussian Transformer with off-the-shelf vision foundation model supervision, preserving Gaussian rendering while adding open-vocabulary 3D semantics.
- [GAOT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03566): Generates articulated objects from text with diffusion models, adding language-conditioned movable-object synthesis.
- [GaussianBlender: Instant Stylization of 3D Gaussians with Disentangled Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03683): Learns disentangled latent spaces for feed-forward text-driven 3D Gaussian stylization, avoiding per-asset optimization and improving multi-view consistency.
- [Outlier View Rejection in Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04012): studies emergent outlier-view rejection in visual geometry grounded Transformers.
- [Radiance Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04076): introduces radiance meshes for volumetric reconstruction.
- [SyncTrack4D: Cross-Video Motion Alignment and Video Synchronization for Multi-Video 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04315): Aligns motion across unsynchronized videos before 4D Gaussian reconstruction, using cross-video tracking cues to stabilize dynamic scene geometry over time.
- [Bridging Simulation and Reality: Cross-Domain Transfer with Semantic 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04731): Projects multiview semantic 2D fields into 3D via Gaussian splatting and filters background features to provide domain-invariant spatial inputs for sim-to-real robot policies.
- [SpaceControl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05343): Adds test-time spatial control to 3D generative modeling, strengthening controllable spatial generation coverage.
- [Tracking-Guided 4D Generation: Foundation-Tracker Motion Priors for 3D Model Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06158): Couples multi-view video diffusion with foundation point tracking and hybrid 4DGS reconstruction so tracker-derived motion priors guide 3D model animation.
- [DragMesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06424): Enables interactive 3D generation through mesh dragging controls, adding direct user control to spatial asset synthesis.
- [Generalized Geometry Encoding Volume for Real-time Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06793): Builds a real-time stereo network with depth-aware structural priors and dynamic cost aggregation, improving generalization without the latency of large stereo foundation models.
- [sim2art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07698): Models articulated objects from a single video using only synthetic training data, extending spatial reconstruction for interactive assets.
- [Lang3D-XL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07807): embeds language into 3D Gaussians for large-scale scene representation.
- [CVP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08135): Introduces a central-peripheral-vision-inspired multimodal model for spatial reasoning, adding an architecture line for visual spatial evidence allocation.
- [Photo3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08535): Improves photorealistic 3D generation through structure-aligned detail enhancement, adding a general image-to-3D generation method.
- [D4RT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08924): Uses a unified transformer and query-based decoding to reconstruct dynamic 3D scenes from video efficiently.
- [3DID: Direct 3D Inverse Design for Aerodynamics with Physics-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08987): 3DID adds physics-Aware Optimization to direct 3D Inverse Design for Aerodynamics.
- [View-on-Graph: Zero-shot 3D Visual Grounding via Vision-Language Reasoning on Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09215): Performs zero-shot 3D grounding by reasoning over scene graphs with vision-language models, making object localization depend on explicit relational structure.
- [UniPart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09435): Unifies part-level 3D generation and geometry-segmentation latents, improving structured 3D asset generation.
- [Long-LRM++: Preserving Fine Details in Feed-Forward Wide-Coverage Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10267): Extends feed-forward large reconstruction models to wide-coverage inputs while preserving fine geometric details that are usually lost in fast sparse-view reconstruction.
- [Breaking the Vicious Cycle: Coherent 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse and Motion-Blurred Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10369): Improves 3D Gaussian splatting when observations are both sparse and motion-blurred.
- [E-RayZer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10950): treats self-supervised 3D reconstruction as spatial visual pretraining.
- [SceneMaker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10957): Generates open-set 3D scenes through decoupled de-occlusion and pose estimation, extending single-image spatial scene generation.
- [Video-Codec 3DGS Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11186): Compresses 3D Gaussian splatting with lightweight video-codec techniques, adding a practical spatial-representation efficiency route.
- [RoomPilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11234): Synthesizes controllable interactive indoor environments through multimodal semantic parsing.
- [Prior-Enhanced Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scene Reconstruction from Casual Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11356): Refines dynamic Gaussian splatting with segmentation, epipolar-error maps, skeleton sampling, re-identification tracks, virtual-view depth loss, and scaffold projection constraints.
- [Animus3D: Text-driven 3D Animation via Motion Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12534): Uses motion score distillation to transfer text-conditioned video motion priors into 3D animation, giving static assets temporally coherent movement.
- [CoRe3D: Collaborative Reasoning as a Foundation for 3D Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12768): Frames collaborative reasoning as a foundation for 3D intelligence, connecting spatial understanding with multi-view or multi-actor reasoning.
- [I-Scene: 3D Instance Models are Implicit Generalizable Spatial Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13683): Treats 3D instance models as generalizable spatial learners, using object-centric representations to transfer shape and scene knowledge across 3D tasks.
- [An intercomparison of generative machine learning methods for downscaling precipitation at fine spatial scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13987): Compares generative downscaling models for fine-scale precipitation fields, emphasizing how spatial resolution and uncertainty affect geophysical prediction.
- [Repurposing 2D Diffusion Models for 3D Shape Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13991): Adapts 2D diffusion priors to complete partial 3D shapes, using image-generation knowledge as a prior for missing geometry.
- [AnimaMimic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14133): Uses video diffusion priors to animate static 3D meshes, bridging 2D motion imagination and explicit 3D animation.
- [4D-RaDiff: Latent Diffusion for 4D Radar Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14235): Trains a latent diffusion model for 4D radar point-cloud sequences, synthesizing dynamic radar geometry for data augmentation and perception research.
- [SS4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14284): Trains a native 4D generator over structured spacetime latents for dynamic 3D object synthesis from monocular video.
- [Broadening View Synthesis of Dynamic Scenes from Constrained Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14406): Introduces ExpanDyNeRF, which uses Gaussian-splatting priors and pseudo side-view supervision from the SynDM dataset to render dynamic monocular scenes under wider viewpoint changes.
- [ART](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14671): Introduces an articulated reconstruction transformer, extending spatial reconstruction for objects whose parts move.
- [MVGSR: Multi-View Consistent 3D Gaussian Super-Resolution via Epipolar Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15048): Uses epipolar guidance to super-resolve 3D Gaussian scenes while enforcing multi-view consistency across rendered views.
- [Bilateral Spatial Reasoning about Street Networks: Graph-based RAG with Qualitative Spatial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15388): Combines graph-based retrieval with qualitative spatial representations so language models can reason over bilateral relations in street networks.
- [Scaling Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs through Programmatic Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16237): The method scales MLLM spatial reasoning through programmatically generated training data.
- [PoseMoE: Mixture-of-Experts Network for Monocular 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16494): Applies mixture-of-experts routing to monocular 3D human pose estimation so specialized experts can handle different body configurations and visual conditions.
- [M-PhyGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16885): Infers multi-material object dynamics from video, linking spatial reconstruction with physics-aware scene and object modeling.
- [Voxel-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17528): compresses 3D Gaussian splatting with quantized scaffolds and run-length coding.
- [Symmetrization of 3D Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18953): Audits symmetry preservation in 3D generative models and proposes a data-centric symmetrization intervention for point-cloud generation.
- [VOIC: Visible-Occluded Integrated Guidance for 3D Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18954): Integrates visible-region evidence with occluded-space guidance for 3D semantic scene completion, targeting voxel geometry and semantic labels behind observed surfaces.
- [OmniEgoCap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19283): reconstructs egocentric human motion with camera-agnostic sequence-level modeling.
- [4D Gaussian Splatting as a Learned Dynamical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19648): Recasts 4DGS as a continuous-time neural dynamical system, enabling sparse temporal learning, time extrapolation, and localized dynamics injection beyond per-frame deformation.
- [FlashLips: 100-FPS Mask-Free Latent Lip-Sync using Reconstruction Instead of Diffusion or GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20033): Uses reconstruction-based latent lip synchronization instead of diffusion or GAN sampling, targeting real-time 3D-aware talking-face animation.
- [Dreamcrafter: Immersive Editing of 3D Radiance Fields Through Flexible, Generative Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20129): Provides a VR radiance-field editing architecture that combines direct 3D manipulation with generative edits over NeRF or 3DGS scene representations.
- [Enhancing annotations for 5D apple pose estimation through 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20148): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting reconstructions to improve 5D apple pose annotations, turning reconstructed fruit geometry into stronger pose-estimation supervision.
- [SegMo: Segment-aligned Text to 3D Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21237): Aligns text segments with generated 3D human motion intervals so motion synthesis follows fine-grained temporal language structure.
- [SemCovert: Secure and Covert Video Transmission via Deep Semantic-Level Hiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22233): Co-designs semantic hiding and recovery models for covert video transmission, addressing privacy leakage from temporal semantic representations rather than 3D reconstruction.
- [Split4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22745): decomposes 4D scene reconstruction without requiring video segmentation.
- [Hash Grid Feature Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22882): prunes hash-grid features for more efficient neural spatial representations.
- [Spatial Interpolation of Room Impulse Responses based on Deeper Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Residual Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22915): Uses deeper residual PINNs to interpolate room impulse responses from sparse measurements, modeling spatial sound propagation under acoustic physics constraints.
- [GeoTeacher: Geometry-Guided Semi-Supervised 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23147): Uses geometry-guided teacher signals for semi-supervised 3D object detection, exploiting unlabeled spatial data while constraining pseudo-labels with 3D structure.
- [Video and Language Alignment in 2D Systems for 3D Multi-object Scenes with Multi-Information Derivative-Free Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24826): Adapts 2D vision-language systems to 3D multi-object scenes by using derivative-free camera control and mutual-information objectives to handle occlusions and feature differentiation.
- [A novel framework for multi-step water level predicting by spatial–temporal deep learning models based on integrated physical models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jhydrol.2025.133683): Presents a workflow, retrieval/tool loop, verification method, prompting strategy, or orchestration harness for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception.
- [Scene-LLM](https://doi.org/10.1109/WACV61041.2025.00220): Introduces Extending Language Model for 3D Visual Reasoning, a model-side contribution for Spatial.
- [DreamReward-X: Boosting High-Quality 3D Generation with Human Preference Alignment](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3609680): Aligns 3D generation with human preferences through a reward model, adding preference optimization to spatial asset synthesis.
- [CraftsMan3D: High-fidelity Mesh Generation with 3D Native Diffusion and Interactive Geometry Refiner](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00500): Generates high-fidelity meshes with native 3D diffusion and an interactive geometry refiner, adding a strong 3D asset-generation method.
- [4D-Fly: Fast 4D Reconstruction from a Single Monocular Video](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01553): Reconstructs 4D structure quickly from a single monocular video, adding an efficient dynamic-scene reconstruction model.
- [Look at the Sky](https://doi.org/10.1109/TVCG.2025.3549187): Adds sky-aware modeling to efficient 3D Gaussian splatting in outdoor scenes, improving in-the-wild spatial reconstruction.
- [Articulated Object Understanding from a Single Video](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCVW69036.2025.00674): Infers articulated-object structure from a single video sequence, supporting movable-object understanding for spatial models.
- [LLaFS++](https://doi.org/10.1109/TPAMI.2025.3573609): Uses large language models to support few-shot image segmentation, extending language-guided dense spatial perception.
- [MotionDreamer](https://doi.org/10.1109/3DV66043.2025.00087): uses semantic video-diffusion features for zero-shot 3D mesh animation, linking video priors with motion-ready spatial assets.
- [Efficient Dynamic Scene Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.02501): Separates static and dynamic components in 4D Gaussian scenes to make dynamic-scene editing more efficient.
- [FRPGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3557012): Builds fast, robust, and photorealistic monocular dynamic-scene reconstruction with deformable 3D Gaussians.
- [C3DGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/jstars.2025.3529261): Compresses 3D Gaussian surface-reconstruction models for large-scale scenes from multiview UAV imagery.
- [Spatial-Temporal Transformer for Point Cloud Registration](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.displa.2025.103139): Uses spatial-temporal Transformer modeling for point-cloud registration in digital modeling of complex environments.
- [Spatial-Temporal Joint Network for Point-Cloud Completion](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.displa.2025.103245): Uses spatial-temporal joint modeling for point-cloud completion, extending 3D spatial reconstruction coverage.
- [Structure-Guided Memory-Efficient 3D Gaussians](https://doi.org/10.1109/TVCG.2025.3637033): Uses structure guidance to make large-scale 3D Gaussian reconstruction more memory efficient.
- [Dynamic Gaussian Streams](https://doi.org/10.1109/MMSP64401.2025.11324257): Compresses volumetric video with codebook-based quantization over dynamic Gaussian streams.
- [Geometric Redundancy Removal for 3D Gaussian Coding](https://doi.org/10.1109/VCIP67698.2025.11396895): Eliminates geometric representation redundancy for 3D Gaussian splat coding.
- [AniMo](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.00186): introduces a species-aware model for text-driven animal motion generation.
- [StaticNeRF Freeze-Frame Mapping](https://doi.org/10.1109/LRA.2025.3632068): uses uncertainty-guided NeRF map reconstruction in dynamic scenes.
- [CGS-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/IROS60139.2025.11246845): applies compact 3D Gaussian splatting to dense visual SLAM.
- [LatentHOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.01623): uses latent hand diffusion for generalizable hand-object motion generation.
- [Hand-held Object Reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.01141): reconstructs hand-held objects from RGB video while accounting for dynamic interaction.
- [VGGT-Mapping](https://doi.org/10.1109/IRAC67707.2025.11381128): builds a robot-navigation mapping system on top of Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer representations.
- [MNE-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.00146): performs multi-agent neural SLAM for mobile robots, adding a collaborative spatial-mapping model.
- [GSArch](https://doi.org/10.1109/HPCA61900.2025.00037): uses architectural support to break memory barriers in 3D Gaussian splatting training.
- [3DGS-to-PC](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCVW69036.2025.00395): converts 3D Gaussian splatting representations into dense point clouds.
- [DGS-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3645351): performs robust visual SLAM with 3D Gaussian splatting in dynamic environments.
- [Mamba4D](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.01642): uses disentangled spatial-temporal state-space models for efficient 4D point-cloud video understanding.
- [Mesh-Aligned 3DGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3544760): aligns Gaussian splats to meshes for multi-resolution anti-aliasing rendering.
- [MBA-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/TPAMI.2025.3596976): adds motion-blur awareness to dense visual SLAM with radiance-field representations.
- [SDD-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/LRA.2025.3561565): uses semantic-driven dynamic SLAM with Gaussian splatting.
- [InterGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/VCIP67698.2025.11396809): introduces inter-predictive coding for Gaussian-splatting sequences, extending efficient dynamic 3D representation compression.
- [Scalable Gaussian Splatting Coding](https://doi.org/10.1109/MMSP64401.2025.11324364): targets low-latency immersive visual communication through scalable 3DGS coding.
- [MeGS-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/IROS60139.2025.11246063): improves memory efficiency in Gaussian-splatting SLAM with graph signal processing.
- [Spatial Coordinate Transformation for 3D Neural Implicit Mapping](https://doi.org/10.1109/LRA.2025.3595031): improves 3D neural implicit mapping through spatial coordinate transformation.
- [RelationLMM: Large Multimodal Model as Open and Versatile Visual Relationship Generalist](https://doi.org/10.1109/TPAMI.2025.3531452): Frames a large multimodal model as an open visual-relationship generalist, strengthening relation understanding across visual scenes.
- [Diffusion-CAD: Controllable Diffusion Model for Generating Computer-Aided Design Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3535797): Uses a controllable diffusion model to generate computer-aided design models, extending spatial generation toward structured CAD assets.
- [NexusGS: Sparse View Synthesis with Epipolar Depth Priors in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.02496): Uses epipolar depth priors for sparse-view synthesis with 3D Gaussian splatting, improving spatial reconstruction from limited views.
- [TGAvatar: Reconstructing 3D Gaussian Avatars With Transformer-Based Tri-Plane](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3543892): Reconstructs 3D Gaussian avatars with transformer-based tri-planes, adding an avatar modeling route for spatial human representation.
- [HyperPoint: Multimodal 3D foundation model in hyperbolic space](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112800): Builds a multimodal 3D foundation model in hyperbolic space, extending point-cloud representation learning for spatial understanding.
- [Sparsegs: Sparse View Synthesis Using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://doi.org/10.1109/3dv66043.2025.00100): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting for sparse-view synthesis, improving spatial scene reconstruction when observations are limited.
- [SPOK: tokenizing geographic space for enhanced spatial reasoning in GeoAI](https://doi.org/10.1080/13658816.2025.2497810): Tokenizes geographic space for spatial reasoning in GeoAI, adding a geospatial representation method for models that reason over locations and spatial relations.
- [Dimensionx: Create Any 3D and 4D Scenes From a Single Image With Decoupled Video Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01271): Creates 3D and 4D scenes from one image using decoupled video diffusion.
- [POI-LLM: Leveraging POI Enhanced Large Language Models for Spatial-Temporal Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccbdai66607.2025.11388271): Uses point-of-interest enhanced large language models for spatial-temporal modeling, extending LLM use into geospatial representation tasks.
- [TAPTR3D: Decoupled 3D Point Tracking Boosts 2D and Further Enhances 3D Tracking Accuracy](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsp.2025.3616009): Decouples 3D point tracking from 2D correspondence estimation so explicit 3D trajectories can improve both image-plane tracking and spatial tracking accuracy.
- [Pos3R: 6D Pose Estimation for Unseen Objects Made Easy](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01567): Uses pose priors for unseen-object 6D pose estimation, reducing dependence on category-specific CAD models or dense correspondences.
- [Rethinking Correspondence-based Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00117): Reexamines correspondence-based category-level 6D pose estimation, focusing on how correspondence design affects generalization across object instances.
- [UniBVR: Balancing visual and reasoning abilities in unified 3D scene understanding](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.132599): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [VGMamba: Attribute-to-Location Clue Reasoning for Quantity-Agnostic 3D Visual Grounding](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00503): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [MAMoE: A Multi-Agent Mixture-of-Experts Framework for LLM-Assisted 3D Object Reconstruction and Transmission](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnse.2025.3618771): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [ReTracker: Exploring Image Matching for Robust Online Any Point Tracking](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00410): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [DS-Stereo: Deep-Shallow Information Interaction for Stereo Matching](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3617730): Introduces or analyzes a model-side method for spatial, 3D, reconstruction, or grounded perception, adding training, architecture, alignment, or generation coverage.
- [TG-TSGNet: A Text-Guided Arbitrary-Resolution Terrain Scene Generation Network](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2025.3644231): Generates arbitrary-resolution terrain scenes from text guidance, linking language conditioning with spatial terrain layout and texture synthesis.
- [Let us Make a Splan: Risk-Aware Trajectory Optimization in a Normalized Gaussian Splat](https://doi.org/10.1109/tro.2025.3584559): Represents the planning scene as a normalized Gaussian splat and optimizes trajectories with risk awareness over that continuous spatial field.
- [Hi-Gaussian: Hierarchical Gaussians Under Normalized Spherical Projection for Single-View 3D Reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02662): Uses hierarchical Gaussians under normalized spherical projection to reconstruct single-view 3D objects with scale-aware spatial detail.
- [LeOp-GS: Learned Optimizer With Dynamic Gradient Update for Sparse-View 3DGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3616156): Learns an optimizer with dynamic gradient updates for sparse-view 3DGS, improving reconstruction from limited camera observations.
- [Watermarking via Gaussian noise modulation in diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.132188): Off-axis for spatial modeling; the work watermarks diffusion models with Gaussian noise modulation rather than contributing 3D representation, geometry, or scene reasoning.
- [MS3D: High-Quality 3D Generation via Multi-Scale Representation Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02444): Models 3D generation with multi-scale representations so coarse structure and fine geometry can be synthesized at high quality.
- [Enhanced Geometry and Semantics for Camera-Based 3D Semantic Scene Completion](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2025.3635475): develops enhanced geometry and semantics for camera-based 3d semantic scene completion.
- [Multi-view stereo 3D building reconstruction with sparse depth and edge location priors](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.autcon.2025.106365): combines its base model with sparse depth and edge location priors to improve multi-view stereo 3d building reconstruction.
- [CSG-Fusion: Consistent Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting via Matching-based Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccvw69036.2025.00276): CSG-Fusion uses Matching-based Fusion to improve consistent sparse-view gaussian splatting.
- [GSRecon: Efficient Generalizable Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction from Sparse Views](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02351): GSRecon develops efficient generalizable gaussian splatting for surface reconstruction from sparse views.
- [Generalizable 3D Gaussian splatting via multi-view stereo and consistency constraints](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.131696): uses multi-view stereo and consistency constraints to improve generalizable 3d gaussian splatting.
- [Facilitate and Scale Up the Creation of 3D Meshes, 6D Category-Based Datasets and Grasping with Generative Models: GenVegeFruits3D](https://doi.org/10.1109/icip55913.2025.11084401): Facilitate and Scale Up the Creation of 3D Meshes, 6D Category-Based Datasets and Grasping with Generative Models adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Obj3Dify: Occlusion-Invariant 3D Reconstruction of Hand-Held Objects](https://doi.org/10.1109/ijcnn64981.2025.11229385): Obj3Dify adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Dp-M3D: Monocular 3D object detection algorithm with depth perception capability](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.113539): Dp-M3D combines its base model with depth perception capability to improve monocular 3d object detection algorithm.
- [Physically Realizable Adversarial Creating Attack Against Vision-Based BEV Space 3D Object Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2025.3526056): adds a 3D or 4D modeling route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [3D-MMFN: Multi-level multimodal fusion network for 3D industrial image anomaly detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.aei.2025.103284): Contributes model-side spatial methods, such as training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, or world-model techniques.
- [S2M2: Scalable Stereo Matching Model for Reliable Depth Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02481): S2M2 targets reliable Depth Estimation with scalable Stereo Matching Model.
- [FedSTDN: A Federated Learning-Enabled Spatial-Temporal Prediction Model for Wireless Traffic Prediction](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmc.2025.3559066): Adds a model entry for spatial, centered on spatial, three-dimensional, or grounding contribution.
- [MambaTraffic: Contrastive Traffic Flow Prediction With Spatial–Temporal State Space Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/mits.2025.3525848): MambaTraffic combines its base model with Spatial–Temporal State Space Models to improve contrastive traffic flow prediction.
- [Disparity guidance and spatial-angular interaction for single-view-based light field synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112147): develops disparity guidance and spatial-angular interaction for single-view-based light field synthesis.
- [V2X-Gaussians: Gaussian Splatting for Multi-Agent Cooperative Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1109/iv64158.2025.11097436): V2X-Gaussians develops gaussian splatting for multi-agent cooperative dynamic scene reconstruction.
