- [Mamba-3VL: Taming State Space Model for 3D Vision Language Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00592): A model-side contribution for spatial reasoning; core idea: Taming State Space Model for 3D Vision Language Learning.
- [VisionCube: 3D-Aware Vision-Language Model for Multi-Step Spatial Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvprw67362.2025.00309): VisionCube targets multi-Step Spatial Reasoning with 3D-Aware Vision-Language Model.
- [Enhancing Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Vision-Language Models via Depth-Aware Feature Integration](https://doi.org/10.1109/snpd65828.2025.11254066): Extends CLIP with a lightweight depth branch that fuses RGB and depth features while preserving pretrained image-text knowledge, improving relative-position and distance judgments on spatial reasoning benchmarks.
- [XStreamVGGT: Extremely Memory-Efficient Streaming Vision Geometry Grounded Transformer with KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01204): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for spatial and physical reasoning. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [BARE: Towards Bias-Aware and Reasoning-Enhanced One-Tower Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01526): BARE is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [Thinking with Blueprints: Assisting Vision-Language Models in Spatial Reasoning via Structured Object Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01984): Trains VLMs to build object-centric JSON-style blueprints before answering, combining supervised traces, blueprint-aware rewards, and anti-shortcut augmentation for spatial reasoning.
- [MorphGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02716): Transfers articulated 3D motion from videos with morphology-adaptive Gaussian-splatting representations.
- [SCAR-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04348): adds spatial-context attention over residuals for progressive Gaussian splatting.
- [3D-Agent:Tri-Modal Multi-Agent Collaboration for Scalable 3D Object Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04404): Uses tri-modal agents over multi-view images, text, and point clouds to generate scalable 3D object annotations with cross-view aggregation.
- [DIFF-MF: A Difference-Driven Channel-Spatial State Space Model for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05538): DIFF-MF targets multi-Modal Image Fusion with a Difference-Driven Channel-Spatial State Space Model.
- [3D Instruction Ambiguity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05991): Defines Ambi3D, a scene-grounded ambiguity task and dataset where models decide whether an instruction has one unambiguous target in a 3D scene.
- [RigMo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06378): Unifies rigging and motion learning for generative animation, strengthening animation-ready 3D asset modeling.
- [3D CoCa v2: Contrastive Learners with Test-Time Search for Generalizable Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06496): 3D CoCa v2 adds test-Time Search for Generalizable Spatial Intelligence to contrastive Learners.
- [Mon3tr: Monocular 3D Telepresence with Pre-built Gaussian Avatars as Amortization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07518): Uses pre-built Gaussian avatars to amortize monocular 3D telepresence reconstruction, reducing per-session modeling cost for real-time remote presence.
- [Leveraging 3D Representation Alignment and RGB Pretrained Priors for LiDAR Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07692): Combines 3D representation alignment with RGB pretrained priors for LiDAR scene generation.
- [Smooth Operator: Smooth Verifiable Reward Activates Spatial Reasoning Ability of Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07695): Smooth Operator uses smooth verifiable rewards to activate spatial reasoning in vision-language models.
- [The Spatial Blindspot of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09954): makes spatial or geometric structure explicit in the model objective, representation, or evaluation target.
- [SUG-Occ: Explicit Semantics and Uncertainty Guided Sparse Learning for Efficient 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11396): Guides sparse occupancy learning with explicit semantic and uncertainty priors, reducing redundant voxel computation while preserving geometry and labels.
- [Proc3D: Procedural 3D Generation and Parametric Editing of 3D Shapes with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12234): Represents generated shapes as procedural compact graphs so LLM-authored 3D models remain editable through exposed parameters and rule structure.
- [GaussianTrimmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12683): trims 3DGS segmentation boundaries online, extending Gaussian scene-segmentation methods.
- [Left-Right Symmetry Breaking in CLIP-style Vision-Language Models Trained on Synthetic Spatial-Relation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12809): Studies CLIP-style VLMs trained on synthetic spatial-relation data, adding a model-side lens on when left-right relation learning improves or distorts spatial reasoning.
- [TreeDGS: Aerial Gaussian Splatting for Distant DBH Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12823): Applies aerial Gaussian splatting to distant tree reconstruction so diameter-at-breast-height can be measured from spatial scene geometry.
- [POCI-Diff: Position Objects Consistently and Interactively with 3D-Layout Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14056): Guides diffusion with 3D layouts so generated objects keep consistent positions while users interactively adjust spatial placement.
- [IIR-VLM: In-Context Instance-level Recognition for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14188): Studies in-context instance-level recognition for VLMs, targeting fine-grained distinction of individual people or objects beyond category-level visual grounding.
- [Structured Image-Based Coding for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14510): compresses Gaussian splats through structured image-based coding.
- [Symmetry Informative and Agnostic Feature Disentanglement for 3D Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14804): Disentangles symmetry-informative and symmetry-agnostic shape descriptors for meshes and point clouds, improving symmetry-aware matching, editing, and segmentation.
- [ScenDi: 3D-to-2D Scene Diffusion Cascades for Urban Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15221): Cascades 3D scene structure into 2D diffusion stages to generate urban scenes with spatial layout constraints and view-level realism.
- [Assessing Situational and Spatial Awareness of VLMs with Synthetically Generated Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15780): Uses synthetically generated videos to probe VLM situational and spatial awareness, exposing whether models track positions, relations, and scene changes over time.
- [GR3EN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16272): Performs generative relighting for 3D environments, adding lighting-aware scene editing to spatial generation coverage.
- [PPISP: Physically-Plausible Compensation and Control of Photometric Variations in Radiance Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18336): Adds a physically plausible ISP correction module for radiance-field reconstruction, disentangling camera-intrinsic and capture-dependent photometric effects so novel-view rendering can handle exposure and white-balance shifts.
- [VGGT-SLAM 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19887): performs real-time dense feed-forward scene reconstruction for SLAM-style spatial perception.
- [FreeFix: Boosting 3D Gaussian Splatting via Fine-Tuning-Free Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20857): Uses fine-tuning-free image diffusion with interleaved 2D-3D refinement and per-pixel confidence masks to repair extrapolated 3DGS novel views without overfitting scene-specific diffusion models.
- [PLANING](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22046): proposes a loosely coupled Triangle-Gaussian framework for streaming 3D reconstruction.
- [Geometry without Position? When Positional Embeddings Help and Hurt Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22231): Studies when positional embeddings help or hurt spatial reasoning, making coordinate and position encoding a model-side design axis.
- [TTSA3R: Training-Free Temporal-Spatial Adaptive Persistent State for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22615): TTSA3R targets streaming 3D Reconstruction with training-Free Temporal-Spatial Adaptive Persistent State.
- [UrbanGS: A Scalable and Efficient Architecture for Geometrically Accurate Large-Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02089): Scales 3DGS to urban scenes with depth-consistent D-normal regularization, confidence-weighted geometry alignment, spatially adaptive Gaussian pruning, and partitioned view assignment.
- [From Single Scan to Sequential Consistency: A New Paradigm for LIDAR Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03198): Introduces TempLoc, a LiDAR relocalization model that estimates global coordinates, inter-frame correspondences, and uncertainty-guided fusion to enforce sequentially consistent 6-DoF poses.
- [Pi-GS: Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting with Dense π^3 Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03327): Improves sparse-view Gaussian splatting with dense pi-cubed initialization, targeting better geometry before optimization.
- [Constrained Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03538): adds constraints to dynamic Gaussian splatting for time-varying 3D reconstruction.
- [DMS2F-HAD: A Dual-branch Mamba-based Spatial–Spectral Fusion Network for Hyperspectral Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04102): Uses dual Mamba branches for hyperspectral anomaly detection, separately modeling spatial and spectral features before dynamic gated fusion for efficient rare-target localization.
- [Skin Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04805): Learns compact skinning tokens for unified autoregressive rigging, adding a token-based route to 3D articulation.
- [PoseGaussian: Pose-Driven Novel View Synthesis for Robust 3D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05190): Builds a pose-guided 3DGS human novel-view synthesis pipeline in which pose priors refine depth and temporal pose encoders stabilize dynamic human reconstruction.
- [RFM-Pose:Reinforcement-Guided Flow Matching for Fast Category-Level 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05257): Replaces slow score-based pose diffusion with flow matching and PPO-guided hypothesis scoring for faster category-level 6D pose estimation.
- [COSMOS: Coherent Supergaussian Modeling with Spatial Priors for Sparse-View 3D Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06044): Uses coherent supergaussian modeling with spatial priors for sparse-view 3D splatting.
- [MGP-KAD: Multimodal Geometric Priors and Kolmogorov-Arnold Decoder for Single-View 3D Reconstruction in Complex Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06158): Fuses RGB features with class-level geometric priors and a Kolmogorov-Arnold decoder to improve single-view 3D reconstruction in noisy, object-diverse scenes.
- [Scalable Adaptation of 3D Geometric Foundation Models via Weak Supervision from Internet Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07891): Uses weak supervision from internet video to adapt 3D geometric foundation models, expanding spatial model training beyond curated 3D labels.
- [FLAG-4D: Flow-Guided Local-Global Dual-Deformation Model for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08558): Models dynamic scenes with flow-guided local and global deformation networks that warp canonical 3D Gaussians over time for temporally coherent 4D reconstruction.
- [Spatial Features in Multimodal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08713): mechanistically analyzes when spatial features emerge during multimodal fine-tuning, linking VLM adaptation to spatial reasoning capability.
- [Toward Fine-Grained Facial Control in 3D Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09736): Introduces FG-3DGS, which disentangles low- and high-frequency facial motion regions and adds post-render alignment for temporally stable 3D talking-head generation.
- [ERGO: Excess-Risk-Guided Optimization for High-Fidelity Monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10278): Decomposes noisy synthesized-view supervision into excess risk and Bayes error, then uses view-specific adaptive loss weights with geometry- and texture-aware objectives to make monocular 3DGS reconstruction less artifact-prone.
- [LaSSM: Efficient Semantic-Spatial Query Decoding via Local Aggregation and State Space Models for 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11007): Initializes 3D instance queries from semantic-spatial superpoints and decodes them with coordinate-guided state space blocks, reducing attention cost in point-cloud instance segmentation.
- [ReaDy-Go: Real-to-Sim Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting Simulation for Environment-Specific Visual Navigation with Moving Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11575): ReaDy-Go combines its base model with Moving Obstacles to improve real-to-sim dynamic 3d gaussian splatting simulation for environment-specific visual navigation.
- [WorldTree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11845): Builds 4D dynamic worlds from monocular video with tree-chain structure, strengthening persistent spatial-world modeling.
- [SPRig](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12740): Learns pose-invariant rigging from mesh sequences through self-supervised consistency, improving dynamic 3D asset preparation.
- [GSM-GS: Geometry-Constrained Single and Multi-view Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12796): Constrains 3DGS surface reconstruction with image-gradient sub-region weighting and geometry-guided cross-view point associations for stronger geometric detail and multi-view consistency.
- [From Pixels to Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13912): Uses reinforcement learning to improve spatial reasoning for content-aware layout design.
- [Learnable Multi-level Discrete Wavelet Transforms for 3D Gaussian Splatting Frequency Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14199): Uses multi-level wavelet frequency modulation as a coarse-to-fine curriculum for 3DGS, reducing Gaussian growth while preserving novel-view synthesis quality.
- [Learning Proposes, Geometry Disposes: A Modular Framework for Efficient Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14409): Combines learned proposal generation with geometric verification, adding a modular model-side route for efficient spatial reasoning.
- [SAM 3D Body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15989): Builds robust full-body human mesh recovery, adding a reusable 3D human-representation model line.
- [Adaptive Multi-Scale Channel-Spatial Attention Aggregation Framework for 3D Indoor Semantic Scene Completion Toward Assisting Visually Impaired](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16385): Uses parallel channel-spatial attention and adaptive multi-scale gating to lift wearable RGB views into 3D semantic scene completion for indoor obstacle awareness.
- [B3-Seg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17134): performs camera-free, training-free 3DGS segmentation with analytic EIG and Beta-Bernoulli updates.
- [4DSTAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18830): Uses spatial-temporal state propagation for autoregressive 4D object generation with stronger temporal consistency.
- [SymPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19117): Uses symbolic projective layout for allocentric spatial reasoning in VLMs. Core idea: represent layout structure explicitly so models can reason from external spatial frames rather than only egocentric cues.
- [DICArt: Advancing Category-level Articulated Object Pose Estimation in Discrete State-Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19565): Formulates articulated-object pose estimation as discrete diffusion with hierarchical kinematic coupling, denoising rigid-part poses while respecting articulation constraints.
- [TraceVision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19768): Builds a trajectory-aware VLM for human-like spatial understanding. Core idea: use trajectory structure as model-side supervision so spatial reasoning reflects paths and viewpoint changes rather than isolated frames.
- [RL-RIG: A Generative Spatial Reasoner via Intrinsic Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19974): Trains a generative spatial reasoner with intrinsic reflection.
- [3D Aesthetic Camera Viewpoint Suggestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20363): learns a 3D aesthetic field for camera viewpoint suggestion.
- [AeroDGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22376): performs physically consistent dynamic Gaussian splatting for single-sequence aerial 4D reconstruction.
- [BetterScene: 3D Scene Synthesis with Representation-Aligned Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22596): Adapts pretrained video diffusion as a representation-aligned prior for sparse-photo novel view synthesis, recovering view-consistent detail without dense scene captures.
- [SceneTransporter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22785): Uses optimal transport-guided compositional latent diffusion for single-image structured 3D scene generation.
- [Align then Adapt: Rethinking Parameter-Efficient Transfer Learning in 4D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23069): Separates alignment and adaptation for parameter-efficient transfer from 3D pretrained models to 4D point-cloud perception.
- [Evidential Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23574): Adds evidential uncertainty estimation to NeRF rendering, jointly predicting aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty in one forward pass while preserving reconstruction quality.
- [FocusTrack: One-Stage Focus-and-Suppress Framework for 3D Point Cloud Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24133): Uses a one-stage focus-and-suppress framework for 3D point-cloud object tracking, improving spatial tracking under distractors.
- [VoxelDiffusionCut: Non-destructive Internal-part Extraction via Iterative Cutting and Structure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00116): Uses a voxel diffusion model to estimate hidden internal parts from observed cut surfaces, enabling iterative non-destructive cutting plans for 3D disassembly.
- [Station2Radar: query conditioned gaussian splatting for precipitation field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00418): Introduces query-conditioned Gaussian splatting for precipitation fields, rendering only rainfall-support regions from station observations and satellite imagery for efficient weather reconstruction.
- [HeroGS: Hierarchical Guidance for Robust 3D Gaussian Splatting under Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01099): Guides sparse-view 3DGS hierarchically with pseudo-dense image supervision, feature-adaptive densification/pruning, and co-pruned geometry consistency.
- [ArtLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01142): Uses a 3D LLM to generate articulated assets, connecting language-conditioned structure generation with interactive spatial objects.
- [CoSMo3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01205): Uses LLM-guided canonical spatial modeling for open-world promptable 3D semantic part segmentation, shifting part understanding from sensor pose coordinates to canonical object frames.
- [TIMI: Training-Free Image-to-3D Multi-Instance Generation with Spatial Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01371): Uses training-free instance separation, spatial layout preservation, and multi-instance view fusion to exploit existing image-to-3D priors for spatially faithful multi-object generation.
- [Dehallu3D: Hallucination-Mitigated 3D Generation from Single Image via Cyclic View Consistency Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01601): Mitigates single-image 3D generation hallucinations with cyclic view-consistency refinement, adjacent-view continuity, adaptive smoothness, and an outlier-risk metric.
- [Sparse View Distractor-Free GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01603): performs distractor-free Gaussian splatting under sparse-view settings.
- [OnlineX: Unified Online 3D Reconstruction and Understanding with Active-to-Stable State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02134): Builds an online feed-forward 3DGS framework with active and stable memory states, jointly reconstructing appearance and language fields from streaming images.
- [VLMFusionOcc3D: VLM Assisted Multi-Modal 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02609): Injects LoRA-adapted VLM semantic priors, weather-aware sensor fusion, and depth-aware geometry alignment into multimodal 3D occupancy prediction.
- [Direct Reward Fine-Tuning on Poses for Single Image to 3D Human in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02619): Post-trains single-image 3D human reconstruction with a pose reward over generated multi-view latents, using pose-video-derived DrPose15K instead of new 3D assets.
- [DuoMo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03265): uses dual motion diffusion for world-space human reconstruction.
- [LoGeR: Long-Context Geometric Reconstruction with Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03269): LoGeR adds hybrid Memory to long-Context Geometric Reconstruction.
- [Yolo-Key-6D: Single Stage Monocular 6D Pose Estimation with Keypoint Enhancements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03879): Adds 3D keypoint projection heads and continuous 9D rotation regression to a YOLO-style monocular network for real-time 6D object pose estimation.
- [NOVA3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04179): Uses a non-pixel-aligned visual transformer for amodal 3D reconstruction, strengthening spatial reasoning over occluded object structure.
- [EmbodiedSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04254): builds online feed-forward semantic 3D Gaussians for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding.
- [ZipMap: Linear-Time Stateful 3D Reconstruction via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04385): ZipMap uses test-Time Training for linear-Time Stateful 3D Reconstruction.
- [DSA-SRGS: Super-Resolution Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Sparse-View DSA Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04770): Combines a DSA-specific super-resolution prior, confidence-aware pseudo-label weighting, and sub-pixel Gaussian densification for dynamic sparse-view vascular 4D reconstruction.
- [MultiGO++: Monocular 3D Clothed Human Reconstruction via Geometry-Texture Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04993): Improves monocular clothed-human reconstruction with synthetic textured scans, region-aware geometry extraction, Fourier geometry encoding, and a dual reconstruction U-Net.
- [Interpretable Perception and Reasoning for Audiovisual Geolocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05708): Fuses audio acoustic atoms, GRPO-tuned multimodal reasoning, and Riemannian flow matching on the sphere for high-precision audiovisual geolocation.
- [PatchCue: Enhancing Vision-Language Model Reasoning with Patch-Based Visual Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05869): Targets visual spatial cues, spatial relations, or spatial reasoning models.
- [GenHOI: Towards Object-Consistent Hand-Object Interaction with Temporally Balanced and Spatially Selective Object Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06048): Adds temporally balanced reference-object tokens and spatial attention gates to video generation models so hand-object interaction videos preserve object identity and contact regions.
- [3DGS-HPC: Distractor-free 3D Gaussian Splatting with Hybrid Patch-wise Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07587): Uses patch-wise static/transient classification and a hybrid photometric-perceptual metric to remove distractors from real-world 3DGS reconstruction.
- [3ViewSense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07751): Uses orthographic-view grounding and a simulate-and-reason mechanism to improve VLM mental rotation, reconstruction, and view-consistent spatial reasoning.
- [Point Cloud as a Foreign Language for Multi-modal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09173): Treats raw point clouds as discrete tokens for an end-to-end 3D MLLM, pairing a lightweight 3D tokenizer with preference optimization for open-ended 3D QA.
- [ProGS 3DGS Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09703): provides progressive coding for 3D Gaussian splatting representations.
- [VLM-Loc: Localization in Point Cloud Maps via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09826): Transforms point-cloud maps into BEV images and scene graphs so VLMs can perform interpretable text-to-point-cloud localization over 3D layouts.
- [S2D Sparse-to-Dense Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10893): lifts sparse inputs to dense 3D reconstruction with minimal observations.
- [Pointy - A Lightweight Transformer for Point Cloud Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10963): Adds model-side coverage for spatial and 3D capability, focusing on point-cloud foundation model.
- [Mobile-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11531): adapts Gaussian splatting for real-time mobile devices.
- [CEI-3D: Collaborative Explicit-Implicit 3D Reconstruction for Realistic and Fine-Grained Object Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11810): Combines an implicit SDF with locally controllable handler points and disentangled physical properties, enabling part-wise 3D object edits that propagate consistently across views.
- [Pano360: Perspective to Panoramic Vision with Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12013): Generates panoramic views from perspective inputs while enforcing geometric consistency across views.
- [Spatial Reasoning is Not a Free Lunch: A Controlled Study on LLaVA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12545): Controls LLaVA variants to show that CLIP-style encoders, 1D image-token flattening, and positional encoding choices materially shape VLM spatial-reasoning failures.
- [Coherent Human-Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12789): Reconstructs coherent human-scene geometry from multi-person multi-view video in one pass, strengthening spatial human-scene modeling.
- [Spectral Defense for 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12796): defends 3D Gaussian splatting against resource-targeting attacks through spectral analysis.
- [Locatability-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Image Geo-Localization with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13628): Uses a locatability score to choose adaptive reasoning depth for VLM image geolocalization and reduce fixed-depth hallucination.
- [SK-Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14152): Adds skeleton-based structural control to native 3D generation, making generated assets follow explicit geometry.
- [ChArtist: Generating Pictorial Charts with Unified Spatial and Subject Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14209): Generates pictorial charts with a skeleton-based spatial control representation, subject-driven reference control, adaptive position encoding, and spatially gated attention.
- [NanoGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16103): simplifies Gaussian splats without training, improving lightweight 3D scene representations.
- [DualPrim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16133): Combines positive and negative primitives for compact 3D reconstruction, strengthening primitive-based spatial representation.
- [Iris: Bringing Real-World Priors into Diffusion Model for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16340): Injects real-world priors into diffusion models for monocular depth estimation.
- [SegviGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16869): Repurposes 3D generative models for part segmentation, connecting generation priors with spatial parsing.
- [OnlineHMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17355): performs online world-grounded human mesh recovery from video, strengthening dynamic human spatial understanding.
- [Material Magic Wand: Material-Aware Grouping of 3D Parts in Untextured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17370): Learns material-aware embeddings for untextured mesh parts with supervised contrastive loss, letting one selected part retrieve geometrically varied parts sharing the same material.
- [PanoVGGT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17571): performs feed-forward 3D reconstruction from panoramic imagery.
- [TAPESTRY: From Geometry to Appearance via Consistent Turntable Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17735): Conditions video diffusion on explicit 3D geometry to generate consistent turntable videos that can be back-projected into UV textures or supervise 3DGS.
- [LoST: Level of Semantics Tokenization for 3D Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17995): Introduces semantic-level tokenization for 3D shapes, improving token-based spatial asset modeling.
- [Inst4DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18402): decomposes instances in 4D Gaussian splatting with multi-video label-permutation learning.
- [SwiftGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18634): uses episodic priors for immediate satellite surface recovery, extending large-scale spatial reconstruction.
- [Polynomial-Kernel Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18707): replaces exponential kernels with polynomial kernels in Gaussian splatting.
- [Points-to-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18782): Conditions 3D generation on point-cloud priors so generated assets preserve structure, strengthening model-side spatial asset generation.
- [CustomTex: High-fidelity Indoor Scene Texturing via Multi-Reference Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19121): Textures indoor 3D scenes from per-instance reference images through semantic and pixel-level VSD distillation, improving controllable high-fidelity scene appearance.
- [DreamPartGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19216): Uses semantically grounded collaborative latent denoising for part-level 3D generation, strengthening structured spatial asset synthesis.
- [Discovering Governing Spatial Interaction Mechanisms in Dynamic Urban Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19537): Combines LLM-generated differential-equation hypotheses with neural fitting to identify governing spatial interaction laws in dynamic urban systems.
- [PCSTracker: Long-Term Scene Flow Estimation for Point Cloud Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19762): Estimates long-term scene flow in point-cloud sequences with iterative geometry-motion optimization, spatiotemporal trajectory updates, and sliding-window propagation.
- [Cov2Pose: Leveraging Spatial Covariance for Direct Manifold-aware 6-DoF Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19961): Uses spatial covariance for direct manifold-aware 6-DoF object pose estimation.
- [Memory Over Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20530): localizes 3D objects without full reconstruction, adding a spatial-memory alternative to map-heavy localization pipelines.
- [The Role and Relationship of Initialization and Densification in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20714): Analyzes how initialization and densification interact in 3D Gaussian splatting, clarifying model-design choices behind spatial reconstruction quality.
- [SARe: Structure-Aware Large-Scale 3D Fragment Reassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21611): Reassembles large-scale 3D fragments with structure awareness, fitting spatial reconstruction and scene assembly.
- [Know3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22782): Prompts 3D generation with knowledge from vision-language models, linking VLM semantics with spatial asset synthesis.
- [Looking Beyond the Window: Global-Local Aligned CLIP for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23030): Extends CLIP open-vocabulary segmentation beyond sliding-window locality with global-local token alignment, proxy anchors, and scale-aware attention normalization.
- [GSwap: Realistic Head Swapping With Dynamic Neural Gaussian Field.](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23168): Embeds dynamic neural Gaussian portrait priors into a SMPL-X surface so video head swapping preserves 3D consistency, expressions, and foreground-background blending.
- [PhysSkin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23194): Learns physics-informed neural skinning fields for real-time, discretization-agnostic articulated-object animation.
- [FilterGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23891): provides traversal-free parallel filtering and adaptive shrinking for large-scale LoD 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [LightSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24146): Builds fast and memory-efficient open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding on Gaussian splats, extending language-aligned spatial representation.
- [Teacher-Student Diffusion Model for Text-Driven 3d Hand Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24407): Uses a teacher-student diffusion setup for text-driven 3D hand motion, where auxiliary MANO-style guidance improves a text-only student at inference.
- [Scalable Object Relation Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24721): Encodes object relations to improve 3D spatial reasoning in LLMs, adding a model-side route for grounding language reasoning in structured spatial relations.
- [Relaxed Rigidity Dynamic 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24994): Uses ray-based grouping and relaxed rigidity to preserve local geometry in monocular dynamic Gaussian splatting.
- [SpatialStack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27437): Stacks geometry features through VLM layers rather than only late fusion, aligning local 3D geometry, visual context, and language for 3D spatial reasoning.
- [SPREAD: Spatial-Physical REasoning via geometry Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27573): Uses a graph-transformer diffusion model conditioned on posed scene point clouds plus collision, relation, and gravity guidance to generate physically coherent 3D scenes.
- [DipGuava](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28003): Disentangles personalized Gaussian features for 3D head avatars from monocular video, extending spatial generation coverage for controllable human avatar representations.
- [MolmoPoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28069): Adds pointing and grounding tokens so VLMs can output visual locations more accurately as a model-native behavior.
- [To View Transform or Not to View Transform: NeRF-based Pre-training Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28090): Analyzes NeRF-based pretraining for autonomous-driving 3D perception, showing how view transformation can conflict with radiance-field geometry priors.
- [ObjectMorpher: 3D-Aware Image Editing via Deformable 3DGS Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28152): Lifts image objects into deformable 3D Gaussians so ambiguous 2D edits become geometry-grounded object manipulations with interactive control.
- [M2H-MX: Multi-Task Dense Visual Perception for Real-Time Monocular Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29236): Builds a real-time monocular model for 3D scene graph construction by combining dense semantic, geometric, depth, and mapping predictions.
- [Bioinspired123D: Generative 3D Modeling System for Bioinspired Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29592): Uses a lightweight code-as-geometry pipeline to turn text prompts into parametric, fabricable bioinspired 3D structures with controllable geometry.
- [GRVS: a Generalizable and Recurrent Approach to Monocular Dynamic View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29734): Uses a recurrent monocular dynamic-view-synthesis model to improve geometric consistency across static and dynamic regions without per-scene 4D optimization.
- [TRiGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00538): uses temporal rigid-body motion for scalable 4D Gaussian splatting.
- [STAR: Mitigating Cascading Errors in Spatial Reasoning via Turn-point Alignment and Segment-level DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00558): Mitigates cascading errors in structured spatial navigation with turn-point alignment, RedMaze-23K annotations, and segment-level preference optimization for self-correction.
- [Compact Keyframe-Optimized Multi-Agent Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00804): Compacts Gaussian maps and centralizes loop closure for multi-agent RGB-D 3DGS SLAM, reducing communicated map data while preserving reconstruction fidelity.
- [GS^2: Graph-based Spatial Distribution Optimization for Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01884): Optimizes compact 3DGS with ELBO-based adaptive densification, opacity-aware pruning, and graph feature encoding that shifts Gaussians toward better spatial distributions.
- [SDesc3D: Towards Layout-Aware 3D Indoor Scene Generation from Short Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01972): Generates indoor 3D layouts from short descriptions by enriching sparse text with multi-view scene priors, functionality-aware grounding, and iterative self-rectification.
- [Streaming Real-Time Rendered Scenes as 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02851): Streams an evolving 3D Gaussian scene representation from a renderer to clients, enabling local viewpoint rendering instead of fixed-view 2D video streaming.
- [Token Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02870): Warps visual tokens to support viewpoint-shift spatial reasoning without observing every nearby view.
- [GP-4DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02915): Uses variational Gaussian processes for probabilistic 4D Gaussian splatting from monocular video.
- [HOIGS: Human-Object Interaction Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04016): Models human-object interaction in dynamic 3DGS with cross-attention between human and object deformation features, improving occlusion, contact, and manipulation reconstruction.
- [Indoor Asset Detection in Large Scale 360{\deg} Drone-Captured Imagery via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05316): Associates 2D detections and masks into 3D Gaussian object instances using a semantic-spatial codebook for large indoor asset detection.
- [GaussianGrow: Geometry-aware Gaussian Growing from 3D Point Clouds with Text Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05721): Grows 3D Gaussians from point clouds under text guidance, using multi-view diffusion supervision and iterative inpainting of unobserved regions.
- [LiveStre4m](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06740): performs feed-forward live novel-view streaming from unposed multi-view video.
- [RePL: Pseudo-label Refinement for Semi-supervised LiDAR Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06825): Refines semi-supervised LiDAR pseudo-labels with masked reconstruction and a dedicated training strategy to reduce confirmation bias in 3D semantic segmentation.
- [Location Is All You Need: Continuous Spatiotemporal Neural Representations of Earth Observation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07092): Represents Earth-observation regions as continuous coordinate-conditioned spatiotemporal neural fields that can reconstruct satellite imagery and transfer to downstream tasks.
- [Mem3R: Streaming 3D Reconstruction with Hybrid Memory via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07279): Mem3R uses test-Time Training for streaming 3D Reconstruction with Hybrid Memory.
- [Fast Spatial Memory with Elastic Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07350): adds elastic Test-Time Training to fast Spatial Memory.
- [Training-free Spatially Grounded Geometric Shape Encoding (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07522): Encodes arbitrary 2D geometric shapes with training-free Zernike-basis geometry and harmonic pose fields, preserving invertibility, adaptivity, and frequency richness.
- [Direct Segmentation without Logits Optimization for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07723): Derives open-vocabulary segmentation maps analytically from distribution-discrepancy structure, avoiding iterative logits optimization and model-specific attention tuning.
- [TOOLCAD: Exploring Tool-Using Large Language Models in Text-to-CAD Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07960): Explores tool-using LLMs for text-to-CAD generation with reinforcement learning, linking spatial generation and tool-use training.
- [Novel View Synthesis as Video Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08500): reframes novel-view synthesis as video completion, linking spatial reconstruction with temporal generation.
- [SelfEvo 4D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08532): uses self-distillation over unlabeled videos to improve multi-view 4D perception models.
- [GaussiAnimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08547): Reconstructs and rigs animatable categories with dynamic-level modeling, adding category-level 3D animation reconstruction.
- [MeshOn: Intersection-Free Mesh-to-Mesh Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08799): Fits one mesh onto another with VLM-initialized alignment, geometric attraction losses, intersection-preventing barriers, and diffusion-assisted deformation.
- [Integrated electro-optic attention nonlinearities for transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09512): targets transformers with integrated electro-optic attention nonlinearities.
- [ExpertEdit Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10466): learns skill-aware motion editing from expert videos.
- [HiddenObjects: Scalable Diffusion-Distilled Spatial Priors for Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10675): HiddenObjects targets object Placement with scalable Diffusion-Distilled Spatial Priors.
- [Disentangled Point Diffusion for Precise Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11793): develops disentangled point diffusion for precise object placement.
- [ArtifactWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12251): scales 3D Gaussian splatting artifact restoration by leveraging video generation models.
- [DreamStereo: Towards Real-Time Stereo Inpainting for HD Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12270): DreamStereo adds a spatial model for 3D or 4D generation, reconstruction, view synthesis, or spatial reasoning.
- [Reconstruction of a 3D wireframe from a single line drawing via generative depth estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13549): uses generative depth estimation to improve reconstruction of a 3d wireframe from a single line drawing.
- [Efficient Multi-View 3D Object Detection by Dynamic Token Selection and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13586): uses dynamic Token Selection and Fine-Tuning to realize efficient Multi-View 3D Object Detection.
- [Design and Behavior of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Layers in CNN-based Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13761): Analyzes how transformer mixture-of-experts layers form spatial structure, using expert organization as the model-side object of study.
- [SpatialEvo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14144): Introduces Self-Evolving Spatial Intelligence via Deterministic Geometric Environments, a model-side contribution for Spatial.
- [Giving Faces Their Feelings Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14541): Adds explicit emotion control to feedforward single-image 3D head avatars, extending controllable 3D human representation generation.
- [Learning to Draw ASCII Improves Spatial Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14641): Trains language models to construct ASCII spatial layouts from text, showing that explicit layout generation can improve spatial reasoning over downstream QA.
- [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672): SPAGBias makes spatial or geometric structure explicit in the model objective, representation, or evaluation target.
- [Hybrid Latents Surfel Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14928): combines geometry and appearance latents for surfel splatting.
- [GlobalSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15284): uses global scene tokens for efficient feed-forward 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [UniMamba: A Unified Spatial-Temporal Modeling Framework with State-Space and Attention Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16325): UniMamba adds state-Space and Attention Integration to a Unified Spatial-Temporal Modeling Framework.
- [FlowRefiner: Flow Matching-Based Iterative Refinement for 3D Turbulent Flow Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17149): FlowRefiner targets 3D Turbulent Flow Simulation with flow Matching-Based Iterative Refinement.
- [DGSSM: Diffusion guided state-space models for multimodal salient object detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17585): DGSSM targets multimodal salient object detection with diffusion guided state-space models.
- [ViPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17623): Uses video-informed pose spaces for auto-rigged meshes, extending spatial asset generation toward motion-ready 3D structures.
- [View-Consistent 3D Scene Editing via Dual-Path Structural Correspondense and Semantic Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17801): uses dual-Path Structural Correspondense and Semantic Continuity for view-Consistent 3D Scene Editing.
- [HMR-Net: Hierarchical Modular Routing for Cross-Domain Object Detection in Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18866): HMR-Net targets cross-Domain Object Detection in Aerial Images with hierarchical Modular Routing.
- [AnyRecon: Arbitrary-View 3D Reconstruction with Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19747): Uses video diffusion priors for arbitrary-view 3D reconstruction, adding a generative route for spatial scene recovery from flexible viewpoints.
- [UniCon3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19923): reconstructs contact-aware 4D human-scene interactions from monocular video.
- [Gaussians on a Diet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20046): trains high-quality 3D Gaussian splats under memory bounds.
- [GSCompleter: A Distillation-Free Plugin for Metric-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting Completion in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20155): Completes sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting scenes through a generate-then-register workflow, reducing iterative repair and distillation cost.
- [ATIR: Towards Audio-Text Interleaved Contextual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20267): Off-axis for spatial modeling; it targets audio-text contextual retrieval rather than 3D, geometry, or spatial scene understanding.
- [Animator-Centric Skeleton Generation on Objects with Fine-Grained Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20539): Generates object skeletons with fine-grained details, supporting animator-oriented 3D rigging and articulation workflows.
- [PILOT: One Physics-Integrated Generation Framework to Unify 2D and 3D Radio Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23533): PILOT contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [Spatiotemporal Degradation-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting for Realistic Underwater Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23551): Spatiotemporal Degradation-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting for Realistic Underwater Scene Reconstruction adds a model-side method, architecture, training recipe, reward/verifier, or data-generation route for spatial.
- [DecompKAN: Decomposed Patch-KAN for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23968): DecompKAN targets long-Term Time Series Forecasting with decomposed Patch-KAN.
- [Diffusion Model as a Generalist Segmentation Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24575): uses diffusion-based generation or distillation to control geometry, views, placement, or 3D content synthesis.
- [Power Foam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24994): unifies real-time differentiable ray tracing and rasterization for spatial rendering.
- [DouC: Dual-Branch CLIP for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24997): DouC targets training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation with dual-Branch CLIP.
- [Planar Gaussian Splatting with Bilinear Spatial Transformer for Wireless Radiance Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25945): Represents wireless radiance fields with 2D planar Gaussians in 3D coordinates and uses a bilinear spatial transformer to model long-range electromagnetic coupling among primitives on angular grids.
- [MesonGS++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26799): compresses 3D Gaussian splatting through post-training compression and hyperparameter search.
- [FreeOcc: Training-Free Embodied Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28115): FreeOcc is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [Two-View Accumulation as the Primary Training Lever for Hybrid-Capture Gaussian Splatting: A Variance-Decomposition View of When Gradient Surgery Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00052): Two-View Accumulation as the Primary Training Lever for Hybrid-Capture Gaussian Splatting is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [Beyond Heuristics: Learnable Density Control for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00408): Replaces heuristic Gaussian density control with a learnable mechanism for 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [MOC-3D: Manifold-Order Consistency for Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01743): MOC-3D develops manifold-order consistency for text-to-3d generation.
- [Mix3R: Mixing Feed-forward Reconstruction and Generative 3D Priors for Joint Multi-view Aligned 3D Reconstruction and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03359): Combines feed-forward reconstruction with generative 3D priors for multi-view 3D reconstruction and pose estimation.
- [PhysForge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05163): Generates physics-grounded 3D assets for interactive virtual worlds, linking asset synthesis to embodied and simulation-ready spatial environments.
- [LAMP Multi-Camera Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05390): tracks people across cameras in a metric 3D world with localization-aware modeling.
- [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459): Proposes a privacy-preserving retrieval paradigm for spatial RAG, keeping location-sensitive retrieval useful while reducing disclosure of place information.
- [Ray-Aware Pointer Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05749): adds adaptive pointer-memory updates for streaming 3D reconstruction.
- [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975): uses improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems for physical Fidelity Reconstruction.
- [AdpSplit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06876): uses error-driven adaptive splitting to speed up geometry discovery in 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [Velocity-Space 3D Asset Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07385): Edits 3D assets in velocity space, adding a controllable spatial asset editing method.
- [PolarVLM: Bridging the Semantic-Physical Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07574): Bridges semantic and physical grounding in vision-language models, strengthening spatially meaningful multimodal representation.
- [DVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07971): Uses discrete voxel diffusion for 3D generation and editing, adding a volumetric generative route for spatial assets.
- [PhysHanDI: Physics-Based Reconstruction of Hand-Deformable Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09538): PhysHanDI is adjacent to the spatial model inventory but needs parent review for a stronger mechanism-specific summary.
- [BEA-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09662): goes beyond radiance supervision in 3D Gaussian splatting for precise object extraction.
- [Hyperbolic Distillation: Geometry-Guided Cross-Modal Transfer for Robust 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09899): targets robust 3D Object Detection with hyperbolic Distillation: Geometry-Guided Cross-Modal Transfer.
- [AdaptSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10239): adapts vision foundation models for feed-forward 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [Interface-Centric Generative States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10438): Frames open-world 3D structure through interface-centric generative states, adding a spatial model line for representing and generating interactive 3D structure.
- [MAGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10760): builds monocular multi-agent Gaussian-splatting SLAM for geometrically and photometrically consistent reconstruction.
- [Pixal3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10922): Generates pixel-aligned 3D assets from images, extending image-to-3D spatial generation beyond avatar-specific reconstruction.
- [ScaleMoGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11704): uses autoregressive next-scale prediction for human motion generation.
- [PairDropGS: Paired Dropout-Induced Consistency Regularization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12072): Uses paired dropout-induced consistency regularization to reduce sparse-view overfitting in 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [Learning Ego-Centric BEV Representations from a Perspective-Privileged View: Cross-View Supervision for Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12218): Learning Ego-Centric BEV Representations from a Perspective-Privileged View targets online HD Map Construction with cross-View Supervision.
- [GeoQuery: Geometry-Query Diffusion for Sparse-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12399): Uses geometry-query diffusion for sparse-view reconstruction, adding a diffusion-based route for recovering 3D structure from limited observations.
- [ORBIT: Preserving Foundational Language Capabilities in GenRetrieval via Origin-Regulated Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12419): ORBIT uses origin-Regulated Merging for preserving Foundational Language Capabilities in GenRetrieval.
- [GTA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12957): Advances image-to-3D world generation by separating geometry construction from appearance synthesis through video diffusion.
- [Sparse Code Uplifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13600): Improves efficient 3D language Gaussian splatting through sparse-code uplift, strengthening open-vocabulary spatial scene representations.
- [R-DMesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13838): Uses rectified dynamic mesh flow for video-guided 3D animation.
- [Implicit spatial-frequency fusion of hyperspectral and lidar data via kolmogorov-arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14239): uses kolmogorov-arnold networks for implicit spatial-frequency fusion of hyperspectral and lidar data.
- [SceneForge: Structured World Supervision from 3D Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14399): SceneForge targets structured World Supervision from 3D Interventions.
- [FactorizedHMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854): Builds a hybrid framework for video human mesh recovery, extending reusable 3D human representation from video.
- [Denoising-GS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14880): adds spatial-aware denoising to Gaussian splatting.
- [3D Skew-Normal Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15010): contributes a 3D or 4D representation route for reconstruction, generation, grounding, or scene understanding.
- [3DTMDet: A Dual-Path Synergy Network of Transformer and SSM for 3D Object Detection in Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15546): 3DTMDet targets 3D Object Detection in Point Clouds with a Dual-Path Synergy Network of Transformer and SSM.
- [Viewpoint-Dependent 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15708): uses viewpoint-dependent spatial relationships for 3D segmentation.
- [WorldAct: Activating Monolithic 3D Worlds into Interactive-Ready Object-Centric Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15843): Turns monolithic 3D worlds into object-centric interactive scenes, fitting spatial models that prepare generated environments for action.
- [Smart target point control for Gaussian Splatting methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16158): targets gaussian Splatting methods with smart target point control.
- [UniCAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05058): Unifies CAD reconstruction, generation, and question answering with a multi-modal multi-task CAD benchmark and universal CAD model, making CAD geometry a shared spatial-modeling target rather than isolated subtasks.
- [SCSV](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIP.2026.3671692): generates dynamic 3D scenes from sparse views with spatial-temporal consistency, extending spatial generation to under-observed 4D scenes.
- [Generative LiDAR Building Reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1109/TGRS.2026.3670027): combines spatial context with generative modeling for building reconstruction from airborne LiDAR, adding a geospatial 3D reconstruction line.
- [MA-MAE3D](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11461790): adds memory augmentation to masked autoencoding for point-cloud completion, strengthening 3D spatial reconstruction coverage.
- [MAGICITY4D](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11464740): generates controllable and editable 4D city scenes with MLLM-enhanced procedural content generation.
- [Multiscale Gaussian Splatting Scene Understanding](https://doi.org/10.1109/TGRS.2026.3690135): uses K-Planes encoding for multiscale Gaussian-splatting scene understanding.
- [Structured Gaussian Mapping for Immersive XR](https://doi.org/10.1109/VRW70859.2026.00236): Builds memory-efficient structured Gaussian maps for immersive XR, extending practical spatial-scene representations.
- [3D Superquadric Splatting](https://doi.org/10.1109/WACV61042.2026.00500): uses superquadric primitives in 3D splatting for structured spatial representation.
- [CodecGS-IR](https://doi.org/10.1109/DCC66757.2026.00042): uses implicit representations for decoder-friendly video-based Gaussian-splat compression.
- [Feature-Plane 3DGS for Mobile VR](https://doi.org/10.1109/VRW70859.2026.00037): adapts feature-plane-based 3D Gaussian splatting to mobile VR from video streams.
- [DyGS-SLAM](https://doi.org/10.1109/JSEN.2026.3662522): combines dynamic-object segmentation with multiview long-term Gaussian consistency for high-fidelity SLAM.
- [LiDAR Odometry Scan Correction](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACCESS.2026.3680632): uses spatial analysis to correct non-repetitive LiDAR scans for odometry in large facility environments.
- [Monocular Multi-Object 3D Visual Language Tracking](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIP.2026.3661407): combines monocular 3D tracking with visual-language grounding.
- [Distraction-Free Outdoor 3DGS](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462029): uses enhanced depth propagation for distraction-free outdoor 3D Gaussian splatting.
- [MemGaussian](https://doi.org/10.1109/GAIIS69281.2026.11519282): uses alternating attention and regularization for text-to-3D Gaussian generation.
- [Cylindrical Densification for G-PCC LiDAR](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIP.2026.3653212): enhances geometry after G-PCC compression of LiDAR point clouds.
- [DSTI-Net Change Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/TGRS.2026.3669158): uses semantic-guided dynamic spatial-temporal interaction for 2D and 3D change detection.
- [Hie4DGS: Hierarchical 4D Gaussian Splatting From Monocular Dynamic Video](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2026.3656737): Reconstructs hierarchical 4D Gaussian splats from monocular dynamic video, adding a dynamic-scene spatial representation method.
- [Splatter Layout: Geometry-embedded 3D Reconstruction via Surface Unfolding](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00744): Splatter Layout uses surface Unfolding for geometry-embedded 3D Reconstruction.
- [GeoAgentic-RAG: A Multi-Agent framework for autonomous geospatial reasoning and visual insight generation with LLM](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jag.2026.105195): GeoAgentic-RAG combines its base model with LLM to improve a multi-agent framework for autonomous geospatial reasoning and visual insight generation.
- [UnsOcc: An Occupancy Grid-Based General Detection Network for Unstructured Scenes](https://doi.org/10.1109/tim.2026.3676098): UnsOcc develops an occupancy grid-based general detection network for unstructured scenes.
- [Moving Infrared Small Target Detection via Motion-Aided Integrated Spatial–Temporal Network](https://doi.org/10.1109/tgrs.2026.3673387): uses motion-Aided Integrated Spatial–Temporal Network for moving Infrared Small Target Detection.
- [A physics-informed Temporal–Spatial gated Kolmogorov–Arnold network for real-time response prediction of floating structures](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.oceaneng.2026.124767): targets real-time response prediction of floating structures with a physics-informed Temporal–Spatial gated Kolmogorov–Arnold network.
- [Low-Rank Spectral–Spatial Super-Resolution of Hyperspectral Images Using KAN-Based GAN](https://doi.org/10.1109/tgrs.2026.3651549): uses kAN-Based GAN for low-Rank Spectral–Spatial Super-Resolution of Hyperspectral Images.
- [Low-Latency Telepresence via Gaussian Splatting With Safety-Aware Control for Mobile Manipulators](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3673834): uses gaussian Splatting With Safety-Aware Control for Mobile Manipulators for low-Latency Telepresence.
- [SSA-Pose: Spatial shape awareness for RGB-based category-level object pose estimation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.displa.2026.103539): SSA-Pose targets rGB-based category-level object pose estimation with spatial shape awareness.
- [SA-U-KAN: Spatial Attention Guided Kolmogorov–Arnold Networks for Optic Disc and Cup Segmentation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsens.2026.3656677): SA-U-KAN targets optic Disc and Cup Segmentation with spatial Attention Guided Kolmogorov–Arnold Networks.
